## api-app/src/integrations/channels/prisma-channel-resolver.ts

```text
import { BadRequestException, ConflictException, Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { Prisma } from '@prisma/client';
import { PrismaService } from '../../infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { ChannelCredentialResolver, type ResolvedChannelCredential } from '../../domain/channels/channel-credential-resolver';
import {
  ChannelResolver,
  type ChannelAvailability,
  type ConversationAvailabilityReference,
  type ChannelOperationContext,
  type ChannelOrigin,
  type ConversationChannelReference,
  type ResolvedChannelOperation,
  type VerifiedIngressChannelReference,
} from '../../domain/channels/transport/channel';
import { ChannelAdapterRegistry } from './channel-adapter-registry';

type Candidate = {
  id: string;
  buId: string;
  type: ChannelOrigin;
  status: string;
  generation: number;
  pairingEpoch: number;
  verifiedEpoch: number | null;
  ownNumberVerified: boolean;
  providerConnectionState: string;
  receiptVerifiedAt: Date | null;
  requiresWindow: boolean;
  providerIdentities: Array<{ id: string; buId: string; provider: ChannelOrigin; currentChannelId: string | null; managedLifecycle: boolean }>;
};

@Injectable()
export class PrismaChannelResolver extends ChannelResolver {
  constructor(
    private readonly prisma: PrismaService,
    private readonly credentials: ChannelCredentialResolver,
    private readonly adapters: ChannelAdapterRegistry,
  ) { super(); }

  async resolveForConversation(reference: ConversationChannelReference): Promise<ResolvedChannelOperation> {
    const { candidate } = await this.selectForConversation(reference);
    return this.bind(candidate);
  }

  async resolveForIngress(reference: VerifiedIngressChannelReference): Promise<ResolvedChannelOperation> {
    const conversation = await this.prisma.conversation.findFirst({
      where: { id: reference.conversationId, buId: reference.buId },
      select: { id: true, buId: true, contact: true },
    });
    if (!conversation) throw new ConflictException({ code: 'CHANNEL_CONTEXT_CHANGED' });
    const candidate = await this.findCandidate(reference.channelId, reference.buId);
    const identity = candidate?.providerIdentities.find((item) => item.id === reference.providerResourceIdentityId);
    if (!candidate || candidate.type !== reference.channelOrigin || candidate.generation !== reference.generation
      || candidate.pairingEpoch !== reference.pairingEpoch || !identity || identity.buId !== reference.buId
      || identity.provider !== reference.channelOrigin || identity.currentChannelId !== reference.channelId
      || !identity.managedLifecycle) {
      throw new ConflictException({ code: 'CHANNEL_CONTEXT_CHANGED' });
    }
    return this.bind(candidate);
  }

  async inspectForConversation(reference: ConversationChannelReference): Promise<ChannelAvailability> {
    try {
      const conversation = await this.prisma.conversation.findFirst({
        where: { id: reference.conversationId, buId: reference.buId }, select: { id: true },
      });
      if (!conversation) return this.unavailable(null, 'CANAL_SELECIONADO_INDISPONIVEL');
      const { candidate, selectionReason, selectedOrigin } = await this.selectCandidate(reference);
      if (!candidate) {
        return this.unavailable(selectedOrigin, selectionReason ?? 'CANAL_SELECIONADO_INDISPONIVEL');
      }
      const identity = candidate.providerIdentities.find((item) => item.currentChannelId === candidate.id);
      let reason: ChannelAvailability['reason'] = null;
      if (!candidate.ownNumberVerified || candidate.verifiedEpoch !== candidate.pairingEpoch || !identity
        || !identity.managedLifecycle || identity.provider !== candidate.type || identity.buId !== candidate.buId) {
        reason = 'CHANNEL_IDENTITY_NOT_VERIFIED';
      } else if (candidate.status !== 'CONECTADO' || !candidate.receiptVerifiedAt
        || (candidate.type === 'OFICIAL' && candidate.providerConnectionState !== 'SUBSCRIBED')
        || (candidate.type === 'NAO_OFICIAL' && candidate.providerConnectionState !== 'CONNECTED')
        || !this.adapters.locallyConfigured(candidate.type)) {
        reason = 'CHANNEL_NOT_READY';
      } else if (candidate.type === 'OFICIAL') {
        const credential = await this.credentials.inspectActiveCredential(candidate.id, candidate.generation);
        if (!credential.ready) reason = 'PENDING_CREDENTIAL';
      }

      const window = candidate.requiresWindow ? await this.window(reference.conversationId) : { open: true, expiresAt: null };
      if (!reason && candidate.requiresWindow && !window.open) reason = 'JANELA_FECHADA';
      return {
        available: reason === null,
        reason,
        channelOrigin: candidate.type,
        requiresWindow: candidate.requiresWindow,
        windowOpen: window.open,
        expiresAt: window.expiresAt,
      };
    } catch {
      return this.unavailable(null, 'CANAL_SELECIONADO_INDISPONIVEL');
    }
  }

  /** Batch-only GET view: BU bindings and credential metadata are read once for the page. */
  async inspectManyForConversations(references: readonly ConversationAvailabilityReference[]): Promise<Map<string, ChannelAvailability>> {
    if (!references.length) return new Map();
    const buId = references[0].buId;
    if (references.some((reference) => reference.buId !== buId)) {
      return new Map(references.map(({ conversationId }) => [conversationId, this.unavailable(null, 'CANAL_SELECIONADO_INDISPONIVEL')]));
    }
    try {
      const ids = references.map(({ conversationId }) => conversationId);
      const channels = await this.channels(buId);
      const credentialState = await this.credentials.inspectActiveCredentials(channels
        .filter((candidate) => candidate.type === 'OFICIAL')
        .map(({ id, generation }) => ({ channelId: id, generation })));
      const selectedById = new Map<string, {
        candidate: Candidate | null;
        selectedOrigin: ChannelOrigin | null;
        selectionReason: ChannelAvailability['reason'] | null;
      }>();
      for (const reference of references) {
        let selectedOrigin = reference.latestMessageOrigin;
        let candidate: Candidate | null;
        let selectionReason: ChannelAvailability['reason'] | null = null;
        if (selectedOrigin) {
          candidate = channels.find((item) => item.type === selectedOrigin) ?? null;
          if (!candidate) selectionReason = 'CANAL_SELECIONADO_INDISPONIVEL';
        } else if (channels.length === 0) {
          candidate = null; selectionReason = 'CANAL_AUSENTE';
        } else if (channels.length > 1) {
          candidate = null; selectionReason = 'CANAL_AMBIGUO';
        } else {
          candidate = channels[0]; selectedOrigin = candidate.type;
        }
        selectedById.set(reference.conversationId, { candidate, selectedOrigin, selectionReason });
      }
      const windowIds = references
        .filter(({ conversationId }) => selectedById.get(conversationId)?.candidate?.requiresWindow)
        .map(({ conversationId }) => conversationId);
      const windows = windowIds.length
        ? await this.latestInboundByConversation(windowIds)
        : new Map<string, Date>();
      const result = new Map<string, ChannelAvailability>();
      for (const id of ids) {
        const { candidate, selectedOrigin, selectionReason } = selectedById.get(id)!;
        if (!candidate) {
          result.set(id, this.unavailable(selectedOrigin, selectionReason ?? 'CANAL_SELECIONADO_INDISPONIVEL'));
          continue;
        }
        const identity = candidate.providerIdentities.find((item) => item.currentChannelId === candidate.id);
        let reason: ChannelAvailability['reason'] = null;
        if (!candidate.ownNumberVerified || candidate.verifiedEpoch !== candidate.pairingEpoch || !identity
          || !identity.managedLifecycle || identity.provider !== candidate.type || identity.buId !== candidate.buId) {
          reason = 'CHANNEL_IDENTITY_NOT_VERIFIED';
        } else if (candidate.status !== 'CONECTADO' || !candidate.receiptVerifiedAt
          || (candidate.type === 'OFICIAL' && candidate.providerConnectionState !== 'SUBSCRIBED')
          || (candidate.type === 'NAO_OFICIAL' && candidate.providerConnectionState !== 'CONNECTED')
          || !this.adapters.locallyConfigured(candidate.type)) {
          reason = 'CHANNEL_NOT_READY';
        } else if (candidate.type === 'OFICIAL' && !credentialState.get(candidate.id)?.ready) {
          reason = 'PENDING_CREDENTIAL';
        }
        let windowOpen = true;
        let expiresAt: Date | null = null;
        if (candidate.requiresWindow) {
          const lastInbound = windows.get(id);
          windowOpen = Boolean(lastInbound && lastInbound.getTime() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 > Date.now());
          expiresAt = lastInbound ? new Date(lastInbound.getTime() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000) : null;
          if (!reason && !windowOpen) reason = 'JANELA_FECHADA';
        }
        result.set(id, {
          available: reason === null, reason, channelOrigin: candidate.type,
          requiresWindow: candidate.requiresWindow, windowOpen, expiresAt,
        });
      }
      return result;
    } catch {
      return new Map(references.map(({ conversationId }) => [conversationId, this.unavailable(null, 'CANAL_SELECIONADO_INDISPONIVEL')]));
    }
  }

  async contextAfterDispatch(context: ChannelOperationContext): Promise<'CURRENT' | 'CHANGED' | 'UNKNOWN'> {
    try {
      const status = await this.contextStatus(context, false);
      return status;
    } catch {
      return 'UNKNOWN';
    }
  }

  private async bind(candidate: Candidate): Promise<ResolvedChannelOperation> {
    const availability = await this.inspectCandidate(candidate);
    if (availability) this.throwUnavailable(availability);
    const credential = await this.credentials.resolve(candidate.id);
    const context: ChannelOperationContext = Object.freeze({
      channelId: candidate.id,
      buId: candidate.buId,
      channelOrigin: candidate.type,
      providerResourceIdentityId: credential.providerResourceIdentityId,
      generation: credential.generation,
      pairingEpoch: credential.pairingEpoch,
      activeCredentialVersion: credential.activeCredentialVersion,
      requiresWindow: candidate.requiresWindow,
    });
    if (!this.sameCredentialContext(candidate, credential)) {
      throw new ConflictException({ code: 'CHANNEL_CONTEXT_CHANGED' });
    }
    const factory = this.adapters.get(candidate.type);
    if (!factory) throw new ConflictException({ code: 'CHANNEL_NOT_READY' });
    const channel = factory.create(credential, async () => {
      try { return (await this.contextStatus(context, true)) === 'CURRENT'; }
      catch { return false; }
    });
    return { channel, context };
  }

  private async inspectCandidate(candidate: Candidate): Promise<ChannelAvailability['reason']> {
    const identity = candidate.providerIdentities.find((item) => item.currentChannelId === candidate.id);
    if (!candidate.ownNumberVerified || candidate.verifiedEpoch !== candidate.pairingEpoch || !identity
      || !identity.managedLifecycle || identity.provider !== candidate.type || identity.buId !== candidate.buId) {
      return 'CHANNEL_IDENTITY_NOT_VERIFIED';
    }
    if (candidate.status !== 'CONECTADO' || !candidate.receiptVerifiedAt
      || (candidate.type === 'OFICIAL' && candidate.providerConnectionState !== 'SUBSCRIBED')
      || (candidate.type === 'NAO_OFICIAL' && candidate.providerConnectionState !== 'CONNECTED')
      || !this.adapters.locallyConfigured(candidate.type)) return 'CHANNEL_NOT_READY';
    if (candidate.type === 'OFICIAL' && !(await this.credentials.inspectActiveCredential(candidate.id, candidate.generation)).ready) {
      return 'PENDING_CREDENTIAL';
    }
    return null;
  }

  private async selectForConversation(reference: ConversationChannelReference) {
    const conversation = await this.prisma.conversation.findFirst({
      where: { id: reference.conversationId, buId: reference.buId },
      select: { id: true, buId: true, contact: true },
    });
    if (!conversation) throw new ConflictException({ code: 'CONVERSATION_IDENTITY_MISMATCH' });
    const { candidate, selectionReason } = await this.selectCandidate(reference);
    if (!candidate) this.throwUnavailable(selectionReason ?? 'CANAL_AUSENTE');
    const reason = await this.inspectCandidate(candidate);
    if (reason) this.throwUnavailable(reason);
    if (candidate.requiresWindow) {
      const window = await this.window(conversation.id);
      if (!window.open) this.throwUnavailable('JANELA_FECHADA');
    }
    return { conversation, candidate };
  }

  private async selectCandidate(reference: ConversationChannelReference): Promise<{
    candidate: Candidate | null; selectedOrigin: ChannelOrigin | null; selectionReason: ChannelAvailability['reason'] | null;
  }> {
    const latest = await this.prisma.message.findFirst({
      where: { conversationId: reference.conversationId },
      orderBy: [{ createdAt: 'desc' }, { id: 'desc' }],
      select: { channelOrigin: true },
    });
    const channels = await this.channels(reference.buId);
    if (latest) {
      const selectedOrigin = latest.channelOrigin as ChannelOrigin;
      const candidate = channels.find((channel) => channel.type === selectedOrigin) ?? null;
      return { candidate, selectedOrigin, selectionReason: candidate ? null : 'CANAL_SELECIONADO_INDISPONIVEL' };
    }
    if (channels.length === 0) return { candidate: null, selectedOrigin: null, selectionReason: 'CANAL_AUSENTE' };
    if (channels.length > 1) return { candidate: null, selectedOrigin: null, selectionReason: 'CANAL_AMBIGUO' };
    return { candidate: channels[0], selectedOrigin: channels[0].type, selectionReason: null };
  }

  private async channels(buId: string): Promise<Candidate[]> {
    return this.prisma.buChannel.findMany({
      where: { buId },
      select: {
        id: true, buId: true, type: true, status: true, generation: true, pairingEpoch: true,
        verifiedEpoch: true, ownNumberVerified: true, providerConnectionState: true,
        receiptVerifiedAt: true, requiresWindow: true,
        providerIdentities: { select: { id: true, buId: true, provider: true, currentChannelId: true, managedLifecycle: true } },
      },
    }) as unknown as Promise<Candidate[]>;
  }

  private async findCandidate(channelId: string, buId: string): Promise<Candidate | null> {
    const rows = await this.prisma.buChannel.findMany({ where: { id: channelId, buId }, select: {
      id: true, buId: true, type: true, status: true, generation: true, pairingEpoch: true,
      verifiedEpoch: true, ownNumberVerified: true, providerConnectionState: true,
      receiptVerifiedAt: true, requiresWindow: true,
      providerIdentities: { select: { id: true, buId: true, provider: true, currentChannelId: true, managedLifecycle: true } },
    } });
    return (rows[0] as unknown as Candidate | undefined) ?? null;
  }

  private async contextStatus(context: ChannelOperationContext, beforeDispatch: boolean): Promise<'CURRENT' | 'CHANGED' | 'UNKNOWN'> {
    const where: Prisma.BuChannelWhereInput = {
      id: context.channelId, buId: context.buId, type: context.channelOrigin,
      generation: context.generation, pairingEpoch: context.pairingEpoch,
      ownNumberVerified: true, verifiedEpoch: context.pairingEpoch,
      ...(beforeDispatch ? {
        status: 'CONECTADO', receiptVerifiedAt: { not: null },
        providerConnectionState: context.channelOrigin === 'OFICIAL' ? 'SUBSCRIBED' : 'CONNECTED',
      } : {}),
    };
    const channel = await this.prisma.buChannel.findFirst({
      where,
      select: { id: true, credential: { select: { version: true, generation: true } },
        providerIdentities: { where: { id: context.providerResourceIdentityId }, select: { id: true, buId: true, provider: true, currentChannelId: true, managedLifecycle: true } } },
    });
    const identity = channel?.providerIdentities[0];
    if (!channel || !identity || identity.buId !== context.buId || identity.provider !== context.channelOrigin
      || identity.currentChannelId !== context.channelId || !identity.managedLifecycle) {
      return 'CHANGED';
    }
    if (context.channelOrigin === 'OFICIAL') {
      const credential = channel.credential;
      if (!credential || credential.generation !== context.generation) return 'CHANGED';
      if (beforeDispatch && credential.version !== context.activeCredentialVersion) return 'CHANGED';
    }
    return 'CURRENT';
  }

  private async window(conversationId: string): Promise<{ open: boolean; expiresAt: Date | null }> {
    const lastInbound = await this.prisma.message.findFirst({
      where: { conversationId, direction: 'IN' }, orderBy: [{ createdAt: 'desc' }, { id: 'desc' }], select: { createdAt: true },
    });
    if (!lastInbound) return { open: false, expiresAt: null };
    const expiresAt = new Date(lastInbound.createdAt.getTime() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    return { open: expiresAt.getTime() > Date.now(), expiresAt };
  }

  private async latestInboundByConversation(conversationIds: string[]): Promise<Map<string, Date>> {
    if (!conversationIds.length) return new Map();
    const rows = await this.prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ conversation_id: string; created_at: Date }>>`
      SELECT requested.conversation_id, latest.created_at
      FROM unnest(ARRAY[${Prisma.join(conversationIds)}]::text[]) AS requested(conversation_id)
      CROSS JOIN LATERAL (
        SELECT message.created_at
        FROM messages AS message
        WHERE message.conversation_id = requested.conversation_id AND message.direction = 'IN'
        ORDER BY message.created_at DESC, message.id DESC
        LIMIT 1
      ) AS latest
    `;
    return new Map(rows.map((row) => [row.conversation_id, row.created_at]));
  }

  private sameCredentialContext(candidate: Candidate, credential: ResolvedChannelCredential): boolean {
    const identity = candidate.providerIdentities.find((item) => item.id === credential.providerResourceIdentityId);
    return credential.channelId === candidate.id && credential.buId === candidate.buId
      && credential.provider === candidate.type && credential.generation === candidate.generation
      && credential.pairingEpoch === candidate.pairingEpoch && identity?.currentChannelId === candidate.id
      && identity?.buId === candidate.buId && identity.provider === candidate.type;
  }

  private unavailable(channelOrigin: ChannelOrigin | null, reason: ChannelAvailability['reason']): ChannelAvailability {
    return { available: false, reason, channelOrigin, requiresWindow: false, windowOpen: false, expiresAt: null };
  }

  private throwUnavailable(reason: ChannelAvailability['reason']): never {
    if (reason === 'CANAL_AUSENTE') throw new BadRequestException({ code: reason, error: 'Esta BU não tem canal conectado.' });
    if (reason === 'CANAL_AMBIGUO') throw new ConflictException({ code: reason, error: 'Selecione uma origem de conversa antes de responder.' });
    throw new ConflictException({ code: reason, error: reason === 'JANELA_FECHADA'
      ? 'Fora da janela de 24h da última mensagem recebida.'
      : 'O canal selecionado não está disponível para envio.' });
  }
}

```

## api-app/src/integrations/evolution/evolution.client.spec.ts

```text
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { EvolutionHttpClient, EvolutionIndisponivelError } from './evolution.client';

describe('EvolutionHttpClient instance creation response', () => {
  const before = {
    url: process.env.EVOLUTION_API_URL,
    key: process.env.EVOLUTION_API_KEY,
    webhook: process.env.EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_URL, secret: process.env.EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET,
  };
  afterEach(() => {
    vi.unstubAllGlobals();
    if (before.url === undefined) delete process.env.EVOLUTION_API_URL; else process.env.EVOLUTION_API_URL = before.url;
    if (before.key === undefined) delete process.env.EVOLUTION_API_KEY; else process.env.EVOLUTION_API_KEY = before.key;
    if (before.webhook === undefined) delete process.env.EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_URL; else process.env.EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_URL = before.webhook;
    if (before.secret === undefined) delete process.env.EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET; else process.env.EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET = before.secret;
  });

  it('returns only QR material when create confirms the exact instance name', async () => {
    process.env.EVOLUTION_API_URL = 'https://evolution.invalid';
    process.env.EVOLUTION_API_KEY = 'fixture-key';
    process.env.EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_URL = 'https://api.invalid/webhooks/evolution';
    process.env.EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET = 'fixture-secret';
    const fetchFake = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, json: async () => ({
      instance: { instanceName: 'lh-channel-1' }, hash: 'must-not-escape', qrcode: { base64: 'fixture-qr' },
    }) });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchFake);
    const result = await new EvolutionHttpClient().criarInstancia('lh-channel-1');
    expect(result).toEqual({ qrcode: 'fixture-qr' });
    expect(JSON.stringify(result)).not.toContain('must-not-escape');
    expect(fetchFake).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    const requestBody = JSON.parse(fetchFake.mock.calls[0][1].body as string);
    expect(requestBody.webhook).toMatchObject({ url: 'https://api.invalid/webhooks/evolution', headers: { 'x-leadshug-webhook-secret': 'fixture-secret' } });
  });

  it.each([
    {},
    { instance: { instanceName: 'another-instance' }, qrcode: { base64: 'must-not-escape' } },
  ])('rejects a successful HTTP response without exact created identity confirmation', async (payload) => {
    process.env.EVOLUTION_API_URL = 'https://evolution.invalid';
    process.env.EVOLUTION_API_KEY = 'fixture-key';
    const fetchFake = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, json: async () => payload });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchFake);
    await expect(new EvolutionHttpClient().criarInstancia('lh-requested-instance')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(EvolutionIndisponivelError);
  });

  it('requires an explicit SUCCESS response for exact-instance cleanup', async () => {
    process.env.EVOLUTION_API_URL = 'https://evolution.invalid';
    process.env.EVOLUTION_API_KEY = 'fixture-key';
    const fetchFake = vi.fn()
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => ({ status: 'SUCCESS', error: false }) })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => ({ status: 'SUCCESS', error: true }) });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchFake);
    const client = new EvolutionHttpClient();
    await expect(client.apagarInstancia('lh-exact-name')).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    await expect(client.apagarInstancia('lh-exact-name')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(EvolutionIndisponivelError);
    expect(String(fetchFake.mock.calls[0][0])).toContain('/instance/delete/lh-exact-name');
  });

  it('rejects redirects without following them or exposing the configured key', async () => {
    process.env.EVOLUTION_API_URL = 'https://evolution.invalid';
    process.env.EVOLUTION_API_KEY = 'fixture-secret-key';
    const fetchFake = vi.fn().mockRejectedValue(new TypeError('redirect fixture with secret URL'));
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchFake);
    const error = await new EvolutionHttpClient().criarInstancia('lh-safe-name').catch((caught: unknown) => caught as Error);
    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(EvolutionIndisponivelError);
    expect(error.message).not.toMatch(/fixture-secret-key|secret URL/);
    expect(fetchFake.mock.calls[0][1].redirect).toBe('error');
  });

  it('returns ACCEPTED only with an external ID and treats HTTP and post-dispatch failures as UNKNOWN once', async () => {
    process.env.EVOLUTION_API_URL = 'https://evolution.invalid';
    process.env.EVOLUTION_API_KEY = 'fixture-key';
    const fetchFake = vi.fn()
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => ({ key: { id: 'evo-id-1' } }) })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => ({ key: {} }) })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: false, status: 503, json: async () => ({ message: 'ambiguous provider body' }) })
      .mockRejectedValueOnce(new TypeError('timeout after dispatch'))
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => { throw new SyntaxError('malformed'); } })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: false, status: 503, json: async () => ({ key: { id: 'contradictory-id' } }) })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => ({}) });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchFake);
    const client = new EvolutionHttpClient();
    await expect(client.enviarTexto('fixture-instance', 'user:opaque', 'hello'))
      .resolves.toMatchObject({ outcome: 'ACCEPTED', externalMessageId: 'evo-id-1' });
    await expect(client.enviarTexto('fixture-instance', 'user:opaque', 'hello'))
      .resolves.toMatchObject({ outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'ACK_WITHOUT_ID' });
    await expect(client.enviarTexto('fixture-instance', 'user:opaque', 'hello'))
      .resolves.toMatchObject({ outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED' });
    await expect(client.enviarTexto('fixture-instance', 'user:opaque', 'hello'))
      .resolves.toMatchObject({ outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED' });
    await expect(client.enviarTexto('fixture-instance', 'user:opaque', 'hello'))
      .resolves.toMatchObject({ outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED' });
    await expect(client.enviarTexto('fixture-instance', 'user:opaque', 'hello'))
      .resolves.toMatchObject({ outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED' });
    await expect(client.enviarTexto('fixture-instance', 'user:opaque', 'hello'))
      .resolves.toMatchObject({ outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED' });
    expect(fetchFake).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(7);
    expect(fetchFake.mock.calls.every(([, options]) => options.signal instanceof AbortSignal)).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

## api-app/src/integrations/evolution/evolution.client.ts

```text
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import type { ChannelSendResult } from '../../domain/channels/transport/channel';

export const EVOLUTION_CLIENT = Symbol('EVOLUTION_CLIENT');

export interface InstanciaCriada {
  qrcode: string | null;
}
export interface InstanciaObservada { name: string; ownerJid: string | null; connectionStatus: string | null; }

/**
 * Porta do canal não oficial. Adaptador de SAÍDA — a Evolution API é serviço
 * interno do projeto, **sem domínio público** (ADR-0002).
 */
export interface EvolutionClient {
  configurado(): boolean;
  criarInstancia(nome: string): Promise<InstanciaCriada>;
  qrDaInstancia(nome: string): Promise<string | null>;
  estadoDaInstancia(nome: string): Promise<string>;
  consultarInstancia(nome: string): Promise<InstanciaObservada | null>;
  apagarInstancia(nome: string): Promise<void>;
  /** Não existe janela aqui — o limite é o risco de ban, não a plataforma. */
  enviarTexto(instancia: string, para: string, texto: string): Promise<ChannelSendResult>;
}

export class EvolutionIndisponivelError extends Error {}

@Injectable()
export class EvolutionHttpClient implements EvolutionClient {
  private readonly base = process.env.EVOLUTION_API_URL ?? '';
  private readonly chave = process.env.EVOLUTION_API_KEY ?? '';
  private readonly webhook = process.env.EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_URL ?? '';
  private readonly webhookSecret = process.env.EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET ?? '';

  configurado(): boolean {
    return this.base.length > 0 && this.chave.length > 0;
  }

  async criarInstancia(nome: string): Promise<InstanciaCriada> {
    const corpo: Record<string, unknown> = {
      instanceName: nome,
      qrcode: true,
      integration: 'WHATSAPP-BAILEYS',
    };
    if (this.webhook && this.webhookSecret) {
      corpo.webhook = {
        enabled: true,
        url: this.webhook,
        headers: { 'x-leadshug-webhook-secret': this.webhookSecret },
        byEvents: false,
        base64: false,
        events: ['MESSAGES_UPSERT', 'CONNECTION_UPDATE'],
      };
    }
    const data = await this.chamar('POST', '/instance/create', corpo);
    const payload = data as { instance?: { instanceName?: unknown }; qrcode?: { base64?: string } | string; base64?: string };
    if (!payload.instance || payload.instance.instanceName !== nome) {
      throw new EvolutionIndisponivelError('A Evolution não confirmou a identidade da instância solicitada.');
    }
    return {
      qrcode:
        (typeof payload.qrcode === 'string' ? payload.qrcode : payload.qrcode?.base64) ??
        payload.base64 ??
        null,
    };
  }

  async qrDaInstancia(nome: string): Promise<string | null> {
    const data = await this.chamar('GET', `/instance/connect/${nome}`);
    const payload = data as { qrcode?: { base64?: string } | string; base64?: string };
    return (typeof payload.qrcode === 'string' ? payload.qrcode : payload.qrcode?.base64) ?? payload.base64 ?? null;
  }

  async estadoDaInstancia(nome: string): Promise<string> {
    const data = await this.chamar('GET', `/instance/connectionState/${nome}`);
    return (data as { instance?: { state?: string } }).instance?.state ?? 'desconhecido';
  }

  async consultarInstancia(nome: string): Promise<InstanciaObservada | null> {
    const query = new URLSearchParams({ instanceName: nome });
    const data = await this.chamar('GET', `/instance/fetchInstances?${query.toString()}`);
    const instances = Array.isArray(data) ? data : [];
    const found = instances.find((item) => item && typeof item === 'object' && (item as { name?: unknown }).name === nome) as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
    if (!found) return null;
    return {
      name: typeof found.name === 'string' ? found.name : nome,
      ownerJid: typeof found.ownerJid === 'string' ? found.ownerJid : null,
      connectionStatus: typeof found.connectionStatus === 'string' ? found.connectionStatus : null,
    };
  }

  async apagarInstancia(nome: string): Promise<void> {
    const result = await this.chamar('DELETE', `/instance/delete/${encodeURIComponent(nome)}`) as { status?: unknown; error?: unknown };
    if (result.status !== 'SUCCESS' || result.error !== false) throw new EvolutionIndisponivelError('A Evolution não confirmou a remoção da instância.');
  }

  async enviarTexto(instancia: string, para: string, texto: string): Promise<ChannelSendResult> {
    if (!instancia || !this.configurado()) return { outcome: 'BLOCKED', reason: 'CHANNEL_NOT_READY' };
    let response: Response;
    try {
      response = await fetch(`${this.base}/message/sendText/${encodeURIComponent(instancia)}`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { apikey: this.chave, 'content-type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ number: para, text: texto }),
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10_000), redirect: 'error',
      });
    } catch {
      return { outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED', legacyCode: 'EVOLUTION_RECUSOU' };
    }
    let data: unknown;
    try {
      data = await response.json();
    } catch {
      return { outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED', legacyCode: 'EVOLUTION_RECUSOU' };
    }
    if (!response.ok) return { outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED', legacyCode: 'EVOLUTION_RECUSOU' };
    if (!isRecord(data)) return { outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED', legacyCode: 'EVOLUTION_RECUSOU' };
    if (data.error) return { outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED', legacyCode: 'EVOLUTION_RECUSOU' };
    if (data.key !== undefined && data.key !== null && !isRecord(data.key)) {
      return { outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED', legacyCode: 'EVOLUTION_RECUSOU' };
    }
    const key = isRecord(data.key) ? data.key : null;
    if (!key) return { outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED', legacyCode: 'EVOLUTION_RECUSOU' };
    if (key && Object.hasOwn(key, 'id') && typeof key.id !== 'string') {
      return { outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED', legacyCode: 'EVOLUTION_RECUSOU' };
    }
    const id = typeof key?.id === 'string' && key.id.trim() ? key.id : null;
    return id
      ? { outcome: 'ACCEPTED', externalMessageId: id }
      : { outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'ACK_WITHOUT_ID', legacyCode: 'EVOLUTION_RECUSOU' };
  }

  private async chamar(metodo: string, caminho: string, corpo?: unknown): Promise<unknown> {
    let res: Response;
    try {
      res = await fetch(`${this.base}${caminho}`, {
        method: metodo,
        headers: { apikey: this.chave, 'content-type': 'application/json' },
        body: corpo ? JSON.stringify(corpo) : undefined,
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10_000), redirect: 'error',
      });
    } catch {
      throw new EvolutionIndisponivelError('A Evolution API está temporariamente indisponível.');
    }
    const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
    if (!res.ok) {
      void data;
      throw new EvolutionIndisponivelError('A Evolution API recusou a operação.');
    }
    return data;
  }
}

function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
}
```

```text
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { MetaHttpClient, MetaIndisponivelError } from './meta.client';

describe('MetaHttpClient scoped credentials', () => {
  const vars = ['META_APP_ID', 'META_APP_SECRET', 'META_SIGNUP_CONFIG_ID', 'GRAPH_VERSION', 'WHATSAPP_TOKEN'];
  const before = new Map(vars.map((key) => [key, process.env[key]]));
  afterEach(() => {
    vi.unstubAllGlobals();
    for (const key of vars) {
      const value = before.get(key);
      if (value === undefined) delete process.env[key]; else process.env[key] = value;
    }
  });

  it('exposes only public installation capability and is unavailable when unset', () => {
    delete process.env.META_APP_ID;
    delete process.env.META_APP_SECRET;
    delete process.env.META_SIGNUP_CONFIG_ID;
    process.env.WHATSAPP_TOKEN = 'fake-legacy-token';
    delete process.env.GRAPH_VERSION;
    expect(new MetaHttpClient().capabilities()).toMatchObject({ available: false, reason: 'NOT_CONFIGURED', appId: null, configId: null, graphVersion: null });
  });

  it('rejects partial installation setup without returning its secret', () => {
    process.env.META_APP_ID = 'fake-app-id';
    process.env.META_APP_SECRET = '';
    process.env.META_SIGNUP_CONFIG_ID = '';
    process.env.GRAPH_VERSION = '';
    expect(new MetaHttpClient().capabilities()).toMatchObject({ available: false, reason: 'PARTIAL_CONFIGURATION', appId: 'fake-app-id' });
  });

  it.each([undefined, '', 'v26', '26.0', 'v26.1', 'vabc.0'])('fails closed for missing, blank, or malformed Graph version (%s)', (version) => {
    process.env.META_APP_ID = 'fake-app-id';
    process.env.META_APP_SECRET = 'fake-secret';
    process.env.META_SIGNUP_CONFIG_ID = 'fake-config';
    if (version === undefined) delete process.env.GRAPH_VERSION; else process.env.GRAPH_VERSION = version;
    expect(new MetaHttpClient().capabilities()).toMatchObject({ available: false, reason: 'PARTIAL_CONFIGURATION', graphVersion: null });
  });

  it('does not call Graph for a malformed configured version', async () => {
    process.env.META_APP_ID = 'fake-app-id';
    process.env.META_APP_SECRET = 'fake-secret';
    process.env.META_SIGNUP_CONFIG_ID = 'fake-config';
    process.env.GRAPH_VERSION = 'https://graph.invalid';
    const fetchFake = vi.fn();
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchFake);
    await expect(new MetaHttpClient().registrarNumero('phone-id', '123456', 'scoped-token')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(MetaIndisponivelError);
    expect(fetchFake).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(new MetaHttpClient().capabilities().graphVersion).toBeNull();
    process.env.GRAPH_VERSION = '';
    await expect(new MetaHttpClient().enviarTexto('phone-id', '5584999990000', 'fixture body', 'scoped-channel-token'))
      .resolves.toMatchObject({ outcome: 'BLOCKED', reason: 'CHANNEL_NOT_READY' });
    expect(fetchFake).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('exchanges code in a POST body and never places it in the URL', async () => {
    process.env.META_APP_ID = 'fake-id';
    process.env.META_APP_SECRET = 'fake-secret';
    process.env.META_SIGNUP_CONFIG_ID = 'fake-config';
    process.env.GRAPH_VERSION = 'v26.0';
    expect(new MetaHttpClient().capabilities()).toMatchObject({ available: true, reason: 'AVAILABLE', graphVersion: 'v26.0' });
    const fetchFake = vi.fn()
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => ({ access_token: 'candidate-token' }) })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => ({ data: { app_id: 'fake-id', is_valid: true, scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management', 'whatsapp_business_messaging'], granular_scopes: [{ scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['123456'] }, { scope: 'whatsapp_business_messaging', target_ids: ['123456'] }] } }) });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchFake);
    const result = await new MetaHttpClient().exchangeCode('sensitive-code');
    expect(result.accessToken).toBe('candidate-token');
    expect(fetchFake.mock.calls[0][0]).not.toContain('sensitive-code');
    expect(String(fetchFake.mock.calls[0][1].body)).toContain('code=sensitive-code');
  });

  it('does not use legacy token and emits a safe error for provider details', async () => {
    process.env.WHATSAPP_TOKEN = 'legacy-token-must-not-be-used';
    const fetchFake = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ ok: false, status: 400, json: async () => ({ error: { message: 'secret provider detail' } }) });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchFake);
    await expect(new MetaHttpClient().enviarTexto('phone-id', '558400000000', 'hello'))
      .resolves.toMatchObject({ outcome: 'BLOCKED', reason: 'PENDING_CREDENTIAL' });
    expect(fetchFake).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('classifies only allowlisted synchronous Graph refusals as REJECTED', async () => {
    process.env.GRAPH_VERSION = 'v26.0';
    const fetchFake = vi.fn()
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: false, status: 400, json: async () => ({ error: { message: 'bad request', code: 100, is_transient: false } }) })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: false, status: 400, json: async () => ({ error: { message: 'unknown refusal', code: 999, is_transient: false } }) })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => ({ messages: [{ id: 'wamid-1' }] }) })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => ({ messages: [{}] }) })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => { throw new SyntaxError('malformed'); } })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: false, status: 503, json: async () => ({ messages: [{ id: 'contradictory-id' }] }) })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: false, status: 400, json: async () => ({
        error: { message: 'refusal with contradictory accepted ID', code: 100, is_transient: false }, messages: [{ id: 'contradictory-refusal-id' }],
      }) })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: false, status: 400, json: async () => ({
        error: { message: 'refusal with contradictory success', code: 100, is_transient: false }, success: true,
      }) })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: false, status: 400, json: async () => ({
        error: { message: 'malformed transient flag', code: 100, is_transient: 'false' },
      }) })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: false, status: 400, json: async () => ({
        error: { message: 'later acknowledgement ID', code: 100, is_transient: false }, messages: [{}, { id: 'later-id' }],
      }) })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: false, status: 400, json: async () => ({
        error: { message: 'malformed message ID', code: 100, is_transient: false }, messages: [{ id: 42 }],
      }) })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => ({ messages: [{}, { id: 'second-id' }] }) })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => ({ messages: [{ id: 42 }] }) })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => ({ success: false, messages: [{ id: 'ack-with-false-success' }] }) })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => ({ success: false, messages: [{}] }) })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => ({ success: 'false', messages: [{ id: 'malformed-success' }] }) });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchFake);
    const client = new MetaHttpClient();
    await expect(client.enviarTexto('phone-id', '558400000000', 'hello', 'scoped'))
      .resolves.toMatchObject({ outcome: 'REJECTED', code: 'META_RECUSOU' });
    await expect(client.enviarTexto('phone-id', '558400000000', 'hello', 'scoped'))
      .resolves.toMatchObject({ outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED' });
    await expect(client.enviarTexto('phone-id', '558400000000', 'hello', 'scoped'))
      .resolves.toMatchObject({ outcome: 'ACCEPTED', externalMessageId: 'wamid-1' });
    await expect(client.enviarTexto('phone-id', '558400000000', 'hello', 'scoped'))
      .resolves.toMatchObject({ outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'ACK_WITHOUT_ID' });
    await expect(client.enviarTexto('phone-id', '558400000000', 'hello', 'scoped'))
      .resolves.toMatchObject({ outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED' });
    await expect(client.enviarTexto('phone-id', '558400000000', 'hello', 'scoped'))
      .resolves.toMatchObject({ outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED' });
    await expect(client.enviarTexto('phone-id', '558400000000', 'hello', 'scoped'))

    await expect(client.enviarTexto('phone-id', '558400000000', 'hello', 'scoped'))

    await expect(client.enviarTexto('phone-id', '558400000000', 'hello', 'scoped'))

    await expect(client.enviarTexto('phone-id', '558400000000', 'hello', 'scoped'))

    await expect(client.enviarTexto('phone-id', '558400000000', 'hello', 'scoped'))

    await expect(client.enviarTexto('phone-id', '558400000000', 'hello', 'scoped'))

    await expect(client.enviarTexto('phone-id', '558400000000', 'hello', 'scoped'))
      .resolves.toMatchObject({ outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED' });
    expect(fetchFake).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(16);
  });

  it('requires both scoped permissions on the same WABA before listing phone numbers', async () => {
    process.env.META_APP_ID = 'fake-id';
    process.env.META_APP_SECRET = 'fake-secret';
    process.env.META_SIGNUP_CONFIG_ID = 'fake-config';
    process.env.GRAPH_VERSION = 'v26.0';
    const tokenInfo = { data: { app_id: 'fake-id', is_valid: true, scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management', 'whatsapp_business_messaging'], granular_scopes: [

  it('verifies the selected phone on the provider-returned page cursor and rejects a foreign phone', async () => {
    process.env.META_APP_ID = 'fake-id';
    process.env.META_APP_SECRET = 'fake-secret';
    process.env.META_SIGNUP_CONFIG_ID = 'fake-config';
    process.env.GRAPH_VERSION = 'v26.0';
    const fetchFake = vi.fn()
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => ({ data: { app_id: 'fake-id', is_valid: true, scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management', 'whatsapp_business_messaging'], granular_scopes: [
        { scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['123456'] },
        { scope: 'whatsapp_business_messaging', target_ids: ['123456'] },
      ] } }) })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => ({ data: [{ id: '987654321', display_phone_number: '+55 84 99999-0000' }] }) });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchFake);
    const client = new MetaHttpClient();
    const number = await client.confirmarNumero('123456', '987654321', 'candidate', 'provider-cursor-page-2');
    expect(number).toMatchObject({ id: '987654321', telefone: '5584999990000' });
    const edgeUrl = String(fetchFake.mock.calls[1][0]);
    expect(edgeUrl).toContain('/v26.0/123456/phone_numbers');
    expect(new URL(edgeUrl).searchParams.get('after')).toBe('provider-cursor-page-2');
    expect(String(fetchFake.mock.calls[0][1].headers.authorization)).toBe('Bearer fake-id|fake-secret');

    const foreign = vi.fn()
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => ({ data: { app_id: 'fake-id', is_valid: true, scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management', 'whatsapp_business_messaging'], granular_scopes: [
        { scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['123456'] },
        { scope: 'whatsapp_business_messaging', target_ids: ['123456'] },
      ] } }) })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => ({ data: [{ id: '987654321' }] }) });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', foreign);
    await expect(client.confirmarNumero('123456', '111111111', 'candidate', 'provider-cursor-page-2')).resolves.toBeNull();
  });

  it('verifies a persisted phone on the second scoped page without following provider URLs', async () => {
    process.env.META_APP_ID = 'fake-id'; process.env.META_APP_SECRET = 'fake-secret'; process.env.META_SIGNUP_CONFIG_ID = 'fake-config'; process.env.GRAPH_VERSION = 'v26.0';
    const grant = { data: { app_id: 'fake-id', is_valid: true, scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management', 'whatsapp_business_messaging'], granular_scopes: [
      { scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['123456'] }, { scope: 'whatsapp_business_messaging', target_ids: ['123456'] },
    ] } };
    const fetchFake = vi.fn()
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => grant })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => ({ data: [{ id: '111111111' }], paging: { next: 'https://foreign.invalid/secret', cursors: { after: 'cursor-page-2' } } }) })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => ({ data: [{ id: '987654321', display_phone_number: '+55 84 99999-0000' }] }) });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchFake);
    const number = await new MetaHttpClient().verificarNumeroVinculado('123456', '987654321', 'scoped-candidate');

    expect(fetchFake).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3);
    expect(fetchFake.mock.calls[0][1].headers.authorization).toBe('Bearer fake-id|fake-secret');
    for (const [url, options] of fetchFake.mock.calls) {
      expect(String(url)).toMatch(/^https:\/\/graph\.facebook\.com\/v26\.0\//);
      expect(options.redirect).toBe('error');
    }
    expect(fetchFake.mock.calls[1][1].headers.authorization).toBe('Bearer scoped-candidate');
    expect(new URL(String(fetchFake.mock.calls[2][0])).searchParams.get('after')).toBe('cursor-page-2');
  });

      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => grant })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => ({ data: [{ id: '987654321', display_phone_number: '+55 84 99999-0000' }] }) });

  it('fails closed at the page budget and for repeated or oversized cursors', async () => {
    process.env.META_APP_ID = 'fake-id'; process.env.META_APP_SECRET = 'fake-secret'; process.env.META_SIGNUP_CONFIG_ID = 'fake-config'; process.env.GRAPH_VERSION = 'v26.0';
    const grant = { data: { app_id: 'fake-id', is_valid: true, scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management', 'whatsapp_business_messaging'], granular_scopes: [
      { scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['123456'] }, { scope: 'whatsapp_business_messaging', target_ids: ['123456'] },
    ] } };
    const page = (after: string) => ({ ok: true, json: async () => ({ data: [], paging: { next: 'https://graph.facebook.com/next', cursors: { after } } }) });
    const beyondBudget = vi.fn().mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => grant }).mockResolvedValueOnce(page('cursor-one')).mockResolvedValueOnce(page('cursor-two'));
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', beyondBudget);
    const beyondBudgetOperation = new MetaHttpClient().verificarNumeroVinculado('123456', '987654321', 'scoped-candidate');
    await expect(beyondBudgetOperation).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(MetaIndisponivelError);
    await expect(beyondBudgetOperation).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'META_INDISPONIVEL' });
    expect(beyondBudget).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3);

    const cyclic = vi.fn().mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => grant }).mockResolvedValueOnce(page('cursor-one')).mockResolvedValueOnce(page('cursor-one'));
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', cyclic);
    const cyclicOperation = new MetaHttpClient().verificarNumeroVinculado('123456', '987654321', 'scoped-candidate');
    await expect(cyclicOperation).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(MetaIndisponivelError);
    await expect(cyclicOperation).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'META_INDISPONIVEL' });
    expect(cyclic).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3);

    const oversized = vi.fn().mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => grant }).mockResolvedValueOnce(page('x'.repeat(513)));
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', oversized);
    const oversizedOperation = new MetaHttpClient().verificarNumeroVinculado('123456', '987654321', 'scoped-candidate');
    await expect(oversizedOperation).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(MetaIndisponivelError);
    await expect(oversizedOperation).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'META_INDISPONIVEL' });
    expect(oversized).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });

  it('exposes a bounded page cursor only when the provider signals a next page', async () => {
    process.env.META_APP_ID = 'fake-id';
    process.env.META_APP_SECRET = 'fake-secret';
    process.env.META_SIGNUP_CONFIG_ID = 'fake-config';
    process.env.GRAPH_VERSION = 'v26.0';
    const grant = { data: { app_id: 'fake-id', is_valid: true, scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management', 'whatsapp_business_messaging'], granular_scopes: [
      { scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['123456'] }, { scope: 'whatsapp_business_messaging', target_ids: ['123456'] },
    ] } };
    const fetchFake = vi.fn()
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => grant })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => ({ data: [{ id: '987654321' }], paging: { cursors: { after: 'cursor-last-page' } } }) });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchFake);
    await expect(new MetaHttpClient().recursos('candidate', '123456')).resolves.toMatchObject({ next: null });
    fetchFake.mockReset()
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => grant })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => ({ data: [], paging: { next: 'https://provider.invalid/page?secret=hidden', cursors: { after: 'safe-cursor' } } }) });
    await expect(new MetaHttpClient().recursos('candidate', '123456')).resolves.toMatchObject({ next: 'safe-cursor' });
  });

  it('rejects redirects and never follows provider locations with registration credentials', async () => {
    process.env.GRAPH_VERSION = 'v26.0';
    const fetchFake = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ ok: false, status: 307, json: async () => ({ location: 'https://foreign.invalid' }) });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchFake);
    await expect(new MetaHttpClient().registrarNumero('phone-id', '123456', 'candidate-token')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(MetaIndisponivelError);
    expect(fetchFake).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(fetchFake.mock.calls[0][1].redirect).toBe('error');
  });

  it('does not query a WABA outside the currently validated app grant', async () => {
    process.env.META_APP_ID = 'fake-id';
    process.env.META_APP_SECRET = 'fake-secret';
    process.env.META_SIGNUP_CONFIG_ID = 'fake-config';
    process.env.GRAPH_VERSION = 'v26.0';
    const fetchFake = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, json: async () => ({ data: { app_id: 'fake-id', is_valid: true, scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management', 'whatsapp_business_messaging'], granular_scopes: [
      { scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['123456'] },
      { scope: 'whatsapp_business_messaging', target_ids: ['123456'] },
    ] } }) });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchFake);
    await expect(new MetaHttpClient().confirmarNumero('654321', '987654321', 'candidate')).resolves.toBeNull();
    expect(fetchFake).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it.each([
    ['register', 'registrarNumero'],
    ['subscription', 'inscreverWaba'],
  ] as const)('requires an explicit provider success acknowledgement for %s', async (_label, method) => {
    process.env.GRAPH_VERSION = 'v26.0';
    const client = new MetaHttpClient();
    for (const response of [
      { success: false },
      { success: 'false' },
      { success: 'TRUE' },
      {},
    ]) {
      vi.stubGlobal('fetch', vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, json: async () => response }));
      await expect(method === 'registrarNumero'
        ? client.registrarNumero('phone-id', '123456', 'scoped-token')
        : client.inscreverWaba('waba-id', 'scoped-token')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(MetaIndisponivelError);
    }
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, json: async () => ({ success: true }) }));
    await expect(method === 'registrarNumero'
      ? client.registrarNumero('phone-id', '123456', 'scoped-token')
      : client.inscreverWaba('waba-id', 'scoped-token')).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, json: async () => ({ success: 'true' }) }));
    await expect(method === 'registrarNumero'
      ? client.registrarNumero('phone-id', '123456', 'scoped-token')
      : client.inscreverWaba('waba-id', 'scoped-token')).resolves.toBeUndefined();
  });
});
```

## api-app/src/integrations/meta/meta.client.ts

```text
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import type { ChannelSendResult } from '../../domain/channels/transport/channel';

export const META_CLIENT = Symbol('META_CLIENT');

export interface MetaNumber {
  id: string;
  telefone: string | null;
  nomeVerificado: string | null;
}

export interface MetaCapabilities {
  appId: string | null;
  configId: string | null;
  graphVersion: string | null;
  available: boolean;
  reason: 'AVAILABLE' | 'NOT_CONFIGURED' | 'PARTIAL_CONFIGURATION';
}

/** Every customer operation is explicitly scoped to a decrypted channel token. */
export interface MetaClient {
  configurado(): boolean;
  canSendText(): boolean;
  capabilities(): MetaCapabilities;
  exchangeCode(code: string): Promise<{ accessToken: string }>;
  recursos(accessToken: string, wabaId?: string, after?: string): Promise<{ wabas: Array<{ id: string; name: string | null }>; numbers: MetaNumber[]; next: string | null }>;
  numerosDaWaba(wabaId: string, accessToken?: string, after?: string): Promise<{ data: MetaNumber[]; next: string | null }>;
  confirmarNumero(wabaId: string, phoneNumberId: string, accessToken: string, after?: string): Promise<MetaNumber | null>;
  verificarNumeroVinculado(wabaId: string, phoneNumberId: string, accessToken: string): Promise<MetaNumber | null>;
  registrarNumero(phoneNumberId: string, pin: string, accessToken: string): Promise<void>;
  inscreverWaba(wabaId: string, accessToken: string): Promise<void>;
  enviarTexto(phoneNumberId: string, para: string, texto: string, accessToken?: string): Promise<ChannelSendResult>;
}

export class MetaIndisponivelError extends Error {
  readonly code = 'META_INDISPONIVEL';
  constructor(readonly detalhe = 'A Meta recusou a operação.') { super(detalhe); }
}

type MetaGranularScope = { scope: string; targetIds?: string[] };
type MetaDebugGrant = { appId: string; valid: boolean; scopes: string[]; granularScopes: MetaGranularScope[] };

@Injectable()
export class MetaHttpClient implements MetaClient {
  private readonly appId = process.env.META_APP_ID ?? '';
  private readonly appSecret = process.env.META_APP_SECRET ?? '';
  private readonly configId = process.env.META_SIGNUP_CONFIG_ID ?? '';
  private readonly graphVersion = process.env.GRAPH_VERSION?.trim() ?? '';

  private graphVersionValida(): boolean { return /^v\d+\.0$/.test(this.graphVersion); }

  capabilities(): MetaCapabilities {
    const configured = [this.appId, this.appSecret, this.configId, this.graphVersion].filter(Boolean).length;
    const graphValid = this.graphVersionValida();
    const available = configured === 4 && graphValid;
    return {
      appId: this.appId || null,
      configId: this.configId || null,
      graphVersion: graphValid ? this.graphVersion : null,
      available,
      reason: available ? 'AVAILABLE' : configured === 0 ? 'NOT_CONFIGURED' : 'PARTIAL_CONFIGURATION',
    };
  }

  /** Compatibility probe for callers being retired; never supplies a credential. */
  configurado(): boolean { return this.capabilities().available; }
  canSendText(): boolean { return this.graphVersionValida(); }

  async exchangeCode(code: string): Promise<{ accessToken: string }> {
    if (!this.capabilities().available) throw new MetaIndisponivelError('Autorização Meta indisponível.');
    const body = new URLSearchParams({
      client_id: this.appId,
      client_secret: this.appSecret,
      code,
    });
    const result = await this.request('/oauth/access_token', undefined, 'POST', body);
    const accessToken = typeof result.access_token === 'string' ? result.access_token : '';
    if (!accessToken) throw new MetaIndisponivelError('A Meta não concluiu a autorização.');
    await this.validateAccessToken(accessToken);
    return { accessToken };
  }

  async recursos(accessToken: string, wabaId?: string, after?: string) {
    if (!accessToken) throw new MetaIndisponivelError('Credencial scoped do canal necessária.');
    const grant = this.assertCurrentGrant(await this.debugToken(accessToken));
    const wabas = wabaTargets(grant.granularScopes);
    if (!wabaId) return { wabas: wabas.slice(0, 100).map((id) => ({ id, name: null })), numbers: [], next: null };
    if (!wabas.includes(wabaId)) throw new MetaIndisponivelError('WABA não pertence à autorização atual.');
    const page = await this.numerosDaWaba(wabaId, accessToken, after);
    return { wabas: [{ id: wabaId, name: null }], numbers: page.data.slice(0, 100), next: page.next };
  }

  private async validateAccessToken(accessToken: string) {
    this.assertCurrentGrant(await this.debugToken(accessToken));
  }

  private assertCurrentGrant(value: unknown): MetaDebugGrant {
    const grant = parseMetaDebugGrant(value);
    const scopes = new Set([...grant.scopes, ...grant.granularScopes.map(({ scope }) => scope)]);
    if (grant.appId !== this.appId || !grant.valid || !scopes.has('whatsapp_business_management') || !scopes.has('whatsapp_business_messaging') || wabaTargets(grant.granularScopes).length === 0) {
      throw new MetaIndisponivelError('A autorização não concede identidade e escopos Meta necessários.');
    }
    return grant;
  }

  private async debugToken(inputToken: string) {
    const query = new URLSearchParams({ input_token: inputToken });
    const response = await this.request(`/debug_token?${query.toString()}`, `${this.appId}|${this.appSecret}`);
    return response.data;
  }

  async numerosDaWaba(wabaId: string, accessToken: string, after?: string) {
    if (!accessToken) throw new MetaIndisponivelError('Credencial scoped do canal necessária.');
    const url = new URL(`/${encodeURIComponent(wabaId)}/phone_numbers`, `https://graph.facebook.com/${this.graphVersion}`);
    url.searchParams.set('fields', 'id,display_phone_number,verified_name');
    url.searchParams.set('limit', '100');
    if (after) url.searchParams.set('after', after);
    const result = await this.request(url.pathname + url.search, accessToken);
    if (!Array.isArray(result.data)) throw new MetaIndisponivelError('A resposta da Meta está estruturalmente inválida.');
    const rows = result.data.map((row) => {
      if (!isRecord(row) || typeof row.id !== 'string' || row.id.length === 0) {
        throw new MetaIndisponivelError('A resposta da Meta está estruturalmente inválida.');
      }
      const digits = typeof row.display_phone_number === 'string' ? row.display_phone_number.replace(/\D/g, '') : '';
      return { id: row.id, telefone: digits || null, nomeVerificado: typeof row.verified_name === 'string' ? row.verified_name : null };
    });
    const data = rows;
    const paging = result.paging;
    if (paging !== undefined && paging !== null && !isRecord(paging)) {
      throw new MetaIndisponivelError('A resposta da Meta está estruturalmente inválida.');
    }
    const providerNext = isRecord(paging) ? paging.next : undefined;
    if (providerNext !== undefined && providerNext !== null && typeof providerNext !== 'string') {
      throw new MetaIndisponivelError('A resposta da Meta está estruturalmente inválida.');
    }
    const hasNext = typeof providerNext === 'string';
    const cursors = isRecord(paging) ? paging.cursors : undefined;
    if (cursors !== undefined && cursors !== null && !isRecord(cursors)) {
      throw new MetaIndisponivelError('A resposta da Meta está estruturalmente inválida.');
    }
    const next = isRecord(cursors) ? cursors.after : undefined;
    if (hasNext && (typeof next !== 'string' || next.length === 0 || next.length > 512)) {
      throw new MetaIndisponivelError('A verificação do número está inconclusiva.');
    }
    return { data, next: hasNext ? next as string : null };
  }

  async confirmarNumero(wabaId: string, phoneNumberId: string, accessToken: string, after?: string) {
    const grant = this.assertCurrentGrant(await this.debugToken(accessToken));
    const targets = wabaTargets(grant.granularScopes);
    if (!targets.includes(wabaId)) return null;
    const page = await this.numerosDaWaba(wabaId, accessToken, after);
    return page.data.find((item) => item.id === phoneNumberId) ?? null;
  }

  /** Revalidates a persisted phone identity across a small, fixed number of pages. */
  async verificarNumeroVinculado(wabaId: string, phoneNumberId: string, accessToken: string) {
    if (!accessToken) throw new MetaIndisponivelError('Credencial scoped do canal necessária.');
    const grant = this.assertCurrentGrant(await this.debugToken(accessToken));
    if (!wabaTargets(grant.granularScopes).includes(wabaId)) return null;

    const cursors = new Set<string>();
    let after: string | undefined;
    for (let pageIndex = 0; pageIndex < 2; pageIndex += 1) {
      const page = await this.numerosDaWaba(wabaId, accessToken, after);
      const number = page.data.find((item) => item.id === phoneNumberId);
      if (number) return number;
      if (!page.next) return null;
      if (page.next.length > 512 || cursors.has(page.next)) {
        throw new MetaIndisponivelError('A verificação do número está inconclusiva.');
      }
      if (pageIndex === 1) throw new MetaIndisponivelError('A verificação do número está inconclusiva.');
      cursors.add(page.next);
      after = page.next;
    }
    throw new MetaIndisponivelError('A verificação do número está inconclusiva.');
  }

  async registrarNumero(phoneNumberId: string, pin: string, accessToken: string) {
    const result = await this.request(`/${encodeURIComponent(phoneNumberId)}/register`, accessToken, 'POST', { messaging_product: 'whatsapp', pin });
    if (result.success !== true && result.success !== 'true') throw new MetaIndisponivelError('A Meta não confirmou o registro do número.');
  }

  async inscreverWaba(wabaId: string, accessToken: string) {
    const result = await this.request(`/${encodeURIComponent(wabaId)}/subscribed_apps`, accessToken, 'POST', {});
    if (result.success !== true && result.success !== 'true') throw new MetaIndisponivelError('A Meta não confirmou a inscrição do webhook.');
  }

  async enviarTexto(phoneNumberId: string, para: string, texto: string, accessToken?: string): Promise<ChannelSendResult> {
    if (!accessToken) return { outcome: 'BLOCKED', reason: 'PENDING_CREDENTIAL' };
    if (!phoneNumberId || !this.graphVersionValida()) return { outcome: 'BLOCKED', reason: 'CHANNEL_NOT_READY' };
    let response: Response;
    try {
      response = await fetch(`https://graph.facebook.com/${this.graphVersion}/${encodeURIComponent(phoneNumberId)}/messages`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { accept: 'application/json', authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}`, 'content-type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ messaging_product: 'whatsapp', to: para, type: 'text', text: { body: texto } }),
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10_000), redirect: 'error',
      });
    } catch {
      return { outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED', legacyCode: 'META_RECUSOU' };
    }

    let payload: unknown;
    try {
      payload = await response.json();
    } catch {
      return { outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED', legacyCode: 'META_RECUSOU' };
    }
    if (!isRecord(payload)) {
      return { outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED', legacyCode: 'META_RECUSOU' };
    }
    if (payload.messages !== undefined && !Array.isArray(payload.messages)) {
      return { outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED', legacyCode: 'META_RECUSOU' };
    }
    if (payload.error !== undefined && payload.error !== null && !isRecord(payload.error)) {
      return { outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED', legacyCode: 'META_RECUSOU' };
    }
    const messages = Array.isArray(payload.messages) ? payload.messages : [];
    const first = messages[0];
    const validMessageEnvelope = messages.every((message) => isRecord(message)
      && (message.id === undefined || (typeof message.id === 'string' && message.id.trim().length > 0)));
    const id = messages.length === 1 && isRecord(first) && typeof first.id === 'string' && first.id.trim() ? first.id : null;
    const error = isRecord(payload.error) ? payload.error : null;
    const successIndicatorValid = payload.success === undefined || typeof payload.success === 'boolean';
    const contradictedFailure = payload.success === false;
    if (response.ok && successIndicatorValid && !contradictedFailure && !error && id) {
      return { outcome: 'ACCEPTED', externalMessageId: id };
    }

    const refusalStatus = response.status === 400 || response.status === 401 || response.status === 403;
    const validGraphError = error !== null
      && typeof error.message === 'string' && error.message.length > 0
      && typeof error.code === 'number';
    const transientFieldValid = error && (error.is_transient === undefined || typeof error.is_transient === 'boolean');
    const contradictorySuccess = payload.success === true;
    const stableRefusal = validGraphError && transientFieldValid && error.is_transient !== true
      && [10, 100, 190].includes(error.code as number);
    if (refusalStatus && stableRefusal && successIndicatorValid && !contradictorySuccess
      && (payload.messages === undefined || messages.length === 0)) {
      return { outcome: 'REJECTED', code: 'META_RECUSOU' };
    }
    const validAckWithoutId = Array.isArray(payload.messages) && messages.length === 1
      && validMessageEnvelope && isRecord(first) && first.id === undefined;
    if (response.ok && successIndicatorValid && !contradictedFailure && !error && validAckWithoutId) {
      return { outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'ACK_WITHOUT_ID', legacyCode: 'META_RECUSOU' };
    }
    return { outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED', legacyCode: 'META_RECUSOU' };
  }

  private async request(path: string, accessToken?: string, method: 'GET' | 'POST' = 'GET', body?: unknown) {
    if (!this.graphVersionValida()) throw new MetaIndisponivelError('Versão Graph não configurada.');
    const headers: Record<string, string> = { accept: 'application/json' };
    if (accessToken) headers.authorization = `Bearer ${accessToken}`;
    if (body instanceof URLSearchParams) headers['content-type'] = 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded';
    else if (body !== undefined) headers['content-type'] = 'application/json';
    let response: Response;
    try {
      response = await fetch(`https://graph.facebook.com/${this.graphVersion}${path}`, {
        method, headers, body: body instanceof URLSearchParams ? body.toString() : body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10_000), redirect: 'error',
      });
    } catch {
      throw new MetaIndisponivelError('A Meta está temporariamente indisponível.');
    }
    if (!response.ok) throw new MetaIndisponivelError('A Meta recusou a operação.');
    let payload: unknown;
    try {
      payload = await response.json();
    } catch {
      throw new MetaIndisponivelError('A resposta da Meta está temporariamente indisponível.');
    }
    if (!isRecord(payload)) throw new MetaIndisponivelError('A resposta da Meta está estruturalmente inválida.');
    return payload;
  }
}

function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
}

function parseMetaDebugGrant(value: unknown): MetaDebugGrant {
  if (!isRecord(value) || typeof value.app_id !== 'string' || typeof value.is_valid !== 'boolean') {
    throw new MetaIndisponivelError('A resposta da Meta está estruturalmente inválida.');
  }
  const scopes = value.scopes === undefined ? [] : parseStringArray(value.scopes);
  const rawGranularScopes = value.granular_scopes === undefined ? [] : value.granular_scopes;
  if (!Array.isArray(rawGranularScopes)) throw new MetaIndisponivelError('A resposta da Meta está estruturalmente inválida.');
  const granularScopes = rawGranularScopes.map((entry): MetaGranularScope => {
    if (!isRecord(entry) || typeof entry.scope !== 'string' || entry.scope.length === 0) {
      throw new MetaIndisponivelError('A resposta da Meta está estruturalmente inválida.');
    }
    const targetIds = entry.target_ids === undefined ? undefined : parseStringArray(entry.target_ids);
    return { scope: entry.scope, ...(targetIds === undefined ? {} : { targetIds }) };
  });
  return { appId: value.app_id, valid: value.is_valid, scopes, granularScopes };
}

function parseStringArray(value: unknown): string[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(value) || value.some((item) => typeof item !== 'string')) {
    throw new MetaIndisponivelError('A resposta da Meta está estruturalmente inválida.');
  }
  return value;
}

function wabaTargets(granular: MetaGranularScope[]): string[] {
  const byScope = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
  for (const item of granular) {
    if (!['whatsapp_business_management', 'whatsapp_business_messaging'].includes(item.scope)) continue;
    const targets = byScope.get(item.scope) ?? new Set<string>();
    for (const id of item.targetIds ?? []) if (/^\d{5,}$/.test(id)) targets.add(id);
    byScope.set(item.scope!, targets);
  }
  const management = byScope.get('whatsapp_business_management') ?? new Set<string>();
  const messaging = byScope.get('whatsapp_business_messaging') ?? new Set<string>();
  return [...management].filter((id) => messaging.has(id));
}
```

## api-app/src/surfaces/bff/bff.module.ts

```text
import { MiddlewareConsumer, Module, NestModule, RequestMethod } from '@nestjs/common';
import { AccessService } from '../../domain/access/access.service';
import { IdentityService } from '../../domain/identity/identity.service';
import { LoginAttemptService } from '../../domain/identity/login-attempt.service';
import { PasswordService } from '../../domain/identity/password.service';
import { SessionService } from '../../infra/session/session.service';
import { ChannelsService } from '../../domain/channels/channels.service';
import { NumberRegistrationService } from '../../domain/channels/number-registration.service';
import { NumberRegistrationsController } from './number-registrations.controller';
import { ConversationsService } from '../../domain/inbox/conversations.service';
import { HistoricoService } from '../../domain/inbox/historico.service';
import { ApiKeyService } from '../../domain/integration/api-key.service';
import { TenancyService } from '../../domain/tenancy/tenancy.service';
import { ModulosService } from '../../domain/produto/modulos.service';
import { SetorService } from '../../domain/setor/setor.service';
import { RegistroDeAtividade } from '../../domain/historico/registro.service';
import { AtividadesService } from '../../domain/historico/atividades.service';
import { OnboardingService } from '../../domain/produto/onboarding.service';
import { PainelService } from '../../domain/painel/painel.service';
import { ArmazenamentoDeArquivo, ArmazenamentoEmDisco } from '../../infra/arquivos/armazenamento';
import { EnvioDeEmail, ResendEmail } from '../../integrations/email/email.client';
import { TokensService } from '../../domain/acesso/tokens.service';
import { ConvitesService } from '../../domain/acesso/convites.service';
import { LimiteDeRecuperacao } from '../../domain/acesso/limite-de-recuperacao.service';
import { ChannelTransportModule } from '../../integrations/channels/channel-transport.module';
import { AuthController } from './auth.controller';
import { ChannelsController } from './channels.controller';
import { ConversationsController } from './conversations.controller';
import { UsuariosService } from '../../domain/identity/usuarios.service';
import { HistoricoController } from './historico.controller';
import { UsuariosController } from './usuarios.controller';
import { IntegracaoController } from './integracao.controller';
import { BusinessUnitsController, MeController } from './me.controller';
import { SetoresController } from './setores.controller';
import { AtividadesController } from './atividades.controller';
import { OnboardingController } from './onboarding.controller';
import { PainelController } from './painel.controller';
import { ArquivosController } from './arquivos.controller';
import { SessionMiddleware } from './session.middleware';
import { TYPEBOT_CLIENT, TypebotHttpClient } from '../../integrations/typebot/typebot.client';

/**
 * Superfície BFF (ADR-0006): o que as telas consomem, autenticado por sessão.
 *
 * O middleware é montado no PREFIXO. É isso que faz o guard ser da superfície e
 * não da rota, e é o que impede o defeito que já mordeu os dois sistemas: no
 * Hub, um router.use(requireAuth) sem path interceptou o webhook público.
 */
@Module({
  imports: [ChannelTransportModule],
  controllers: [
    AuthController,
    MeController,
    BusinessUnitsController,
    ChannelsController,
    NumberRegistrationsController,
    ConversationsController,
    IntegracaoController,
    HistoricoController,
    UsuariosController,
    SetoresController,
    AtividadesController,
    OnboardingController,
    PainelController,
    ArquivosController,
  ],
  providers: [
    SessionService,
    IdentityService,
    PasswordService,
    LoginAttemptService,
    AccessService,
    ChannelsService,
    NumberRegistrationService,
    ConversationsService,
    HistoricoService,
    UsuariosService,
    ApiKeyService,
    TenancyService,
    ModulosService,
    SetorService,
    RegistroDeAtividade,
    AtividadesService,
    OnboardingService,
    PainelService,
    { provide: ArmazenamentoDeArquivo, useClass: ArmazenamentoEmDisco },
    { provide: EnvioDeEmail, useClass: ResendEmail },
    TokensService,
    ConvitesService,
    LimiteDeRecuperacao,
    // Adaptadores de SAÍDA por porta (ADR-0006, Emenda 1): o domínio depende da
    // interface, não do HTTP. É o que deixa o teste provar permissão sem a Meta
    // nem a Evolution no ar.
    { provide: TYPEBOT_CLIENT, useClass: TypebotHttpClient },
  ],
  exports: [SessionService, PasswordService],
})
export class BffModule implements NestModule {
  configure(consumer: MiddlewareConsumer): void {
    consumer
      .apply(SessionMiddleware)
      .forRoutes({ path: 'api/bff/{*path}', method: RequestMethod.ALL });
  }
}
```
