## api-app/test/channel-transport.e2e-spec.ts

```text
import { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import request from 'supertest';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { sealChannelCredential } from '../src/domain/channels/channel-credential-crypto';
import { OutboundService } from '../src/domain/inbox/outbound.service';
import { Channel, type ChannelSendResult, type TextMessage } from '../src/domain/channels/transport/channel';
import { ChannelAdapterFactory } from '../src/integrations/channels/channel-adapter-factory';
import { ChannelAdapterRegistry } from '../src/integrations/channels/channel-adapter-registry';
import type { ResolvedChannelCredential } from '../src/domain/channels/channel-credential-resolver';
import { MetaChannelFactory } from '../src/integrations/channels/meta-channel';
import { EvolutionChannelFactory } from '../src/integrations/channels/evolution-channel';
import { MetaHttpClient } from '../src/integrations/meta/meta.client';
import {
  contratarCanalNaoOficial, criarCanalVerificado, criarMantenedora, evolutionFake, limparBanco, login, metaFake, subirApp,
} from './helpers';

class OpaqueRecipientChannel extends Channel {
  constructor(private readonly sent: TextMessage[]) { super(); }
  async sendMessage(message: TextMessage): Promise<ChannelSendResult> {
    this.sent.push(message);
    return { outcome: 'ACCEPTED', externalMessageId: `opaque-fake-${this.sent.length}` };
  }
}

class OpaqueRecipientFactory extends ChannelAdapterFactory {
  readonly origin = 'OFICIAL' as const;
  constructor(private readonly sent: TextMessage[]) { super(); }
  locallyConfigured() { return true; }
  create() { return new OpaqueRecipientChannel(this.sent); }
}

class MutatingMetaFactory extends ChannelAdapterFactory {
  readonly origin = 'OFICIAL' as const;
  private readonly delegate = new MetaChannelFactory(metaFake);
  constructor(private readonly mutateBeforeFence: () => Promise<void>) { super(); }
  locallyConfigured() { return this.delegate.locallyConfigured(); }
  create(credential: ResolvedChannelCredential, beforeDispatch: () => Promise<boolean>) {
    return this.delegate.create(credential, async () => {
      await this.mutateBeforeFence();
      return beforeDispatch();
    });
  }
}

class MutatingEvolutionFactory extends ChannelAdapterFactory {
  readonly origin = 'NAO_OFICIAL' as const;
  private readonly delegate = new EvolutionChannelFactory(evolutionFake);
  constructor(private readonly mutateBeforeFence: () => Promise<void>) { super(); }
  locallyConfigured() { return this.delegate.locallyConfigured(); }
  create(credential: ResolvedChannelCredential, beforeDispatch: () => Promise<boolean>) {
    return this.delegate.create(credential, async () => {
      await this.mutateBeforeFence();
      return beforeDispatch();
    });
  }
}

describe('common Channel outbound (PostgreSQL)', () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  let prisma: PrismaService;
  let buId: string;
  let conversationId: string;
  let identityId: string;
  let cookie: string;

  beforeAll(async () => { ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp()); });
  beforeEach(async () => {
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    const tenant = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Channel outbound fixture', 'channel-outbound@example.test');
    buId = tenant.businessUnit.id;
    const binding = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, buId, 'OFICIAL', '987654321', { requiresWindow: false });
    identityId = binding.identity.id;
    const conversation = await prisma.conversation.create({ data: {
      buId, contact: 'user:opaque', contactType: 'PESSOA', displayName: 'Opaque recipient', status: 'QUEUED',
    } });
    conversationId = conversation.id;
    cookie = await login(app, 'channel-outbound@example.test');
  });
  afterAll(async () => { if (prisma) await limparBanco(prisma); await app?.close(); });

  it('sends a trusted opaque recipient through one bound adapter and finalizes without stealing a later owner', async () => {
    const gate = await prisma.conversation.update({ where: { id: conversationId }, data: {
      status: 'IN_SERVICE', attendantName: 'Claim before send', attendantOrigin: 'INTERNO',
    } });
    const response = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversationId}/messages`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ texto: 'opaque recipient test' }).expect(201);
    expect(response.body).toMatchObject({ canal: 'OFICIAL' });
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toEqual([{ para: 'user:opaque', texto: 'opaque recipient test' }]);
    const message = await prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { conversationId, direction: 'OUT' } });
    expect(message).toMatchObject({ externalId: expect.any(String), content: 'opaque recipient test', authorUserId: expect.any(String) });
    const finalized = await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: gate.id } });
    expect(finalized).toMatchObject({ attendantName: 'Claim before send', attendantOrigin: 'INTERNO' });
  });

  it('runs an opaque user recipient through an independent Channel fake and never calls provider clients', async () => {
    const sent: TextMessage[] = [];
    const registry = new ChannelAdapterRegistry([new OpaqueRecipientFactory(sent)]);
    const isolated = await subirApp(registry);
    try {
      await limparBanco(isolated.prisma);
      const tenant = await criarMantenedora(isolated.app, 'Opaque fake contract', 'opaque-fake@example.test');
      const localBuId = tenant.businessUnit.id;
      await criarCanalVerificado(isolated.prisma, localBuId, 'OFICIAL', 'opaque-fake-resource', { requiresWindow: false });
      const localConversation = await isolated.prisma.conversation.create({ data: {
        buId: localBuId, contact: 'user:opaque', contactType: 'PESSOA', displayName: 'Opaque fake recipient', status: 'QUEUED',
      } });
      const localCookie = await login(isolated.app, 'opaque-fake@example.test');
      await request(isolated.app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${localConversation.id}/messages`)
        .set('Cookie', localCookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ texto: 'wire-independent fake' }).expect(201);
      expect(sent).toEqual([{ kind: 'text', recipientRef: 'user:opaque', text: 'wire-independent fake' }]);
      expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(0);
      expect(evolutionFake.enviados).toHaveLength(0);
      expect(await isolated.prisma.message.count({ where: { conversationId: localConversation.id, direction: 'OUT' } })).toBe(1);
    } finally {
      await limparBanco(isolated.prisma);
      await isolated.app.close();
    }
  });

  it('blocks real binding changes inside Meta/Evolution adapter pre-dispatch callbacks', async () => {
    const cases = [
      { provider: 'OFICIAL' as const, changed: 'generation' as const },
      { provider: 'OFICIAL' as const, changed: 'pairingEpoch' as const },
      { provider: 'OFICIAL' as const, changed: 'credentialVersion' as const },
      { provider: 'NAO_OFICIAL' as const, changed: 'generation' as const },
      { provider: 'NAO_OFICIAL' as const, changed: 'pairingEpoch' as const },
    ];
    for (const { provider, changed } of cases) {
      let mutate!: () => Promise<void>;
      const previousModuleSwitch = process.env.LH_CHANNEL_UNOFFICIAL;
      if (provider === 'NAO_OFICIAL') process.env.LH_CHANNEL_UNOFFICIAL = 'true';
      const registry = new ChannelAdapterRegistry([provider === 'OFICIAL'
        ? new MutatingMetaFactory(() => mutate())
        : new MutatingEvolutionFactory(() => mutate())]);
      const isolated = await subirApp(registry);
      try {
        await limparBanco(isolated.prisma);
        const tenant = await criarMantenedora(isolated.app, `Pre-dispatch ${changed}`, `pre-dispatch-${changed}@example.test`);
        if (provider === 'NAO_OFICIAL') {
          await contratarCanalNaoOficial(isolated.prisma, tenant.mantenedora.id);
        }
        const localBinding = await criarCanalVerificado(isolated.prisma, tenant.businessUnit.id, provider,
          `pre-dispatch-${provider}-${changed}`, { requiresWindow: false });
        const localConversation = await isolated.prisma.conversation.create({ data: {
          buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contact: `user:opaque-${provider}-${changed}`, contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'QUEUED',
        } });
        const localCookie = await login(isolated.app, `pre-dispatch-${changed}@example.test`);
        let changedOnce = false;
        mutate = async () => {
          if (changedOnce) return;
          changedOnce = true;
          if (changed === 'generation') {
            await isolated.prisma.buChannel.update({ where: { id: localBinding.channel.id }, data: { generation: { increment: 1 } } });
          } else if (changed === 'pairingEpoch') {
            await isolated.prisma.buChannel.update({ where: { id: localBinding.channel.id }, data: { pairingEpoch: { increment: 1 } } });
          } else {
            await isolated.prisma.channelCredential.update({ where: { channelId: localBinding.channel.id }, data: { version: { increment: 1 } } });
          }
        };
        const metaCallsBefore = metaFake.providerCalls;
        const evolutionCallsBefore = evolutionFake.providerCalls;
        await request(isolated.app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${localConversation.id}/messages`)
          .set('Cookie', localCookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ texto: `blocked ${changed}` })
          .expect(409).expect(({ body }) => expect(body).toMatchObject({ code: 'CHANNEL_CONTEXT_CHANGED', outcome: 'BLOCKED' }));
        expect(await isolated.prisma.message.count({ where: { conversationId: localConversation.id, direction: 'OUT' } })).toBe(0);
        expect(metaFake.providerCalls).toBe(metaCallsBefore);
        expect(evolutionFake.providerCalls).toBe(evolutionCallsBefore);
      } finally {
        await limparBanco(isolated.prisma);
        await isolated.app.close();
        if (previousModuleSwitch === undefined) delete process.env.LH_CHANNEL_UNOFFICIAL;
        else process.env.LH_CHANNEL_UNOFFICIAL = previousModuleSwitch;
      }
    }
  });

  it('persists nothing for contradictory successful Meta envelopes', async () => {
    process.env.GRAPH_VERSION = 'v26.0';
    const fetchFake = vi.fn()
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => ({ success: false, messages: [{ id: 'contradictory' }] }) })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => ({ success: false, messages: [{}] }) });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchFake);
    const isolated = await subirApp(new ChannelAdapterRegistry([new MetaChannelFactory(new MetaHttpClient())]));
    try {
      await limparBanco(isolated.prisma);
      const tenant = await criarMantenedora(isolated.app, 'Contradictory Meta ACK', 'contradictory-meta@example.test');
      await criarCanalVerificado(isolated.prisma, tenant.businessUnit.id, 'OFICIAL', 'contradictory-meta-resource', { requiresWindow: false });
      const conversation = await isolated.prisma.conversation.create({ data: {
        buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contact: 'user:opaque', contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'QUEUED',
      } });
      const session = await login(isolated.app, 'contradictory-meta@example.test');
      for (let requestIndex = 1; requestIndex <= 2; requestIndex += 1) {
        await request(isolated.app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversation.id}/messages`)
          .set('Cookie', session).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ texto: `contradictory result ${requestIndex}` })
          .expect(400).expect(({ body }) => expect(body).toMatchObject({
            outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED',
          }));
        expect((await request(isolated.app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversation.id}/messages`)
          .set('Cookie', session)).body.messages.filter((message: { direction: string }) => message.direction === 'OUT')).toHaveLength(0);
        expect(fetchFake).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(requestIndex);
        expect(await isolated.prisma.message.count({ where: { conversationId: conversation.id, direction: 'OUT' } })).toBe(0);
      }
    } finally {
      vi.unstubAllGlobals();
      await limparBanco(isolated.prisma);
      await isolated.app.close();
    }
  });

  it('requires BFF session and CSRF and rejects a foreign-account public send before dispatch', async () => {
    const sentBefore = metaFake.enviados.length;
    const outgoingBefore = await prisma.message.count({ where: { conversationId, direction: 'OUT' } });
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversationId}/messages`)
      .send({ texto: 'without session' }).expect(401);
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversationId}/messages`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).send({ texto: 'without CSRF header' }).expect(403);

    const foreign = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Foreign public destination', 'foreign-public-destination@example.test');
    const foreignCookie = await login(app, 'foreign-public-destination@example.test');
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversationId}/messages`)
      .set('Cookie', foreignCookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ texto: 'valid session without BU grant' }).expect(404);
    const key = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/api/bff/integracao/api-keys').set('Cookie', cookie)
      .set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ nome: 'Scoped channel key', buIds: [buId] }).expect(201);
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/api/v1/messages').set('Authorization', `Bearer ${key.body.chave}`)
      .send({ account_id: foreign.businessUnit.id, telefone: 'user:opaque', texto: 'foreign API key send' })
      .expect(403).expect(({ body }) => expect(body.code).toBe('SEM_ACESSO_A_CONTA'));
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(sentBefore);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { conversationId, direction: 'OUT' } })).toBe(outgoingBefore);
    expect(evolutionFake.enviados).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('holds two tenants in flight with distinct scoped resources and credentials', async () => {
    const tenantB = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Parallel tenant B', 'parallel-tenant-b@example.test');
    const channelA = await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId, type: 'OFICIAL' } });
    const bindingA = {
      channel: channelA,
      identity: await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { currentChannelId: channelA.id } }),
    };
    const bindingB = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, tenantB.businessUnit.id, 'OFICIAL', 'parallel-resource-b', { requiresWindow: false });
    const credentialA = await prisma.channelCredential.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { channelId: bindingA.channel.id } });
    const credentialB = await prisma.channelCredential.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { channelId: bindingB.channel.id } });
    const key = Buffer.from(process.env.CHANNEL_CREDENTIAL_KEY!, 'base64');
    const tokenA = 'tenant-a-distinct-scoped-token';
    const tokenB = 'tenant-b-distinct-scoped-token';
    for (const [binding, credential, token] of [[bindingA, credentialA, tokenA], [bindingB, credentialB, tokenB]] as const) {
      const envelope = sealChannelCredential(token, credential.keyId, key, {
        purpose: 'active', provider: 'OFICIAL', buId: binding.channel.buId,
        channelId: binding.channel.id, generation: binding.channel.generation, version: credential.version,
      });
      await prisma.channelCredential.update({ where: { channelId: binding.channel.id }, data: {
        nonce: new Uint8Array(envelope.nonce), ciphertext: new Uint8Array(envelope.ciphertext), tag: new Uint8Array(envelope.tag),
      } });
      metaFake.credentialByPhone.set(binding.channel.phoneNumberId!, token);
    }
    const conversationA = await prisma.conversation.create({ data: {
      buId, contact: 'user:tenant-a', contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'QUEUED',
    } });
    const conversationB = await prisma.conversation.create({ data: {
      buId: tenantB.businessUnit.id, contact: 'user:tenant-b', contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'QUEUED',
    } });
    const cookieB = await login(app, 'parallel-tenant-b@example.test');
    let arrived = 0;
    let markBothEntered!: () => void;
    let releaseBoth!: () => void;
    const bothEntered = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { markBothEntered = resolve; });
    const held = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { releaseBoth = resolve; });
    metaFake.sendHook = async () => { arrived += 1; if (arrived === 2) markBothEntered(); await held; };
    const pendingA = request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversationA.id}/messages`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ texto: 'tenant A parallel' }).then((response) => response);
    const pendingB = request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversationB.id}/messages`)
      .set('Cookie', cookieB).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ texto: 'tenant B parallel' }).then((response) => response);
    await bothEntered;
    expect(metaFake.credentialCalls.map(({ phoneNumberId, accessToken }) => ({ phoneNumberId, accessToken })).sort((a, b) => a.phoneNumberId.localeCompare(b.phoneNumberId)))
      .toEqual([
        { phoneNumberId: channelA.phoneNumberId!, accessToken: tokenA },
        { phoneNumberId: 'parallel-resource-b', accessToken: tokenB },
      ]);
    releaseBoth();
    const [responseA, responseB] = await Promise.all([pendingA, pendingB]);
    expect(responseA.status).toBe(201);
    expect(responseB.status).toBe(201);
    const [messageA, messageB] = await Promise.all([
      prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { conversationId: conversationA.id, direction: 'OUT' } }),
      prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { conversationId: conversationB.id, direction: 'OUT' } }),
    ]);
    expect(messageA).toMatchObject({ buId, providerResourceIdentityId: bindingA.identity.id, channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL', content: 'tenant A parallel' });
    expect(messageB).toMatchObject({ buId: tenantB.businessUnit.id, providerResourceIdentityId: bindingB.identity.id, channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL', content: 'tenant B parallel' });
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([
      { para: 'user:tenant-a', texto: 'tenant A parallel' },
      { para: 'user:tenant-b', texto: 'tenant B parallel' },
    ]));
  });

  it('reports ambiguous bindings and blocks before the provider call', async () => {
    await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, buId, 'NAO_OFICIAL', `outbound-evolution-${Date.now()}`, { requiresWindow: false });
    const thread = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversationId}/messages`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).expect(200);
    expect(thread.body.janela).toMatchObject({ disponivel: false, motivo: 'CANAL_AMBIGUO', canal: null });
    const before = metaFake.enviados.length;
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversationId}/messages`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ texto: 'must not send' })
      .expect(409).expect(({ body }) => expect(body).toMatchObject({ code: 'CANAL_AMBIGUO', outcome: 'BLOCKED' }));
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(before);
  });

  it('preserves a BFF claim acquired after the provider accepted an in-flight send', async () => {
    let markEntered!: () => void;
    let releaseSend!: () => void;
    const entered = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { markEntered = resolve; });
    const held = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { releaseSend = resolve; });
    metaFake.sendHook = async () => { markEntered(); await held; };
    const pending = request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversationId}/messages`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ texto: 'claim during BFF send' }).then((response) => response);
    await entered;
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversationId}/claim`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').expect(201);
    releaseSend();
    const response = await pending;
    expect(response.status).toBe(201);
    const claimed = await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversationId } });
    expect(claimed).toMatchObject({ status: 'IN_SERVICE', attendantOrigin: 'INTERNO', attendantUserId: expect.any(String) });
  });

  it('gives a detected identity change precedence after dispatch and creates no new Message', async () => {
    metaFake.sendHook = async () => {
      await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.update({ where: { id: identityId }, data: { currentChannelId: null, retiredAt: new Date() } });
    };
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversationId}/messages`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ texto: 'dispatch raced retirement' })
      .expect(409).expect(({ body }) => expect(body).toMatchObject({ code: 'CHANNEL_CONTEXT_CHANGED', outcome: 'ACCEPTED_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE', localMessageState: 'UNCONFIRMED' }));
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { conversationId, direction: 'OUT' } })).toBe(0);
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('does not record the null-ID diagnostic when a PostgreSQL identity fence changes during dispatch', async () => {
    metaFake.sendIdNull = true;
    metaFake.sendHook = async () => {
      await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.update({ where: { id: identityId }, data: { currentChannelId: null, retiredAt: new Date() } });
    };
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversationId}/messages`)

      .expect(409).expect(({ body }) => expect(body).toMatchObject({
        code: 'CHANNEL_CONTEXT_CHANGED', outcome: 'UNKNOWN', diagnosticPersistence: 'UNCONFIRMED',
      }));
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { conversationId, direction: 'OUT' } })).toBe(0);
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('fences a held send after a real same-resource REAUTORIZAR generation change', async () => {
    let markEntered!: () => void;
    let releaseSend!: () => void;
    const entered = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { markEntered = resolve; });
    const held = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { releaseSend = resolve; });
    metaFake.sendHook = async () => { markEntered(); await held; };
    const pending = request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversationId}/messages`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ texto: 'held across reauthorization' }).then((response) => response);
    await entered;

    const created = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/api/bff/number-registrations')
      .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').set('Idempotency-Key', '0f0f0f0f-0f0f-4f0f-8f0f-0f0f0f0f0f0f')
      .send({ buId, tipo: 'OFICIAL', operation: 'REAUTORIZAR', channelId: (await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId, type: 'OFICIAL' } })).id, expectedGeneration: 1 })
      .expect(201);
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/number-registrations/${created.body.attempt.id}/authorize`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ nonce: created.body.nonce, code: 'fixture-held-send-reauthorize' }).expect(200);
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/number-registrations/${created.body.attempt.id}/confirm`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste')
      .send({ wabaId: '123456789', phoneNumberId: '987654321', modoRegistro: 'JA_REGISTRADO', after: 'fixture-page-1' }).expect(200);
    releaseSend();

    const response = await pending;
    expect(response.status).toBe(409);
    expect(response.body).toMatchObject({ code: 'CHANNEL_CONTEXT_CHANGED', outcome: 'ACCEPTED_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE', localMessageState: 'UNCONFIRMED' });
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { conversationId, direction: 'OUT' } })).toBe(0);
    expect(await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { id: (await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId, type: 'OFICIAL' } })).id } }))
      .toMatchObject({ generation: 2 });
  });

  it('accepts a held acknowledged send when only the active credential version rotates', async () => {
    let markEntered!: () => void;
    let releaseSend!: () => void;
    const entered = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { markEntered = resolve; });
    const held = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { releaseSend = resolve; });
    metaFake.sendHook = async () => { markEntered(); await held; };
    const pending = request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversationId}/messages`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ texto: 'held across credential rotation' }).then((response) => response);
    await entered;

    const channel = await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId, type: 'OFICIAL' } });
    const prior = await prisma.channelCredential.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { channelId: channel.id } });
    const key = Buffer.from(process.env.CHANNEL_CREDENTIAL_KEY!, 'base64');
    const nextVersion = prior.version + 1;
    const envelope = sealChannelCredential('fixture-scoped-token', prior.keyId, key, {
      purpose: 'active', provider: 'OFICIAL', buId, channelId: channel.id, generation: channel.generation, version: nextVersion,
    });
    await prisma.channelCredential.update({ where: { channelId: channel.id }, data: {
      version: nextVersion, nonce: new Uint8Array(envelope.nonce), ciphertext: new Uint8Array(envelope.ciphertext), tag: new Uint8Array(envelope.tag),
    } });
    releaseSend();

    const response = await pending;
    expect(response.status).toBe(201);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { conversationId, direction: 'OUT', externalId: 'wamid-enviado-1' } })).toBe(1);
    expect(await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } })).toMatchObject({ generation: channel.generation, pairingEpoch: channel.pairingEpoch });
    expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: identityId } })).toMatchObject({ currentChannelId: channel.id, buId });
  });

  it('keeps verified bot ingress pinned when a different channel origin becomes the latest message', async () => {
    const evolution = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, buId, 'NAO_OFICIAL', `outbound-evolution-${Date.now()}`, { requiresWindow: false });
    await prisma.message.create({ data: {
      id: 'latest-other-origin', conversationId, buId, direction: 'IN', messageType: 'TEXTO', content: 'other origin arrived later',
      channelOrigin: 'NAO_OFICIAL', providerResourceIdentityId: evolution.identity.id, externalId: 'fixture-other-origin', status: 'RECEBIDA',
    } });
    const channel = await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId, type: 'OFICIAL' } });
    const outbound = app.get(OutboundService);
    await outbound.send({
      conversationId, buId, text: 'pinned bot reply', attribution: { kind: 'BOT', name: 'Typebot' },
      ingress: {
        channelId: channel.id, providerResourceIdentityId: identityId, channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL',
        generation: channel.generation, pairingEpoch: channel.pairingEpoch,
      },
    });
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toEqual([{ para: 'user:opaque', texto: 'pinned bot reply' }]);
    expect(evolutionFake.enviados).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('breaks equal-timestamp origin ties deterministically by descending message id', async () => {
    await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, buId, 'NAO_OFICIAL', `outbound-tie-evolution-${Date.now()}`, { requiresWindow: false });
    const sameTime = new Date('2026-10-20T12:00:00.000Z');
    await prisma.message.createMany({ data: [
      { id: 'channel-tie-a', conversationId, buId, direction: 'IN', messageType: 'TEXTO', content: 'official tie', channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL', status: 'RECEBIDA', createdAt: sameTime },
      { id: 'channel-tie-z', conversationId, buId, direction: 'IN', messageType: 'TEXTO', content: 'evolution tie', channelOrigin: 'NAO_OFICIAL', status: 'RECEBIDA', createdAt: sameTime },
    ] });
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversationId}/messages`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ texto: 'tie winner' }).expect(201);
    expect(evolutionFake.enviados).toEqual([{ para: 'user:opaque', texto: 'tie winner' }]);
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('rejects cross-BU and mismatched verified-ingress bindings before dispatch', async () => {
    const setor = await prisma.setor.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { mantenedoraId: (await prisma.businessUnit.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: buId } })).mantenedoraId } });
    const foreign = await prisma.businessUnit.create({
      data: { mantenedoraId: setor.mantenedoraId, setorId: setor.id, name: 'Foreign channel BU' },
    });
    const foreignChannel = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, foreign.id, 'OFICIAL', 'foreign-resource-transport', { requiresWindow: false });
    const outbound = app.get(OutboundService);
    await expect(outbound.send({
      conversationId, buId, text: 'wrong BU', attribution: { kind: 'BOT', name: 'Typebot' },
      ingress: {
        channelId: foreignChannel.channel.id, providerResourceIdentityId: foreignChannel.identity.id, channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL',
        generation: foreignChannel.channel.generation, pairingEpoch: foreignChannel.channel.pairingEpoch,
      },
    })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 409, response: { code: 'CHANNEL_CONTEXT_CHANGED', outcome: 'BLOCKED' } });
    await expect(outbound.send({
      conversationId, buId, text: 'wrong identity', attribution: { kind: 'BOT', name: 'Typebot' },
      ingress: {
        channelId: (await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId, type: 'OFICIAL' } })).id,
        providerResourceIdentityId: foreignChannel.identity.id, channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL', generation: 1, pairingEpoch: 0,
      },
    })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 409, response: { code: 'CHANNEL_CONTEXT_CHANGED', outcome: 'BLOCKED' } });
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('preserves a human BFF claim that overlaps a public API accepted send', async () => {
    const phone = '5584999912345';
    const conversation = await prisma.conversation.create({ data: { buId, contact: phone, contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'QUEUED' } });
    await prisma.message.create({ data: {
      conversationId: conversation.id, buId, direction: 'IN', messageType: 'TEXTO', content: 'public inbound', channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL',
      status: 'RECEBIDA',
    } });
    const key = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/api/bff/integracao/api-keys').set('Cookie', cookie)
      .set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ nome: 'Channel overlap key', buIds: [buId] }).expect(201);
    metaFake.sendHook = async () => {
      await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversation.id}/claim`)
        .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').expect(201);
    };
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/api/v1/messages').set('Authorization', `Bearer ${key.body.chave}`)
      .send({ account_id: buId, telefone: phone, texto: 'public outbound during claim' }).expect(201);
    const claimed = await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversation.id } });
    expect(claimed).toMatchObject({ status: 'IN_SERVICE', attendantUserId: expect.any(String), attendantOrigin: 'INTERNO' });
    expect(claimed.attendantName).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('completes five real-PostgreSQL rounds of twenty overlapping sends with one accepted Message each', async () => {
    const operationsPerRound = 20;
    const operationCounts: number[] = [];
    const acceptedCounts: number[] = [];
    const recordedCounts: number[] = [];
    const perOwnedConversationRowCounts: number[][] = [];
    for (let round = 0; round < 5; round += 1) {
      const conversations = await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: operationsPerRound }, (_, index) => prisma.conversation.create({ data: {
        buId, contact: `user:opaque-${round}-${index}`, contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'QUEUED',
      } })));
      let arrivals = 0;
      let markBarrier!: () => void;
      let release!: () => void;
      const barrier = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { markBarrier = resolve; });
      const gate = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { release = resolve; });
      metaFake.sendHook = async () => {
        arrivals += 1;
        if (arrivals === operationsPerRound) markBarrier();
        await gate;
      };
      const pending = conversations.map((conversation, index) => request(app.getHttpServer())
        .post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversation.id}/messages`).set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste')
        .send({ texto: `round ${round} outbound ${index}` }).then((response) => response));
      await barrier;
      release();
      const responses = await Promise.all(pending);
      metaFake.sendHook = null;
      const accepted = responses.filter((response) => response.status === 201).length;
      const recorded = await prisma.message.count({ where: { conversationId: { in: conversations.map(({ id }) => id) }, direction: 'OUT' } });
      const groupedRows = await prisma.message.groupBy({
        by: ['conversationId'],
        where: { conversationId: { in: conversations.map(({ id }) => id) }, direction: 'OUT' },
        _count: { _all: true },
      });
      const rowCountByConversation = new Map(groupedRows.map((row) => [row.conversationId, row._count._all]));
      const ownedConversationRowCounts = conversations.map(({ id }) => rowCountByConversation.get(id) ?? 0);
      operationCounts.push(responses.length);
      acceptedCounts.push(accepted);
      recordedCounts.push(recorded);
      perOwnedConversationRowCounts.push(ownedConversationRowCounts);
      expect(accepted).toBe(operationsPerRound);
      expect(recorded).toBe(operationsPerRound);
      expect(ownedConversationRowCounts).toEqual(Array(operationsPerRound).fill(1));
    }
    console.log(`[CHANNEL-OUTBOUND-OVERLAP]${JSON.stringify({ repetitions: 5, operationsPerRepetition: operationsPerRound, operationCounts, acceptedCounts, recordedCounts, perOwnedConversationRowCounts, limits: 'Disposable PostgreSQL and provider fake; each distinct owned conversation has exactly one OUT row in this bounded fixture; no same-key exactly-once, capacity, or SLO claim.' })}`);
  });
});

```

## api-app/test/helpers.ts

```text
import { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import { Test } from '@nestjs/testing';
import type { NestExpressApplication } from '@nestjs/platform-express';
import request from 'supertest';
import { AppModule } from '../src/app.module';
import { ChannelAdapterRegistry } from '../src/integrations/channels/channel-adapter-registry';
import { configurarBodyParsers } from '../src/infra/http/body-parsers';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { EVOLUTION_CLIENT, EvolutionClient } from '../src/integrations/evolution/evolution.client';
import { META_CLIENT, MetaClient, MetaIndisponivelError } from '../src/integrations/meta/meta.client';
import { TYPEBOT_CLIENT, TypebotClient } from '../src/integrations/typebot/typebot.client';
import { sealChannelCredential } from '../src/domain/channels/channel-credential-crypto';

/** Dublês das portas de saída: o teste prova permissão, não a Meta. */
export const metaFake: MetaClient & { wabaRecusada: string | null; wabaRecursos: { wabaId: string; phoneNumberId: string; telefone: string }[]; enviados: { para: string; texto: string }[]; credentialByPhone: Map<string, string>; credentialCalls: { phoneNumberId: string; accessToken: string }[]; registrosNumero: number; subscriptionHook: (() => Promise<void>) | null; confirmHook: (() => Promise<void>) | null; registrosHook: (() => Promise<void>) | null; sendHook: ((id: string, para: string, texto: string) => Promise<void>) | null; confirmCalls: number; exchangeCalls: number; exchangeFails: number; providerCalls: number; sendIdNull: boolean } = {
  wabaRecusada: null,
  wabaRecursos: [
    { wabaId: '123456789', phoneNumberId: '111111111', telefone: '5584998880000' },
    { wabaId: '123456789', phoneNumberId: '987654321', telefone: '5584999990000' },
    { wabaId: '555555555', phoneNumberId: '222222222', telefone: '5584997770000' },
  ],
  enviados: [],
  credentialByPhone: new Map(),
  credentialCalls: [],
  registrosNumero: 0,
  subscriptionHook: null,
  confirmHook: null,
  registrosHook: null,
  sendHook: null,
  confirmCalls: 0,
  exchangeCalls: 0,
  exchangeFails: 0,
  providerCalls: 0,
  sendIdNull: false,
  capabilities: () => ({ appId: 'fixture-app', configId: 'fixture-config', graphVersion: 'v26.0', available: true, reason: 'AVAILABLE' }),
  async exchangeCode() {
    metaFake.providerCalls += 1; metaFake.exchangeCalls += 1;
    if (metaFake.exchangeFails > 0) { metaFake.exchangeFails -= 1; throw new Error('fixture exchange response lost'); }
    return { accessToken: 'fixture-scoped-token' };
  },
  async recursos(accessToken, wabaId, after) {
    metaFake.providerCalls += 1;
    if (accessToken !== 'fixture-scoped-token') throw new Error('scoped credential required');
    if (wabaId && metaFake.wabaRecusada === wabaId) throw new Error('WABA recusada');
    const targets = metaFake.wabaRecursos.filter((r) => !wabaId || r.wabaId === wabaId);
    const page = after ? targets.slice(1) : targets.slice(0, 1);
    return { wabas: wabaId ? [{ id: wabaId, name: 'Fixture' }] : [...new Map(targets.map((r) => [r.wabaId, { id: r.wabaId, name: 'Fixture' }])).values()], numbers: page.map((r) => ({ id: r.phoneNumberId, telefone: r.telefone, nomeVerificado: 'Fixture' })), next: !after && targets.length > 1 ? 'fixture-page-1' : null };
  },
  async enviarTexto(phoneNumberId: string, para: string, texto: string, accessToken?: string) {
    metaFake.providerCalls += 1;
    metaFake.credentialCalls.push({ phoneNumberId, accessToken: accessToken ?? '' });
    const expected = metaFake.credentialByPhone.get(phoneNumberId) ?? 'fixture-scoped-token';
    if (accessToken !== expected) throw new Error('scoped credential required');
    metaFake.enviados.push({ para, texto });
    const id = `wamid-enviado-${metaFake.enviados.length}`;
    await metaFake.sendHook?.(id, para, texto);
    return metaFake.sendIdNull
      ? { outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'ACK_WITHOUT_ID', legacyCode: 'META_RECUSOU' }
      : { outcome: 'ACCEPTED', externalMessageId: id };
  },
  configurado: () => true,
  canSendText: () => true,
  async numerosDaWaba(wabaId: string, accessToken?: string, after?: string) {
    metaFake.providerCalls += 1;
    if (metaFake.wabaRecusada === wabaId || accessToken !== 'fixture-scoped-token') throw new Error('WABA recusada');
    const targets = metaFake.wabaRecursos.filter((r) => r.wabaId === wabaId); const page = after ? targets.slice(1) : targets.slice(0, 1);
    return { data: page.map((r) => ({ id: r.phoneNumberId, telefone: r.telefone, nomeVerificado: `WABA ${wabaId}` })), next: !after && targets.length > 1 ? 'fixture-page-1' : null };
  },
  async confirmarNumero(wabaId, phoneNumberId, accessToken, after) {
    metaFake.providerCalls += 1;
    metaFake.confirmCalls += 1;
    await metaFake.confirmHook?.();
    const page = await metaFake.numerosDaWaba(wabaId, accessToken, after);
    return page.data.find((item) => item.id === phoneNumberId) ?? null;
  },
  async verificarNumeroVinculado(wabaId, phoneNumberId, accessToken) {
    metaFake.confirmCalls += 1;
    await metaFake.confirmHook?.();
    const seen = new Set<string>(); let after: string | undefined;
    for (let index = 0; index < 2; index += 1) {
      const page = await metaFake.numerosDaWaba(wabaId, accessToken, after);
      const number = page.data.find((item) => item.id === phoneNumberId);
      if (number) return number;
      if (!page.next) return null;
      if (page.next.length > 512 || seen.has(page.next) || index === 1) throw new MetaIndisponivelError('A verificação do número está inconclusiva.');
      seen.add(page.next); after = page.next;
    }
    throw new MetaIndisponivelError('A verificação do número está inconclusiva.');
  },
  async registrarNumero(_phoneNumberId, pin, accessToken) { metaFake.providerCalls += 1; if (accessToken !== 'fixture-scoped-token' || !/^\d{6}$/.test(pin)) throw new Error('scoped registration credential required'); metaFake.registrosNumero += 1; await metaFake.registrosHook?.(); },
  async inscreverWaba(_wabaId, accessToken) { metaFake.providerCalls += 1; if (accessToken !== 'fixture-scoped-token') throw new Error('scoped subscription credential required'); await metaFake.subscriptionHook?.(); },
};

export const evolutionFake: EvolutionClient & { enviados: { para: string; texto: string }[]; inventory: Map<string, { name: string; ownerJid: string | null; connectionStatus: string | null }>; createCalls: number; providerCalls: number; sendIdNull: boolean; sendHook: ((id: string, para: string, texto: string) => Promise<void>) | null; createHook: ((name: string) => Promise<void>) | null; ownerPhone: string | null; createdState: string; deleteFails: boolean } = {
  enviados: [], inventory: new Map(), createCalls: 0, providerCalls: 0,
  sendHook: null,
  createHook: null,
  configurado: () => true,
  async enviarTexto(_instancia: string, para: string, texto: string) {
    evolutionFake.providerCalls += 1;
    evolutionFake.enviados.push({ para, texto });
    const id = `evo-enviado-${evolutionFake.enviados.length}`;
    await evolutionFake.sendHook?.(id, para, texto);
    return evolutionFake.sendIdNull
      ? { outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'ACK_WITHOUT_ID', legacyCode: 'EVOLUTION_RECUSOU' }
      : { outcome: 'ACCEPTED', externalMessageId: id };
  },
  async criarInstancia(nome) {
    evolutionFake.providerCalls += 1;
    evolutionFake.createCalls += 1;
    evolutionFake.inventory.set(nome, { name: nome, ownerJid: evolutionFake.ownerPhone ? `${evolutionFake.ownerPhone}@s.whatsapp.net` : null, connectionStatus: evolutionFake.createdState });
    await evolutionFake.createHook?.(nome);
    return { qrcode: 'data:image/png;base64,QR' };
  },
  async qrDaInstancia() {
    evolutionFake.providerCalls += 1;
    return 'data:image/png;base64,QR';
  },
  async estadoDaInstancia() {
    evolutionFake.providerCalls += 1;
    return 'open';
  },
  async consultarInstancia(nome) { evolutionFake.providerCalls += 1; return evolutionFake.inventory.get(nome) ?? null; },
  async apagarInstancia(nome) { evolutionFake.providerCalls += 1; if (evolutionFake.deleteFails) throw new Error('fixture cleanup timeout'); evolutionFake.inventory.delete(nome); },
};

/**
 * Liga o canal não oficial nos DOIS níveis: o teto da instalação (env, lido no
 * boot do serviço) e o contrato da Mantenedora (banco) — ADR-0016.
 *
 * Precisa vir ANTES de `subirApp`, porque o teto é lido uma vez na construção.
 * É o mesmo que acontece em produção: mudar módulo de instalação é deploy.
 */
export function tetoComCanalNaoOficial(): void {
  process.env.LH_CHANNEL_UNOFFICIAL = 'true';
}

export async function contratarCanalNaoOficial(prisma: PrismaService, mantenedoraId: string): Promise<void> {
  await prisma.mantenedoraSettings.upsert({
    where: { mantenedoraId },
    create: { mantenedoraId, modulos: { canalNaoOficial: true } },
    update: { modulos: { canalNaoOficial: true } },
  });
}

export const TOKEN_INTERNO = 'token-interno-de-teste';
export const META_APP_SECRET = 'segredo-do-app-meta';
export const EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET = 'fixture-evolution-secret-header';
export const typebotFake: TypebotClient & { enabled: boolean; starts: number; continues: number; startedBots: Array<{ botId: string; contact: string; text: string }>; beforeReply: (() => Promise<void>) | null } = {
  enabled: false, starts: 0, continues: 0, startedBots: [], beforeReply: null,
  configurado() { return typebotFake.enabled; }, async listarBots() { return []; }, editorUrl(id) { return `https://typebot.invalid/${id}`; }, workspaceUrl() { return 'https://typebot.invalid'; },
  async iniciar(botId, contact, text) { typebotFake.starts += 1; typebotFake.startedBots.push({ botId, contact, text }); await typebotFake.beforeReply?.(); return { sessionId: `fixture-typebot-${typebotFake.starts}`, messages: [{ type: 'text', content: { text: 'resposta do bot' } }] }; },
  async continuar() { typebotFake.continues += 1; return { messages: [] }; },
};

export async function subirApp(registryOverride?: ChannelAdapterRegistry, captureQueries = false): Promise<{ app: INestApplication; prisma: PrismaService }> {
  process.env.SESSION_SECRET ||= 'segredo-de-teste';
  process.env.INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN = TOKEN_INTERNO;
  process.env.META_APP_ID = 'fixture-app';
  process.env.META_APP_SECRET = META_APP_SECRET;
  process.env.META_SIGNUP_CONFIG_ID = 'fixture-config';
  process.env.GRAPH_VERSION = 'v26.0';
  process.env.EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET = EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET;
  process.env.EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_URL = 'https://api.example.test/webhooks/evolution';
  process.env.WEBHOOK_VERIFY_TOKEN = 'verify-token-de-teste';
  process.env.CHANNEL_CREDENTIAL_KEY_ID = 'fixture-key-v1';
  process.env.CHANNEL_CREDENTIAL_KEY = Buffer.alloc(32, 17).toString('base64');

  const testingModule = Test.createTestingModule({ imports: [AppModule] })
    .overrideProvider(META_CLIENT)
    .useValue(metaFake)
    .overrideProvider(EVOLUTION_CLIENT)
    .useValue(evolutionFake)
    .overrideProvider(TYPEBOT_CLIENT)
    .useValue(typebotFake);
  if (captureQueries) {
    testingModule.overrideProvider(PrismaService).useValue(new PrismaService({ log: [{ emit: 'event', level: 'query' }] }));
  }
  if (registryOverride) testingModule.overrideProvider(ChannelAdapterRegistry).useValue(registryOverride);
  const moduleRef = await testingModule.compile();
  // A MESMA fiação de produção: sem isto o corpo bruto não existe e o teste de
  // assinatura passaria a provar outra coisa.
  const app = moduleRef.createNestApplication({ bodyParser: false });
  configurarBodyParsers(app as unknown as NestExpressApplication);
  await app.init();
  return { app, prisma: app.get(PrismaService) };
}

/** Limpa na ordem das dependências — o banco de teste é real (ADR-0012 §4). */
/** Cria um usuário com papel escolhido numa BU — para o caso "papel insuficiente". */
export async function criarUsuario(
  prisma: PrismaService,
  mantenedoraId: string,
  buId: string,
  email: string,
  role: 'OWNER' | 'ADMIN' | 'ATENDENTE',
  senha = 'senha-de-teste-1',
): Promise<string> {
  const bcrypt = await import('bcryptjs');
  const user = await prisma.appUser.create({
    data: {
      mantenedoraId,
      name: email,
      email,
      passwordHash: await bcrypt.default.hash(senha, 10),
    },
  });
  await prisma.userBuRole.create({ data: { userId: user.id, buId, role, grantedViaTipo: 'DIRETO' } });
  return user.id;
}

export async function limparBanco(prisma: PrismaService): Promise<void> {
  evolutionFake.inventory.clear();
  evolutionFake.createCalls = 0; evolutionFake.providerCalls = 0; evolutionFake.createHook = null;
  metaFake.enviados.length = 0; metaFake.sendIdNull = false; metaFake.sendHook = null; evolutionFake.enviados.length = 0; evolutionFake.sendIdNull = false; evolutionFake.sendHook = null; metaFake.confirmCalls = 0; metaFake.exchangeCalls = 0; metaFake.exchangeFails = 0; metaFake.providerCalls = 0;
  metaFake.credentialByPhone.clear(); metaFake.credentialCalls.length = 0;
  metaFake.registrosNumero = 0;
  metaFake.wabaRecusada = null;
  metaFake.confirmHook = null; metaFake.registrosHook = null; metaFake.confirmCalls = 0; metaFake.subscriptionHook = null;
  typebotFake.enabled = false; typebotFake.starts = 0; typebotFake.continues = 0; typebotFake.startedBots.length = 0; typebotFake.beforeReply = null;
  evolutionFake.ownerPhone = '5584999990000'; evolutionFake.createdState = 'open'; evolutionFake.deleteFails = false;
  await prisma.webhookEvent.deleteMany();
  await prisma.message.deleteMany();
  await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.deleteMany();
  await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.deleteMany();
  await prisma.channelCredential.deleteMany();
  await prisma.conversation.deleteMany();
  await prisma.activityLog.deleteMany();
  await prisma.tokenDeAcesso.deleteMany();
  await prisma.arquivo.deleteMany();
  await prisma.setorGrant.deleteMany();
  await prisma.userBuRole.deleteMany();
  await prisma.appUser.deleteMany();
  await prisma.buChannel.deleteMany();
  await prisma.businessUnit.deleteMany();
  await prisma.setor.deleteMany();
  await prisma.mantenedora.deleteMany();
  await prisma.loginAttempt.deleteMany();
}

/** Test channel fixture with the same durable ownership and credential invariants as production. */
export async function criarCanalVerificado(
  prisma: PrismaService,
  buId: string,
  type: 'OFICIAL' | 'NAO_OFICIAL',
  externalResourceId: string,
  options: { status?: 'CONECTADO' | 'CONECTANDO' | 'DESCONECTADO'; requiresWindow?: boolean } = {},
) {
  const channel = await prisma.buChannel.create({ data: {
    buId, type, status: options.status ?? 'CONECTADO', requiresWindow: options.requiresWindow ?? type === 'OFICIAL',
    ...(type === 'OFICIAL' ? { phoneNumberId: externalResourceId } : { evolutionInstanceName: externalResourceId }),
    ownNumberVerified: true, verifiedEpoch: 0, providerConnectionState: type === 'OFICIAL' ? 'SUBSCRIBED' : 'CONNECTED',
    receiptVerifiedAt: new Date(),
  } });
  const priorIdentity = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUnique({
    where: { provider_externalResourceId: { provider: type, externalResourceId } },
  });
  if (priorIdentity && (priorIdentity.buId !== buId || priorIdentity.currentChannelId)) {
    throw new Error('Fixture resource identity is owned or active elsewhere.');
  }
  const identity = priorIdentity
    ? await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.update({ where: { id: priorIdentity.id }, data: { currentChannelId: channel.id, retiredAt: null, managedLifecycle: true, cleanupState: 'ACTIVE' } })
    : await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.create({ data: {
      provider: type, externalResourceId, buId, currentChannelId: channel.id, managedLifecycle: true, cleanupState: 'ACTIVE',
    } });
  if (type === 'OFICIAL') {
    const keyId = process.env.CHANNEL_CREDENTIAL_KEY_ID!;
    const key = Buffer.from(process.env.CHANNEL_CREDENTIAL_KEY!, 'base64');
    const envelope = sealChannelCredential('fixture-scoped-token', keyId, key, {
      purpose: 'active', provider: type, buId, channelId: channel.id, generation: channel.generation, version: 1,
    });
    await prisma.channelCredential.create({ data: {
      channelId: channel.id, provider: type, buId, generation: channel.generation, version: 1,
      keyId, nonce: new Uint8Array(envelope.nonce), ciphertext: new Uint8Array(envelope.ciphertext), tag: new Uint8Array(envelope.tag),
    } });
  }
  return { channel, identity };
}

/** Cria uma Mantenedora pela superfície interna — o caminho real de bootstrap. */
/**
 * Cria a Mantenedora **com o onboarding já concluído**.
 *
 * O bootstrap a cria PENDENTE (ADR-0016 §4), e é assim em produção. Os testes
 * que exercitam o produto vêm depois do wizard; quem exercita o portão é
 * `onboarding.e2e-spec.ts`, que usa `criarMantenedoraPendente`.
 */
export async function criarMantenedora(
  app: INestApplication,
  nome: string,
  email: string,
  senha = 'senha-de-teste-1',
) {
  const r = await criarMantenedoraPendente(app, nome, email, senha);
  const prisma = app.get(PrismaService);
  await prisma.mantenedoraSettings.update({
    where: { mantenedoraId: r.mantenedora.id },
    data: { onboardingConcluido: true },
  });
  return r;
}

export async function criarMantenedoraPendente(
  app: INestApplication,
  nome: string,
  email: string,
  senha = 'senha-de-teste-1',
) {
  const res = await request(app.getHttpServer())
    .post('/api/internal/mantenedoras')
    .set('x-internal-token', TOKEN_INTERNO)
    .set('x-requested-with', 'teste')
    .send({ nome, bu: { nome: `Número da ${nome}`, telefone: '5584999990000' }, owner: { nome: 'Dono', email, senha } })
    .expect(201);
  return res.body as {
    mantenedora: { id: string; nome: string };
    businessUnit: { id: string; nome: string };
    owner: { id: string; email: string };
  };
}

/** Faz login e devolve o cookie de sessão para reusar nas próximas chamadas. */
export async function login(app: INestApplication, email: string, senha = 'senha-de-teste-1'): Promise<string> {
  const res = await request(app.getHttpServer())
    .post('/api/bff/auth/login')
    .set('x-requested-with', 'teste')
    .send({ email, senha })
    .expect(200);
  const setCookie = res.headers['set-cookie'] as unknown as string[];
  return setCookie[0].split(';')[0];
}
```

## api-app/test/number-onboarding-message-acceptance.e2e-spec.ts

```text
import { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { MessageAcceptanceService } from '../src/domain/inbox/message-acceptance.service';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { criarMantenedora, limparBanco, subirApp } from './helpers';

describe('number onboarding keyed message acceptance (PostgreSQL)', () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  let prisma: PrismaService;
  let acceptance: MessageAcceptanceService;
  let buId: string;
  let channelId: string;
  let identityId: string;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp());
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    const tenant = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Acceptance fixture', 'acceptance-fixture@example.test');
    buId = tenant.businessUnit.id;
    const channel = await prisma.buChannel.create({
      data: { buId, type: 'OFICIAL', status: 'CONECTADO', phoneNumberId: 'phone-acceptance-fixture', ownNumberVerified: true, verifiedEpoch: 0 },
    });
    channelId = channel.id;
    const identity = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.create({
      data: { provider: 'OFICIAL', externalResourceId: 'phone-acceptance-fixture', buId, currentChannelId: channelId },
    });
    identityId = identity.id;
    await prisma.conversation.create({
      data: { buId, contact: '5584999000002', contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'IN_SERVICE' },
    });
    acceptance = new MessageAcceptanceService(prisma);
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    if (prisma) {
      await prisma.message.deleteMany({ where: { providerResourceIdentityId: identityId } });
      await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.deleteMany({ where: { id: identityId } });
      await limparBanco(prisma);
    }
    await app?.close();
  });

  it('accepts one incoming key once, creating one conversation and unread increment', async () => {
    const input = baseInput({ externalMessageId: 'wamid-in-fixture-1', direction: 'IN', content: 'hello' });
    const [a, b] = await Promise.all([acceptance.accept(input), acceptance.accept(input)]);
    expect(a.message.id).toBe(b.message.id);
    expect([a.creationWinner, b.creationWinner].filter(Boolean)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect([a.reconciled, b.reconciled].filter(Boolean)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { providerResourceIdentityId: identityId, externalId: input.externalMessageId } })).toBe(1);
    const conversation = await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: a.conversation.id } });
    expect(conversation.unreadCount).toBe(1);
  });

  it('reports the first conversation once and keeps a second immediate message in the same conversation', async () => {
    const first = await acceptance.accept(baseInput({ externalMessageId: 'wamid-in-first-conversation', contact: '5584999000003' }));
    const second = await acceptance.accept(baseInput({ externalMessageId: 'wamid-in-second-message', contact: '5584999000003' }));
    expect(first.conversationCreated).toBe(true);
    expect(first.reopened).toBe(false);
    expect(second.conversationCreated).toBe(false);
    expect(second.reopened).toBe(false);
    expect(second.conversation.id).toBe(first.conversation.id);
    expect(second.conversation.unreadCount).toBe(2);
  });

  it('echo-first and sender result reconcile to the same OUT row without changing its identity or createdAt', async () => {
    const echo = await acceptance.accept(baseInput({
      externalMessageId: 'wamid-out-fixture-1', direction: 'OUT', content: 'echo body', contact: '5584999000002',
    }));
    const createdAt = echo.message.createdAt;
    const sender = await acceptance.accept(baseInput({
      externalMessageId: 'wamid-out-fixture-1', direction: 'OUT', content: 'trusted sender body', conversationId: echo.conversation.id,
      contact: '5584999000002',
      senderResult: true, authorName: 'Support fixture',
    }));
    expect(sender.message.id).toBe(echo.message.id);
    expect(sender.message.createdAt).toEqual(createdAt);
    expect(sender.message.authorName).toBe('Support fixture');
    expect(sender.message.content).toBe('trusted sender body');
    expect(sender.creationWinner).toBe(false);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { providerResourceIdentityId: identityId, externalId: 'wamid-out-fixture-1' } })).toBe(1);
    expect(await prisma.conversation.count({ where: { id: echo.conversation.id } })).toBe(1);
    const laterEcho = await acceptance.accept(baseInput({
      externalMessageId: 'wamid-out-fixture-1', direction: 'OUT', content: 'untrusted later echo', contact: '5584999000002',
      conversationId: echo.conversation.id,
    }));
    expect(laterEcho.message.id).toBe(echo.message.id);
    expect(laterEcho.message.createdAt).toEqual(createdAt);
    expect(laterEcho.message.content).toBe('trusted sender body');
    expect(laterEcho.message.authorName).toBe('Support fixture');
  });

  it('persists redacted diagnostics only while the verified identity remains current', async () => {
    const context = {
      providerResourceIdentityId: identityId, buId, channelId, generation: 1, pairingEpoch: 0, channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL' as const,
    };
    await acceptance.persistVerifiedDiagnostic(context, 'messages.received', {
      providerId: 'safe-fixture', apikey: 'fake-apikey-fixture', nested: { access_token: 'fake-token-fixture' },
    });
    const event = await prisma.webhookEvent.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId, eventType: 'messages.received' } });
    expect(event.payload).toEqual({
      providerId: 'safe-fixture', apikey: '[REDACTED]', nested: { access_token: '[REDACTED]' },
    });
    await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.update({ where: { id: identityId }, data: { currentChannelId: null, retiredAt: new Date() } });
    await expect(acceptance.persistVerifiedDiagnostic(context, 'messages.late', { content: 'must not persist' }))
      .rejects.toMatchObject({ response: { code: 'IDENTITY_NOT_CURRENT' } });
    expect(await prisma.webhookEvent.count({ where: { buId, eventType: 'messages.late' } })).toBe(0);
    await expect(prisma.$executeRaw`UPDATE provider_resource_identities SET bu_id = 'different-owner-fixture' WHERE id = ${identityId}`)
      .rejects.toThrow();
  });

  function baseInput(overrides: Partial<Parameters<MessageAcceptanceService['accept']>[0]>) {
    return {
      providerResourceIdentityId: identityId, externalMessageId: 'fixture-id', buId, channelId,
      generation: 1, pairingEpoch: 0, contact: '5584999000001', contactType: 'PESSOA' as const,
      channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL' as const, messageType: 'TEXTO' as const, direction: 'IN' as const,
      content: 'fixture', ...overrides,
    };
  }
});
```
