```text
}

async function seedNumberOnboarding() {
  const suffix = `number-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(16).slice(2, 8)}`;
  const createTenant = async (label) => {
    const mantenedora = await prisma.mantenedora.create({ data: { name: `${label} ${suffix}` } });
    const setor = await prisma.setor.create({ data: { mantenedoraId: mantenedora.id, name: 'Geral' } });
    const bu = await prisma.businessUnit.create({ data: { mantenedoraId: mantenedora.id, setorId: setor.id, name: `${label} BU`, phone: `5511${String(Date.now()).slice(-8)}` } });
    await prisma.mantenedoraSettings.create({ data: { mantenedoraId: mantenedora.id, onboardingConcluido: true, modulos: { canalNaoOficial: true } } });
    return { mantenedora, setor, bu };
  };
  const a = await createTenant('Onboarding A'); const b = await createTenant('Onboarding B');
  const createUser = async (tenant, role, label) => {
    const email = `${label}-${suffix}@teste.local`;
    const user = await prisma.appUser.create({ data: { mantenedoraId: tenant.mantenedora.id, name: label, email, passwordHash: await bcrypt.hash('senha-onboarding-2026', 10) } });
    await prisma.userBuRole.create({ data: {
      userId: user.id, buId: tenant.bu.id, role,
      grantedViaTipo: role === 'OWNER' ? 'MANTENEDORA' : 'DIRETO',
      ...(role === 'OWNER' ? { grantedViaId: tenant.mantenedora.id } : {}),
    } });
    return { id: user.id, email, password: 'senha-onboarding-2026', role };
  };
  return {
    tenantA: { mantenedoraId: a.mantenedora.id, buId: a.bu.id, owner: await createUser(a, 'OWNER', 'owner'), admin: await createUser(a, 'ADMIN', 'admin'), operator: await createUser(a, 'ATENDENTE', 'operator') },
    tenantB: { mantenedoraId: b.mantenedora.id, buId: b.bu.id, owner: await createUser(b, 'OWNER', 'other-owner'), admin: await createUser(b, 'ADMIN', 'other-admin') },
  };
}

async function incoming(data) {
  const id = String(data.id ?? `timeline-event-${Date.now()}`);
  const payload = { entry: [{ changes: [{ field: 'messages', value: {
    metadata: { phone_number_id: data.phoneNumberId },
    contacts: [{ profile: { name: 'Cliente Timeline' } }],
    messages: [{ id, from: '5584911112222', type: 'text', text: { body: String(data.text ?? id) } }],
  } }] }] };
  const raw = JSON.stringify(payload);
  const signature = 'sha256=' + createHmac('sha256', 'segredo-do-app-meta').update(raw).digest('hex');
  const response = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${apiPort}/webhooks/meta`, {
    method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json', 'x-hub-signature-256': signature }, body: raw,
  });
  return { status: response.status, body: await response.text() };
}

const server = http.createServer(async (req, res) => {
  try {
    if (req.method === 'GET' && req.url.startsWith('/provider/resources')) {
      if (req.headers['x-fixture-provider-token'] !== 'timeline-provider-port-only') return json(res, 403, { error: 'forbidden' });
      return json(res, 200, onboarding.resources.length ? onboarding.resources : Array.from(providerInventory.values()));
    }
    if (req.method === 'GET' && req.url === '/provider/state') {
      if (req.headers['x-fixture-provider-token'] !== 'timeline-provider-port-only') return json(res, 403, { error: 'forbidden' });
      const inventory = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(onboarding.evolution.inventory).map(([name, item]) => [name, { name, ownerJid: item.ownerJid, connectionStatus: item.connectionStatus }]));
      return json(res, 200, { resources: onboarding.resources, calls: onboarding.calls, effects: onboarding.effects, evolution: { inventory, outcomes: onboarding.evolution.outcomes }, meta: { grantResourceIds: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(onboarding.meta.grants ?? {}).map(([code, grant]) => [code, grant.resources ?? []])), pages: onboarding.meta.pages, sendMode: onboarding.meta.sendMode }, failures: onboarding.failures, holds: onboarding.holds, holdsAfter: onboarding.holdsAfter, ambiguousAfter: onboarding.ambiguousAfter });
    }
    if (req.method === 'GET' && req.url === '/health') return json(res, 200, { status: 'ok' });
    const data = await bodyOf(req);
    if (req.url.startsWith('/provider/') && req.headers['x-fixture-provider-token'] !== 'timeline-provider-port-only') return json(res, 403, { error: 'forbidden' });
    if (req.method === 'POST' && req.url === '/provider/config') {
      if (data.reset === true) { onboarding.calls = []; onboarding.effects = []; onboarding.failures = {}; onboarding.holds = {}; onboarding.holdsAfter = {}; onboarding.ambiguousAfter = {}; onboarding.resources = []; onboarding.meta = { codeTokens: {}, grants: {}, pages: {}, sendMode: 'accepted' }; onboarding.evolution = { inventory: {}, outcomes: {} }; providerInventory.clear(); barriers.clear(); }
      if (Array.isArray(data.resources)) onboarding.resources = data.resources.map((r) => ({ ...r }));
      if (data.meta && typeof data.meta === 'object') Object.assign(onboarding.meta, data.meta);
      if (data.evolution && typeof data.evolution === 'object') Object.assign(onboarding.evolution, data.evolution);
      if (data.failures && typeof data.failures === 'object') onboarding.failures = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(data.failures).map(([k, v]) => [k, { remaining: Math.max(1, Number(v.remaining ?? 1)), message: String(v.message ?? 'controlled provider failure') }]));
      if (data.holds && typeof data.holds === 'object') onboarding.holds = { ...data.holds };
      if (data.holdsAfter && typeof data.holdsAfter === 'object') onboarding.holdsAfter = { ...data.holdsAfter };
      if (data.ambiguousAfter && typeof data.ambiguousAfter === 'object') onboarding.ambiguousAfter = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(data.ambiguousAfter).map(([k, v]) => [k, { remaining: Math.max(1, Number(v.remaining ?? 1)) }]));
      return json(res, 200, { configured: true });
    }
    if (req.method === 'POST' && req.url === '/provider/release') {
      const b = barriers.get(`${data.operation}:${data.key}`); if (b) b.release();
      return json(res, 200, { released: Boolean(b) });
    }
    if (req.method === 'POST' && req.url === '/provider/call') {
      const call = await providerCall(String(data.operation), data.args ?? {});
      return json(res, 200, { accepted: true, call });
    }
    if (req.method === 'POST' && req.url === '/provider/effect') return json(res, 200, { effect: await providerEffect(String(data.operation), data.args ?? {}) });
    if (req.method === 'POST' && req.url === '/provider/evolution/observe') {
      await providerCall('evolution.observe', { name: data.name });
      const name = String(data.name); const observed = onboarding.evolution.inventory[name];
      return json(res, 200, observed ? { ...observed } : null);
    }
    if (req.method === 'POST' && req.url === '/provider/evolution/create') {
      const call = await providerCall('evolution.create', { name: data.name, attemptId: data.attemptId, channelId: data.channelId, generation: data.generation, pairingEpoch: data.pairingEpoch });
      const created = onboarding.evolution.createOutcome ?? {};
      const instance = { name: String(data.name), ownerJid: data.ownerJid ?? created.ownerJid ?? null, connectionStatus: data.connectionStatus ?? created.connectionStatus ?? 'connecting', qrcode: data.qrcode ?? created.qrcode ?? 'data:image/png;base64,FAKE-QR' };
      onboarding.evolution.inventory[instance.name] = instance;
      await providerEffect('evolution.create', { name: instance.name, attemptId: data.attemptId, channelId: data.channelId, generation: data.generation, pairingEpoch: data.pairingEpoch });
      return json(res, 201, { created: true, instance, call });
    }
    if (req.method === 'POST' && req.url === '/provider/evolution/delete') {
      await providerCall('evolution.delete', { name: data.name });
      const existed = Boolean(onboarding.evolution.inventory[String(data.name)]);
      delete onboarding.evolution.inventory[String(data.name)];
      await providerEffect('evolution.delete', { name: data.name });
      return json(res, 200, { deleted: existed });
    }
    if (req.method === 'POST' && req.url === '/provider/evolution/receipt') {
      const name = String(data.name); const observed = onboarding.evolution.inventory[name];
      if (!observed) return json(res, 404, { error: 'unknown fixture instance' });
      Object.assign(observed, { ownerJid: data.ownerJid ?? observed.ownerJid, connectionStatus: data.connectionStatus ?? observed.connectionStatus });
      await providerEffect('evolution.receipt', { name });
      return json(res, 200, { updated: true });
    }
    if (req.method === 'POST' && req.url === '/provider/attempt/expire') {
      const attemptId = typeof data.attemptId === 'string' ? data.attemptId : '';
      const buId = typeof data.buId === 'string' ? data.buId : '';
      if (!/^[0-9a-f]{32}$/i.test(attemptId) || !/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/i.test(buId)) return json(res, 400, { error: 'invalid attempt scope' });
      const changed = await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.updateMany({
        where: { id: attemptId, buId, operation: 'NOVO', state: 'PENDING_AUTH', channelId: { not: null } },
        data: { expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() - 1_000) },
      });
      if (changed.count !== 1) return json(res, 404, { error: 'eligible fixture attempt not found' });
      return json(res, 200, { expired: true, attemptId, buId });
    }
    if (req.method === 'POST' && req.url === '/seed-number-onboarding') {
      const result = await seedNumberOnboarding(data);
      return json(res, 201, result);
    }
    if (req.method === 'GET' && req.url.startsWith('/audit?')) {
      const query = new URL(req.url, 'http://localhost').searchParams;
      const conversationId = query.get('conversationId');
      const conversation = await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversationId } });
      const messages = await prisma.message.findMany({ where: { conversationId }, orderBy: [{ createdAt: 'asc' }, { id: 'asc' }], select: { id: true, content: true, direction: true, authorName: true, createdAt: true } });
      return json(res, 200, { status: conversation.status, unreadCount: conversation.unreadCount, messages });
    }
    if (req.method === 'GET' && req.url.startsWith('/audit-outbound?')) {
      const query = new URL(req.url, 'http://localhost').searchParams;
      const conversationId = query.get('conversationId');
      const content = query.get('content');
      if (!conversationId || !fixtureConversationIds.has(conversationId) || !content) return json(res, 400, { error: 'invalid fixture audit scope' });
      const messages = await prisma.message.findMany({ where: { conversationId, content, direction: 'OUT' }, select: { status: true, externalId: true } });
      return json(res, 200, {
        total: messages.length,
        accepted: messages.filter((message) => message.status === 'ENVIADA').length,
        failed: messages.filter((message) => message.status === 'FALHOU').length,
        failedWithoutExternalId: messages.filter((message) => message.status === 'FALHOU' && !message.externalId).length,
      });
    }
    if (req.method === 'POST' && req.url === '/seed') return json(res, 201, await seed(data));
    if (req.method === 'POST' && req.url === '/send-mode') {
      if (!['accepted', 'unknown'].includes(data.mode)) return json(res, 400, { error: 'invalid mode' });
      onboarding.meta.sendMode = data.mode;
      return json(res, 200, { mode: onboarding.meta.sendMode });
    }
    if (req.method === 'POST' && req.url === '/select-evolution') {
      const conversation = await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: data.conversationId } });
      const channel = await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: conversation.buId, type: 'NAO_OFICIAL' } });
      const identity = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { currentChannelId: channel.id } });
      const message = await prisma.message.create({ data: {
        conversationId: conversation.id, buId: conversation.buId, direction: 'IN', messageType: 'TEXTO',
        content: 'ingress fixture selects Evolution', channelOrigin: 'NAO_OFICIAL', status: 'RECEBIDA',
        externalId: `fixture-ingress-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(16).slice(2)}`,
        providerResourceIdentityId: identity.id, createdAt: new Date(Date.now() + 60_000),
      } });
      return json(res, 201, { id: message.id });
    }
    if (req.method === 'POST' && req.url === '/incoming') return json(res, 200, await incoming(data));
    if (req.method === 'POST' && req.url === '/late-tie') {
      const conversation = await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: data.conversationId } });
      const message = await prisma.message.create({ data: {
        id: String(data.id), conversationId: conversation.id, buId: conversation.buId,
        direction: 'IN', messageType: 'TEXTO', content: 'inserção empatada tardia',
        channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL', status: 'RECEBIDA', createdAt: new Date(data.createdAt),
      } });
      return json(res, 201, { id: message.id });
    }
    if (req.method === 'POST' && req.url === '/revoke') {
      await prisma.userBuRole.deleteMany({ where: { userId: data.userId, buId: data.businessUnitId } });
      return json(res, 200, { revoked: true });
    }
    if (req.method === 'POST' && req.url === '/burst') {
      const conversation = await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: data.conversationId } });
      const count = Math.max(1, Math.min(Number(data.count ?? 120), 500));
      const base = new Date(data.createdAt ?? Date.now());
      await prisma.message.createMany({ data: Array.from({ length: count }, (_, i) => ({
        conversationId: conversation.id, buId: conversation.buId, direction: 'IN', messageType: 'TEXTO',
        content: `burst ${i + 1}`, channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL', status: 'RECEBIDA',
        createdAt: new Date(base.getTime() + i),
      })) });
      return json(res, 201, { count });
    }
    return json(res, 404, { error: 'not found' });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    return json(res, 500, { error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) });
  }
});
server.listen(fixturePort, '127.0.0.1', () => console.log(`timeline fixtures listening on 127.0.0.1:${fixturePort}`));
process.on('SIGTERM', () => server.close(() => prisma.$disconnect().finally(() => process.exit(0))));
process.on('SIGINT', () => server.close(() => prisma.$disconnect().finally(() => process.exit(0))));

```

## api-app/tsconfig.build.json

```text
{
  "extends": "./tsconfig.json",
  "exclude": ["node_modules", "dist", "test", "**/*.spec.ts"]
}

```

## api-app/vitest.config.e2e.ts

```text
import swc from 'unplugin-swc';
import { defineConfig } from 'vitest/config';

// E2E roda contra Postgres DE VERDADE (ADR-0012 §4): o que precisa ser provado
// é o filtro por BU dentro do SQL, e mock de repositório provaria só que o
// código chama o método.
export default defineConfig({
  test: {
    globals: true,
    environment: 'node',
    include: ['test/**/*.e2e-spec.ts'],
    testTimeout: 30_000,
    hookTimeout: 30_000,
    fileParallelism: false,
    maxWorkers: 1,
    minWorkers: 1,
    sequence: { concurrent: false, shuffle: false },
    setupFiles: ['./test/setup-env.ts'],
  },
  plugins: [swc.vite({ module: { type: 'es6' } })],
});

```

## api-app/vitest.config.ts

```text
import swc from 'unplugin-swc';
import { defineConfig } from 'vitest/config';

// Testes de unidade. E2E tem configuração própria (vitest.config.e2e.ts),
// porque e2e precisa de banco e unidade não — ADR-0012 §1.
export default defineConfig({
  test: {
    globals: true,
    environment: 'node',
    include: ['src/**/*.spec.ts'],
  },
  plugins: [swc.vite({ module: { type: 'es6' } })],
});

```

## docker-compose.channels.yml

```text
services:
  postgres:
    ports: !reset []
    labels:
      com.leadshug.channels.owner: number-onboarding

  api:
    ports: !reset []
    environment:
      NODE_ENV: ${CHANNELS_NODE_ENV:-development}
      WEB_ORIGIN: ${CHANNELS_LOCAL_ORIGIN:-http://localhost:${CHANNELS_GATEWAY_HOST_PORT:-8083}}
      LEADSHUG_API_URL: ""
    labels:
      com.leadshug.channels.owner: number-onboarding

  web:
    ports: !reset []
    environment:
      LEADSHUG_API_URL: ""
    labels:
      com.leadshug.channels.owner: number-onboarding

  gateway:
    image: nginx:alpine
    profiles: [channels-local]
    depends_on:
      api:
        condition: service_healthy
      web:
        condition: service_started
    ports:
      - "127.0.0.1:${CHANNELS_GATEWAY_HOST_PORT:-8083}:80"
    volumes:
      - ./docker/local-gateway.conf.template:/etc/nginx/templates/default.conf.template:ro
    labels:
      com.leadshug.channels.owner: number-onboarding
    restart: unless-stopped

  evolution-postgres:
    image: postgres:16
    profiles: [channels-local]
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: ${EVOLUTION_POSTGRES_DB:-evolution}
      POSTGRES_USER: ${EVOLUTION_POSTGRES_USER:-evolution}
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ${EVOLUTION_POSTGRES_PASSWORD:?Set EVOLUTION_POSTGRES_PASSWORD in .env.testing}
    volumes:
      - evolution_pg_data:/var/lib/postgresql/data
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U $$POSTGRES_USER -d $$POSTGRES_DB"]
      interval: 5s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 20
    labels:
      com.leadshug.channels.owner: number-onboarding
    restart: unless-stopped

  evolution-redis:
    image: redis:7.4-alpine
    profiles: [channels-local]
    command: ["redis-server", "--appendonly", "yes"]
    volumes:
      - evolution_redis_data:/data
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD", "redis-cli", "ping"]
      interval: 5s
      timeout: 3s
      retries: 20
    labels:
      com.leadshug.channels.owner: number-onboarding
    restart: unless-stopped

  evolution:
    image: evoapicloud/evolution-api:v2.3.7@sha256:1bd8afc4a6cf48822e6cf02469aeae7bd35a12a6b616eacd1291926307f4d339
    profiles: [channels-local]
    depends_on:
      evolution-postgres:
        condition: service_healthy
      evolution-redis:
        condition: service_healthy
    environment:
      SERVER_NAME: evolution
      SERVER_TYPE: http
      SERVER_PORT: "8080"
      SERVER_URL: ${EVOLUTION_SERVER_URL:-http://evolution:8080}
      SERVER_DISABLE_DOCS: "true"
      DATABASE_ENABLED: "true"
      DATABASE_PROVIDER: postgresql
      DATABASE_CONNECTION_URI: postgresql://${EVOLUTION_POSTGRES_USER:-evolution}:${EVOLUTION_POSTGRES_PASSWORD:?Set EVOLUTION_POSTGRES_PASSWORD in .env.testing}@evolution-postgres:5432/${EVOLUTION_POSTGRES_DB:-evolution}?schema=public
      DATABASE_CONNECTION_CLIENT_NAME: leadshug_channels_local
      DATABASE_SAVE_DATA_INSTANCE: "true"
      DATABASE_SAVE_DATA_NEW_MESSAGE: "true"
      DATABASE_SAVE_MESSAGE_UPDATE: "true"
      DATABASE_SAVE_DATA_CONTACTS: "true"
      DATABASE_SAVE_DATA_CHATS: "true"
      DATABASE_SAVE_DATA_HISTORIC: "true"
      DATABASE_SAVE_DATA_LABELS: "true"
      AUTHENTICATION_API_KEY: ${EVOLUTION_AUTHENTICATION_API_KEY:?Set EVOLUTION_AUTHENTICATION_API_KEY in .env.testing}
      CACHE_REDIS_ENABLED: "true"
      CACHE_REDIS_URI: redis://evolution-redis:6379/6
      CACHE_REDIS_PREFIX_KEY: leadshug_channels_local
      CACHE_REDIS_SAVE_INSTANCES: "true"
      CACHE_LOCAL_ENABLED: "false"
      LOG_LEVEL: ${EVOLUTION_LOG_LEVEL:-ERROR,WARN}
      LOG_COLOR: "false"
    volumes:
      - evolution_instances:/evolution/instances
    expose:
      - "8080"
    labels:
      com.leadshug.channels.owner: number-onboarding
    restart: unless-stopped

  evolution-header-probe:
    image: node:22.23.3-bookworm-slim
    profiles: [channels-probe]
    entrypoint: ["node", "-e"]
    command:
      - |
        const http = require('node:http');
        const { randomUUID } = require('node:crypto');
        const os = require('node:os');
        const port = 8091;
        const receiverIp = Object.values(os.networkInterfaces()).flat().find(address => address && address.family === 'IPv4' && !address.internal)?.address;
        let callbacks = [];
        const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
          const chunks = [];
          let bytes = 0;
          req.on('data', chunk => { bytes += chunk.length; if (bytes <= 1048576) chunks.push(chunk); });
          req.on('end', () => {
            const headerMatches = req.headers['x-leadshug-webhook-secret'] === process.env.PROBE_EXPECTED_HEADER;
            let event = 'other';
            try {
              const parsed = JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks).toString('utf8'));
              event = typeof parsed.event === 'string' ? parsed.event.toLowerCase() : 'other';
            } catch {}
            const status = headerMatches ? 200 : 403;
            callbacks.push({ headerMatches, event, status });
            console.log(JSON.stringify({ method: req.method, path: req.url === '/probe-callback' ? '/probe-callback' : 'other', event, headerMatches, status }));
            res.writeHead(status).end();
          });
        });
        server.listen(port, '0.0.0.0', async () => {
          const base = 'http://evolution:8080';
          const headers = { 'content-type': 'application/json', apikey: process.env.EVOLUTION_AUTHENTICATION_API_KEY };
          const makeProbe = async (wrong) => {
            const name = 'leadshug-cp17-' + randomUUID();
            const expected = process.env.PROBE_EXPECTED_HEADER;
            const response = await fetch(base + '/instance/create', {
              method: 'POST', headers,
              body: JSON.stringify({ instanceName: name, integration: 'WHATSAPP-BAILEYS', qrcode: false,
                webhook: { enabled: true, events: ['INSTANCE_CREATE'], url: 'http://' + receiverIp + ':8091/probe-callback', headers: { 'x-leadshug-webhook-secret': wrong ? 'deliberately-wrong' : expected }, byEvents: false, base64: false } })
            });
            await response.arrayBuffer();
            const wanted = !wrong;
            const until = Date.now() + 15000;
            while (Date.now() < until && !callbacks.some(c => c.headerMatches === wanted && c.event === 'instance.create')) await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 100));
            const received = callbacks.find(c => c.headerMatches === wanted && c.event === 'instance.create');
            let instanceObservedBeforeDelete = false;
            try {
              const listed = await fetch(base + '/instance/fetchInstances', { headers: { apikey: process.env.EVOLUTION_AUTHENTICATION_API_KEY }, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(2000) });
              const instances = listed.ok ? await listed.json() : null;
              instanceObservedBeforeDelete = Array.isArray(instances) && instances.some(instance => instance?.instance?.instanceName === name || instance?.instanceName === name || instance?.name === name);
            } catch {}
            const del = await fetch(base + '/instance/delete/' + encodeURIComponent(name), { method: 'DELETE', headers, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) });
            await del.arrayBuffer();
            let removed = false;
            const cleanupUntil = Date.now() + 10000;
            while (Date.now() < cleanupUntil) {
              try {
                const listed = await fetch(base + '/instance/fetchInstances', { headers: { apikey: process.env.EVOLUTION_AUTHENTICATION_API_KEY }, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(2000) });
                if (!listed.ok) throw new Error('list_failed');
                const instances = await listed.json();
                if (!Array.isArray(instances)) throw new Error('invalid_list');
                const stillPresent = instances.some(instance => instance?.instance?.instanceName === name || instance?.instanceName === name || instance?.name === name);
                if (!stillPresent) { removed = true; break; }
              } catch {}
              await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 250));
            }
            return { createStatus: response.status, callbackReceived: !!received, callbackStatusExpected: !!received && received.status === (wrong ? 403 : 200), instanceObservedBeforeDelete, deleteStatus: del.status, removed };
          };
          try {
            const correct = await makeProbe(false);
            callbacks = [];
            const wrong = await makeProbe(true);
            console.log(JSON.stringify({ correct, wrong }));
            if (correct.createStatus < 200 || correct.createStatus >= 300 || !correct.callbackReceived || !correct.callbackStatusExpected || !correct.instanceObservedBeforeDelete || correct.deleteStatus < 200 || correct.deleteStatus >= 300 || wrong.createStatus < 200 || wrong.createStatus >= 300 || !wrong.callbackReceived || !wrong.callbackStatusExpected || !wrong.instanceObservedBeforeDelete || wrong.deleteStatus < 200 || wrong.deleteStatus >= 300 || !correct.removed || !wrong.removed) process.exitCode = 1;
          } catch {
            console.log(JSON.stringify({ success: false, error: 'probe_failed' }));
            process.exitCode = 1;
          } finally { server.close(); }
        });
    environment:
      EVOLUTION_AUTHENTICATION_API_KEY: ${EVOLUTION_AUTHENTICATION_API_KEY:?Set EVOLUTION_AUTHENTICATION_API_KEY in .env.testing}
      PROBE_EXPECTED_HEADER: leadshug-cp21-fictitious-provider-probe-header
    networks: [default]
    labels:
      com.leadshug.channels.owner: number-onboarding
    restart: "no"

  validation-postgres:
    image: postgres:16
    profiles: [validation]
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: leadshug_timeline_channels_ci
      POSTGRES_USER: channels_validation
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: channels_validation_only
    volumes:
      - validation_pg_data:/var/lib/postgresql/data
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U $$POSTGRES_USER -d $$POSTGRES_DB"]
      interval: 5s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 20
    networks: [validation]
    labels:
      com.leadshug.channels.owner: number-onboarding-validation

  validation-runner:
    image: node:22.23.3-bookworm-slim
    profiles: [validation]
    depends_on:
      validation-postgres:
        condition: service_healthy
    working_dir: /workspace
    environment:
      DATABASE_URL: postgresql://channels_validation:channels_validation_only@validation-postgres:5432/leadshug_timeline_channels_ci?schema=public
      LEADSHUG_E2E_DATABASE_URL: postgresql://channels_validation:channels_validation_only@validation-postgres:5432/leadshug_timeline_channels_ci?schema=public
      LEADSHUG_API_PORT: "3100"
      LEADSHUG_FIXTURE_PORT: "3101"
      SESSION_SECRET: validation-session-secret-only
      INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN: validation-internal-token-only
      LH_ARQUIVOS_DIR: /tmp/leadshug-channels-validation-files
      LH_CHANNEL_UNOFFICIAL: "true"
      META_APP_ID: fixture-app
      META_APP_SECRET: fixture-meta-secret-only
      META_SIGNUP_CONFIG_ID: fixture-config
      GRAPH_VERSION: v26.0
      CHANNEL_CREDENTIAL_KEY_ID: validation-key-v1
      CHANNEL_CREDENTIAL_KEY: AAECAwQFBgcICQoLDA0ODxAREhMUFRYXGBkaGxwdHh8=
      WEBHOOK_VERIFY_TOKEN: validation-webhook-token-only
      EVOLUTION_API_URL: http://127.0.0.1:3101/provider/evolution
      EVOLUTION_API_KEY: validation-provider-key-only
      EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_URL: http://127.0.0.1:3100/webhooks/evolution
      EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET: validation-evolution-header-secret
      WEB_ORIGIN: http://127.0.0.1:4173
      WEBHOOK_FAKE_MODE: "true"
      CHANNELS_EXPECTED_WEB_PACKAGE_SHA256: ${CHANNELS_EXPECTED_WEB_PACKAGE_SHA256:-}
    volumes:
      - type: bind
        source: ${CHANNELS_SOURCE_WINDOWS:-./}
        target: /workspace
        bind:
          create_host_path: false
      - channels_api_workspace:/workspace/api-app
      - type: bind
        source: ${CHANNELS_API_GIT_WINDOWS:-./api-app/.git}
        target: /workspace/api-app/.git
        read_only: true
        bind: {create_host_path: false}
      - type: bind
        source: ${CHANNELS_API_SRC_WINDOWS:-./api-app/src}
        target: /workspace/api-app/src
        read_only: true
        bind: {create_host_path: false}
      - type: bind
        source: ${CHANNELS_API_TEST_WINDOWS:-./api-app/test}
        target: /workspace/api-app/test
        read_only: true
        bind: {create_host_path: false}
      - type: bind
        source: ${CHANNELS_API_PRISMA_WINDOWS:-./api-app/prisma}
        target: /workspace/api-app/prisma
        read_only: true
        bind: {create_host_path: false}
      - type: bind
        source: ${CHANNELS_API_PACKAGE_JSON_WINDOWS:-./api-app/package.json}
        target: /workspace/api-app/package.json
        read_only: true
        bind: {create_host_path: false}
      - type: bind
        source: ${CHANNELS_API_PACKAGE_LOCK_WINDOWS:-./api-app/package-lock.json}
        target: /workspace/api-app/package-lock.json
        read_only: true
        bind: {create_host_path: false}
      - type: bind
        source: ${CHANNELS_API_ENV_EXAMPLE_WINDOWS:-./api-app/.env.example}
        target: /workspace/api-app/.env.example
        read_only: true
        bind: {create_host_path: false}
      - type: bind
        source: ${CHANNELS_API_DOCKERIGNORE_WINDOWS:-./api-app/.dockerignore}
        target: /workspace/api-app/.dockerignore
        read_only: true
        bind: {create_host_path: false}
      - type: bind
        source: ${CHANNELS_API_GITIGNORE_WINDOWS:-./api-app/.gitignore}
        target: /workspace/api-app/.gitignore
        read_only: true
        bind: {create_host_path: false}
      - type: bind
        source: ${CHANNELS_API_ENTRYPOINT_WINDOWS:-./api-app/docker/entrypoint.sh}
        target: /workspace/api-app/docker/entrypoint.sh
        read_only: true
        bind: {create_host_path: false}
      - type: bind
```

## scripts/check-channels-ci-parity.py

```text
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Fail-closed parity checks for the shared channel validation lifecycle."""
from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
from typing import Any

import yaml


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
WORKFLOW_PATH = ROOT / ".github/workflows/ci.yml"
RUNNER_PATH = ROOT / "scripts/test-channels-local.sh"
COMMON_GUARD = "python3 /workspace/scripts/check-channels-ci-parity.py --self-test"
RETAINED_GUARD = "python3 scripts/check-channels-ci-parity.py --self-test"
WSL_SHARED_SOCKET = "unix:///mnt/wsl/docker-desktop/shared-sockets/guest-services/docker.sock"


class ContractError(Exception):
    pass


class StrictNoAliasLoader(yaml.SafeLoader):
    def compose_node(self, parent: Any, index: Any) -> Any:
        if self.check_event(yaml.AliasEvent):
            raise ContractError("YAML aliases are not accepted")
        return super().compose_node(parent, index)

    def construct_mapping(self, node: yaml.MappingNode, deep: bool = False) -> dict[Any, Any]:
        if not isinstance(node, yaml.MappingNode):
            raise ContractError("expected YAML mapping")
        mapping: dict[Any, Any] = {}
        for key_node, value_node in node.value:
            key = self.construct_object(key_node, deep=deep)
            if key in mapping:
                raise ContractError(f"duplicate YAML key: {key}")
            mapping[key] = self.construct_object(value_node, deep=deep)
        return mapping


def parse_workflow(source: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        value = yaml.load(source, Loader=StrictNoAliasLoader)
    except ContractError:
        raise
    except yaml.YAMLError as exc:
        raise ContractError(f"invalid workflow YAML: {exc}") from exc
    if not isinstance(value, dict) or not isinstance(value.get("jobs"), dict):
        raise ContractError("workflow jobs mapping missing")
    return value


def step_index(steps: list[dict[str, Any]], name: str) -> int:
    for index, step in enumerate(steps):
        if step.get("name") == name:
            return index
    return -1


def validate_contract(workflow: dict[str, Any], runner_source: str) -> None:
    triggers = workflow.get("on", workflow.get(True))
    if not isinstance(triggers, dict) or "pull_request" not in triggers:
        raise ContractError("pull_request workflow trigger missing")
    jobs = workflow.get("jobs")
    if not isinstance(jobs, dict):
        raise ContractError("jobs mapping missing")
    channel = jobs.get("channels-validation")
    if not isinstance(channel, dict):
        raise ContractError("channels-validation job missing")
    if channel.get("if") != "github.event_name == 'pull_request' && github.head_ref != 'main'":
        raise ContractError("channels job must be gated to non-main pull requests")
    if channel.get("runs-on") != "ubuntu-latest" or channel.get("timeout-minutes") != 60:
        raise ContractError("channels job runner or timeout drifted")
    if channel.get("permissions") != {"contents": "read"}:
        raise ContractError("channels job permissions must be read-only")
    if channel.get("continue-on-error") or channel.get("if") in (False, "false"):
        raise ContractError("channels job cannot be skipped or swallow failures")
    steps = channel.get("steps")
    if not isinstance(steps, list):
        raise ContractError("channels job steps missing")
    checkout_indices = [index for index, step in enumerate(steps) if isinstance(step, dict) and step.get("uses") == "actions/checkout@v4"]
    if not checkout_indices:
        raise ContractError("channels job checkout missing")
    private_steps = [step for step in steps if isinstance(step, dict) and step.get("name") == "Checkout private channel validation sources"]
    private_indices = [index for index, step in enumerate(steps) if isinstance(step, dict) and step.get("name") == "Checkout private channel validation sources"]
    if len(private_steps) != 1 or private_indices[0] <= checkout_indices[0]:
        raise ContractError("private API/Web checkout step missing or duplicated")
    private = private_steps[0]
    if "SUBMODULES_REPO_TOKEN" not in (private.get("env") or {}) or "${{ secrets.SUBMODULES_REPO_TOKEN }}" not in private["env"]["SUBMODULES_REPO_TOKEN"]:
        raise ContractError("existing private checkout token pattern missing")
    private_run = str(private.get("run", ""))
    if "test -n \"$SUBMODULES_REPO_TOKEN\"" not in private_run or "git submodule sync -- api-app web-app" not in private_run or "git submodule update --init --depth=1 api-app web-app" not in private_run:
        raise ContractError("private API/Web checkout must fail explicitly and use exact submodule paths")
    validate_steps = [step for step in steps if isinstance(step, dict) and step.get("name") == "Validate channel suite and lifecycle"]
    validate_indices = [index for index, step in enumerate(steps) if isinstance(step, dict) and step.get("name") == "Validate channel suite and lifecycle"]
    if len(validate_steps) != 1 or validate_indices[0] <= private_indices[0]:
        raise ContractError("shared channel runner step missing or duplicated")
    validate = validate_steps[0]
    if (validate.get("env") or {}).get("CHANNELS_DOCKER_TRANSPORT") != "linux" or validate.get("run") != "bash scripts/test-channels-local.sh all":
        raise ContractError("channels CI must use the shared Linux all runner")
    if any(step.get("continue-on-error") or "if" in step for step in steps if isinstance(step, dict)):
        raise ContractError("channels job cannot swallow failures")

    compose_job = jobs.get("compose")
    compose_steps = compose_job.get("steps") if isinstance(compose_job, dict) else None
    if not isinstance(compose_steps, list) or compose_job.get("continue-on-error") or "if" in compose_job:
        raise ContractError("retained compose job missing")
    admission_index = step_index(compose_steps, "Check channel suite admission")
    config_index = step_index(compose_steps, "Validate the runtime topology")
    compose_checkout_index = next((index for index, step in enumerate(compose_steps) if isinstance(step, dict) and step.get("uses") == "actions/checkout@v4"), -1)
    compose_private_index = next((index for index, step in enumerate(compose_steps) if isinstance(step, dict) and step.get("name") == "Checkout private channel validation sources"), -1)
    if admission_index < 0 or config_index < 0 or admission_index >= config_index or compose_checkout_index < 0 or compose_private_index <= compose_checkout_index or compose_private_index >= admission_index:
        raise ContractError("retained compose admission must precede Compose config")
    required_compose_steps = [compose_steps[index] for index in (compose_checkout_index, compose_private_index, admission_index, config_index)]
    if any(step.get("continue-on-error") or "if" in step for step in required_compose_steps):
        raise ContractError("retained compose admission/config steps cannot be skipped or swallow failures")
    compose_private = compose_steps[compose_private_index]
    compose_private_env = compose_private.get("env") or {}
    if "${{ secrets.SUBMODULES_REPO_TOKEN }}" not in str(compose_private_env.get("SUBMODULES_REPO_TOKEN", "")):
        raise ContractError("retained private checkout token missing")
    compose_private_run = str(compose_private.get("run", ""))
    if "test -n \"$SUBMODULES_REPO_TOKEN\"" not in compose_private_run or "git submodule sync -- api-app web-app" not in compose_private_run or "git submodule update --init --depth=1 api-app web-app" not in compose_private_run:
        raise ContractError("retained private checkout must initialize exact API/Web paths")
    admission_run = str(compose_steps[admission_index].get("run", ""))
    if "sudo apt-get update -qq" not in admission_run or "sudo apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends python3-yaml" not in admission_run or RETAINED_GUARD not in admission_run:
        raise ContractError("retained compose guard invocation or prerequisites missing")

    marker_index = runner_source.find("RUNNER_SCRIPT='")
    if marker_index < 0:
        raise ContractError("common RUNNER_SCRIPT missing")
    runner = runner_source[marker_index:]
    runner_body = runner.split("RUNNER_SCRIPT='", 1)[1].split("\n'", 1)[0]
    runner_lines = [line.strip() for line in runner_body.splitlines()]
    if "set -eu" not in runner_lines:
        raise ContractError("common runner must fail on mandatory command errors")
    if COMMON_GUARD not in runner_lines:
        raise ContractError("common parity guard must be a standalone mandatory command")
    if "npm run test:e2e" not in runner_lines:
        raise ContractError("API E2E suite must be a standalone mandatory command")
    if "apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends openssl libssl3 ca-certificates bash git python3 python3-yaml" not in runner:
        raise ContractError("common runner lacks parity guard prerequisites")
    guard_index = runner.find(COMMON_GUARD)
    product_index = runner.find("cd /workspace/api-app\nnpm ci")
    apt_index = runner.find("apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends")
    if guard_index < 0 or not (apt_index < guard_index < product_index):
        raise ContractError("common guard must run after prerequisites and before source/npm/product work")
    ordered = [
        "test -f /workspace/api-app/.env",
        "cmp -s /workspace/api-app/.env /workspace/api-app/.env.example",
        "for private_path in /workspace/.env /workspace/.env.testing; do",
        "bound_sha=\"$(sha256sum /workspace/api-app/package.json",
        "test \"$bound_sha\" = \"$CHANNELS_EXPECTED_API_PACKAGE_SHA256\"",
        "Principal API/Web source binds and API/root dotenv masks verified before npm.",
        "cd /workspace/api-app\nnpm ci",
        "npx prisma -v",
        "npm run prisma:generate",
        "cd /workspace/web-app\nnpm ci",
        "npm run prisma:migrate",
        "npx prisma migrate status",
        "npm run lint",
        "    npm run build\n    npm test\n    npm run test:e2e",
        "cd /workspace/web-app\n    npm run build\n    npm test",
        "npx playwright install --with-deps chromium",
        "npm run test:tela -- --trace off",
    ]
    positions = []
    offset = 0
    for item in ordered:
        position = runner.find(item, offset)
        positions.append(position)
        if position >= 0:
            offset = position + len(item)
    if any(position < 0 for position in positions) or positions != sorted(positions):
        raise ContractError("common API E2E, Web build/unit, and browser suite order drifted")
    if "CHANNELS_CONFIG_ONLY" not in runner_source:
        raise ContractError("config-only safe control missing")
    if "capture_inventory" not in runner_source or "namespace state is unknown" not in runner_source:
        raise ContractError("checked Docker inventories missing")
    if (WSL_SHARED_SOCKET not in runner_source or
            '"${WSL_DISTRO_NAME:-}"' not in runner_source or
            '"${DOCKER_HOST:-}" != "unix:///mnt/wsl/docker-desktop/shared-sockets/guest-services/docker.sock"' not in runner_source or
            'printf \'/run/desktop/mnt/host/%s/%s\'' not in runner_source or
            'CHANNELS_SOURCE_WINDOWS="$(bind_host_path "$ROOT_DIR")"' not in runner_source or
            '$(bind_host_path "$path")' not in runner_source or
            'COMPOSE+=(-f "$temp_compose_overlay")' not in runner_source):
        raise ContractError("WSL Docker Desktop bind path translation or its narrow gate drifted")
    for overlay_fragment in (
        "temp_compose_overlay=\"$(mktemp)\"",
        "validation_api_env_mask:",
        "validation_root_env_mask:",
        "validation_root_env_testing_mask:",
        'file: %s\\n' ,
        '"$(yaml_scalar "$CHANNELS_API_ENV_EXAMPLE_WINDOWS")"',
        '"$(yaml_scalar "$CHANNELS_ROOT_ENV_EXAMPLE_WINDOWS")"',
        'rm -f "$temp_env" "$temp_compose_overlay"',
    ):
        if overlay_fragment not in runner_source:
            raise ContractError("WSL dotenv-mask overlay mapping or cleanup drifted")
    if "Validation cleanup failed" not in runner_source or "trap 'exit 130' INT" not in runner_source or "trap 'exit 143' TERM" not in runner_source:
        raise ContractError("observable lifecycle cleanup or signal handling missing")


def parse_and_validate(workflow_text: str, runner_source: str) -> None:
    validate_contract(parse_workflow(workflow_text), runner_source)


def assert_mutation_rejected(name: str, workflow_text: str, runner_source: str, mutate: Any) -> None:
    workflow = parse_workflow(workflow_text)
    altered_workflow, altered_runner = mutate(copy.deepcopy(workflow), runner_source)
    try:
        validate_contract(altered_workflow, altered_runner)
    except ContractError:
        return
    raise AssertionError(f"drift mutation was accepted: {name}")


def fake_wrapper_checks(runner_path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    results: dict[str, Any] = {"inventory_failures": {}, "empty_config_control": None, "collision_control": None, "mandatory_failure_cleanup": None}
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="channels-parity-selftest-") as tmp:
        root = Path(tmp)
        fake_bin = root / "bin"
        fake_bin.mkdir()
        log_path = root / "docker-calls.jsonl"
        docker = fake_bin / "docker"
        docker.write_text("""#!/usr/bin/env python3
import json, os, sys
args = sys.argv[1:]
with open(os.environ['DOCKER_FAKE_LOG'], 'a', encoding='utf-8') as stream:
    stream.write(json.dumps(args) + '\\n')
joined = ' '.join(args)
if args[:2] == ['ps', '-a']:
    if os.environ.get('DOCKER_FAKE_FAIL') == 'ps': sys.exit(31)
    if os.environ.get('DOCKER_FAKE_COLLISION') == 'container': print('leadshug_channels_validation_existing')
elif args[:2] == ['volume', 'ls']:
    if os.environ.get('DOCKER_FAKE_FAIL') == 'volume': sys.exit(32)
    if os.environ.get('DOCKER_FAKE_COLLISION') == 'volume': print('leadshug_channels_validation_existing')
elif args[:2] == ['network', 'ls']:
    if os.environ.get('DOCKER_FAKE_FAIL') == 'network': sys.exit(33)
    if os.environ.get('DOCKER_FAKE_COLLISION') == 'network': print('leadshug_channels_validation_existing')
elif args and args[0] == 'compose':
    if ' config ' in f' {joined} ':
        expected_root = os.environ.get('DOCKER_FAKE_EXPECT_ROOT')
        if expected_root:
            env_files = [args[index + 1] for index, part in enumerate(args[:-1]) if part == '--env-file']
            values = {}
            if env_files:
                with open(env_files[-1], encoding='utf-8') as stream:
                    for line in stream:
                        key, separator, value = line.rstrip('\\n').partition('=')
                        if separator: values[key] = value
            root = values.get('CHANNELS_SOURCE_WINDOWS')
            dockerignore = values.get('CHANNELS_API_DOCKERIGNORE_WINDOWS')
            print('DOCKER_FAKE_BIND_MATCH=' + str(root == expected_root and dockerignore == expected_root + '/api-app/.dockerignore').lower())
            compose_files = [args[index + 1] for index, part in enumerate(args[:-1]) if part == '-f']
            expect_overlay = os.environ.get('DOCKER_FAKE_EXPECT_OVERLAY') == '1'
            overlay_match = False
            if expect_overlay and len(compose_files) == 3:
                overlay = compose_files[-1]
                contents = open(overlay, encoding='utf-8').read()
                expected_lines = [
                    '  validation_api_env_mask:\\n',
                    '    file: "' + expected_root + '/api-app/.env.example"\\n',
                    '  validation_root_env_mask:\\n',
                    '    file: "' + expected_root + '/.env.example"\\n',
                    '  validation_root_env_testing_mask:\\n',
                    '    file: "' + expected_root + '/.env.example"\\n',
                ]
                overlay_match = all(line in contents.splitlines(keepends=True) for line in expected_lines)
                print('DOCKER_FAKE_OVERLAY_PATH=' + overlay)
            elif not expect_overlay and len(compose_files) == 2:
                overlay_match = True
            print('DOCKER_FAKE_OVERLAY_MATCH=' + str(overlay_match).lower())
        sys.exit(0)
    if ' up ' in f' {joined} ':
        sys.exit(0)
    if ' down ' in f' {joined} ':
        sys.exit(0)
    if ' run ' in f' {joined} ':
        if os.environ.get('DOCKER_FAKE_FAIL_REQUIRED_LEAF') == '1':
            marker = args.index('sh')
            script = args[marker + 2]
            env = os.environ.copy()
            env['PATH'] = os.environ['DOCKER_FAKE_LEAF_BIN'] + os.pathsep + env.get('PATH', '')
            child = __import__('subprocess').run(['sh', '-ec', script, *args[marker + 3:]], env=env)
            sys.exit(child.returncode)
        sys.exit(91)
    sys.exit(0)
sys.exit(0)
""", encoding="utf-8")
        docker.chmod(0o755)
        leaf_bin = root / "leaf-bin"
        leaf_bin.mkdir()
        for command, body in {
            "node": "#!/bin/sh\necho v22.0.0\n",
            "apt-get": "#!/bin/sh\nexit 0\n",
            # The real RUNNER_SCRIPT reaches this mandatory guard after its
            # prerequisites; failing here proves errexit and EXIT cleanup.
            "python3": "#!/bin/sh\nexit 42\n",
        }.items():
            path = leaf_bin / command
            path.write_text(body, encoding="utf-8")
            path.chmod(0o755)

        def run_case(label: str, extra: dict[str, str]) -> tuple[subprocess.CompletedProcess[str], list[list[str]]]:
            log_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
            env = os.environ.copy()
            env.pop("DOCKER_FAKE_FAIL", None)
            env.pop("DOCKER_FAKE_COLLISION", None)
            env.pop("WSL_DISTRO_NAME", None)
            env.pop("DOCKER_HOST", None)
            env.update({
                "PATH": f"{fake_bin}{os.pathsep}{env.get('PATH', '')}",
                "CHANNELS_DOCKER_TRANSPORT": "linux",
                "CHANNELS_CONFIG_ONLY": "1",
                "DOCKER_FAKE_LOG": str(log_path),
                "DOCKER_FAKE_LEAF_BIN": str(leaf_bin),
                **extra,
            })
            result = subprocess.run(["bash", str(runner_path), "all"], cwd=runner_path.parent.parent,
                                    env=env, text=True, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, timeout=30)
            calls = [json.loads(line) for line in log_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()] if log_path.exists() else []
            return result, calls

        for failure in ("ps", "volume", "network"):
            result, calls = run_case(f"fail-{failure}", {"DOCKER_FAKE_FAIL": failure})
            compose_calls = [call for call in calls if call and call[0] == "compose"]
            if result.returncode == 0 or compose_calls:
                raise AssertionError(f"failed {failure} inventory reached Compose or succeeded: {result.stdout[-1000:]}")
            expected = {"ps": 1, "volume": 2, "network": 3}[failure]
            if len(calls) != expected:
                raise AssertionError(f"failed {failure} inventory did not stop immediately: {calls}")
            results["inventory_failures"][failure] = {"exit_code": result.returncode, "docker_calls": len(calls), "compose_calls": len(compose_calls)}

        result, calls = run_case("empty-config", {})
        compose_calls = [call for call in calls if call and call[0] == "compose"]
        if result.returncode != 0 or len(compose_calls) != 1 or not any("config" in call for call in compose_calls):
            raise AssertionError(f"empty namespace did not reach config-only control: {result.stdout[-1000:]}")
        if any(any(word in call for word in ("run", "up", "down")) for call in compose_calls):
            raise AssertionError(f"config-only control mutated resources: {compose_calls}")
        results["empty_config_control"] = {"exit_code": result.returncode, "compose_calls": compose_calls}

        checkout = runner_path.parent.parent.resolve().as_posix()
        native_root = checkout
        result, calls = run_case("native-path-control", {"DOCKER_FAKE_EXPECT_ROOT": native_root})
        if result.returncode != 0 or "DOCKER_FAKE_BIND_MATCH=true" not in result.stdout or "DOCKER_FAKE_OVERLAY_MATCH=true" not in result.stdout:
            raise AssertionError(f"native Linux bind paths changed unexpectedly: {result.stdout[-1000:]}")
        results["native_path_control"] = {"exit_code": result.returncode, "posix_bind_paths_preserved": True, "overlay_added": False}

        parts = checkout.split("/", 3)
        if len(parts) == 4 and parts[1] == "mnt" and len(parts[2]) == 1 and parts[2].islower():
            desktop_root = "/run/desktop/mnt/host/" + parts[2] + "/" + parts[3]
            result, calls = run_case("wsl-path-adapter", {
                "DOCKER_FAKE_EXPECT_ROOT": desktop_root,
                "DOCKER_FAKE_EXPECT_OVERLAY": "1",
                "WSL_DISTRO_NAME": "Ubuntu",
                "DOCKER_HOST": WSL_SHARED_SOCKET,
            })
            if result.returncode != 0 or "DOCKER_FAKE_BIND_MATCH=true" not in result.stdout or "DOCKER_FAKE_OVERLAY_MATCH=true" not in result.stdout:
                raise AssertionError(f"WSL Docker Desktop bind/mask overlay was not faithful: {result.stdout[-1000:]}")
            overlay_marker = next((line.split("=", 1)[1] for line in result.stdout.splitlines() if line.startswith("DOCKER_FAKE_OVERLAY_PATH=")), None)
            if not overlay_marker or Path(overlay_marker).exists():
                raise AssertionError("ephemeral WSL Compose overlay was not removed after the runner exited")
            results["wsl_bind_path_control"] = {"exit_code": result.returncode, "same_checkout_suffix": True, "dotenv_masks_same_public_sources": True, "overlay_removed_after_exit": True}
        else:
            results["wsl_bind_path_control"] = {"skipped": "self-test checkout is not on a WSL-mounted drive"}

        result, calls = run_case("unsupported-wsl-socket", {
            "WSL_DISTRO_NAME": "Ubuntu", "DOCKER_HOST": "unix:///var/run/docker.sock"})
        if result.returncode == 0 or calls:
            raise AssertionError("unsupported WSL Docker socket was not rejected before CLI use")
        results["unsupported_wsl_socket_control"] = {"exit_code": result.returncode, "docker_calls": len(calls)}

        result, calls = run_case("collision", {"DOCKER_FAKE_COLLISION": "container"})
        compose_calls = [call for call in calls if call and call[0] == "compose"]
        if result.returncode == 0 or compose_calls:
            raise AssertionError(f"collision control reached Compose: {result.stdout[-1000:]}")
        results["collision_control"] = {"exit_code": result.returncode, "compose_calls": len(compose_calls)}

        result, calls = run_case("mandatory-leaf-failure", {
            "CHANNELS_CONFIG_ONLY": "0",
            "DOCKER_FAKE_FAIL_REQUIRED_LEAF": "1",
        })
        lifecycle = [call for call in calls if call and call[0] == "compose" and any(part in call for part in ("run", "down"))]
        if result.returncode != 42 or not any("run" in call for call in lifecycle) or not any("down" in call for call in lifecycle):
            raise AssertionError(f"mandatory RUNNER_SCRIPT guard failure did not propagate through observable cleanup: exit={result.returncode}, lifecycle={lifecycle}, output={result.stdout[-1000:]}")
        results["mandatory_failure_cleanup"] = {
            "exit_code": result.returncode,
            "required_leaf": "fake python3 reached through actual RUNNER_SCRIPT and returned 42",
            "compose_lifecycle": ["run", "down"],
            "cleanup_observed": True,
            "provider_or_real_docker_invoked": False,
        }
    return results


def self_test() -> None:
    workflow_text = WORKFLOW_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    runner_source = RUNNER_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    parse_and_validate(workflow_text, runner_source)

    for malformed in ("jobs:\n  a: 1\n  a: 2\n", "jobs: &jobs\n  a: {}\ncopy: *jobs\n"):
        try:
            parse_workflow(malformed)
        except ContractError:
            continue
        raise AssertionError("duplicate-key or YAML-alias control was accepted")

    def workflow_mutation(action: Any) -> Any:
        def apply(workflow: dict[str, Any], runner: str) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], str]:
            altered = action(workflow, runner)
            return altered if isinstance(altered, tuple) else (workflow, runner)
        return apply

    mutations = {
        "missing-pr-trigger": workflow_mutation(lambda workflow, _: (workflow.pop("on", None), workflow.pop(True, None), workflow)[-1]),
        "missing-channels-job": workflow_mutation(lambda workflow, _: workflow["jobs"].pop("channels-validation")),
        "missing-retained-guard": workflow_mutation(lambda workflow, _: workflow["jobs"]["compose"].__setitem__("steps", [step for step in workflow["jobs"]["compose"]["steps"] if step.get("name") != "Check channel suite admission"])),
        "weakened-job-gate": workflow_mutation(lambda workflow, _: workflow["jobs"]["channels-validation"].__setitem__("if", "always()")),
        "swallowed-job-errors": workflow_mutation(lambda workflow, _: workflow["jobs"]["channels-validation"]["steps"][1].__setitem__("continue-on-error", True)),
        "skipped-validate-step": workflow_mutation(lambda workflow, _: next(step for step in workflow["jobs"]["channels-validation"]["steps"] if step.get("name") == "Validate channel suite and lifecycle").__setitem__("if", "${{ false }}")),
        "swallowed-retained-job": workflow_mutation(lambda workflow, _: workflow["jobs"]["compose"].__setitem__("continue-on-error", True)),
        "skipped-retained-admission": workflow_mutation(lambda workflow, _: next(step for step in workflow["jobs"]["compose"]["steps"] if step.get("name") == "Check channel suite admission").__setitem__("if", "${{ false }}")),
        "missing-retained-private-checkout": workflow_mutation(lambda workflow, _: next(step for step in workflow["jobs"]["compose"]["steps"] if step.get("name") == "Checkout private channel validation sources").__setitem__("run", "true")),
        "missing-common-guard": workflow_mutation(lambda workflow, runner: (workflow, runner.replace(COMMON_GUARD, ""))),
        "swallowed-common-guard-failure": workflow_mutation(lambda workflow, runner: (workflow, runner.replace(COMMON_GUARD, COMMON_GUARD + " || true"))),
        "missing-e2e": workflow_mutation(lambda workflow, runner: (workflow, runner.replace("npm run test:e2e", "echo skipped"))),
        "swallowed-e2e-failure": workflow_mutation(lambda workflow, runner: (workflow, runner.replace("npm run test:e2e", "npm run test:e2e || true"))),
        "missing-browser": workflow_mutation(lambda workflow, runner: (workflow, runner.replace("npm run test:tela -- --trace off", "echo skipped"))),
        "missing-migrate": workflow_mutation(lambda workflow, runner: (workflow, runner.replace("npm run prisma:migrate", "echo skipped"))),
        "missing-generate": workflow_mutation(lambda workflow, runner: (workflow, runner.replace("npm run prisma:generate", "echo skipped"))),
        "missing-lint": workflow_mutation(lambda workflow, runner: (workflow, runner.replace("npm run lint", "echo skipped"))),
        "reordered-suite": workflow_mutation(lambda workflow, runner: (workflow, runner.replace("npm run test:e2e", "TEMP").replace("npm run build", "npm run test:e2e").replace("TEMP", "npm run build"))),
        "missing-shell-guard": workflow_mutation(lambda workflow, runner: (workflow, runner.replace("capture_inventory", "inventory_capture"))),
        "widened-wsl-socket": workflow_mutation(lambda workflow, runner: (workflow, runner.replace(WSL_SHARED_SOCKET, "unix:///var/run/docker.sock"))),
        "changed-wsl-host-prefix": workflow_mutation(lambda workflow, runner: (workflow, runner.replace("/run/desktop/mnt/host/%s/%s", "/run/desktop/other/%s/%s"))),
        "wrong-wsl-mask-source": workflow_mutation(lambda workflow, runner: (workflow, runner.replace('"$(yaml_scalar "$CHANNELS_API_ENV_EXAMPLE_WINDOWS")"', '"$(yaml_scalar "$CHANNELS_ROOT_ENV_EXAMPLE_WINDOWS")"'))),
        "missing-wsl-overlay": workflow_mutation(lambda workflow, runner: (workflow, runner.replace('COMPOSE+=(-f "$temp_compose_overlay")', "# overlay removed"))),
    }
    for name, mutation in mutations.items():
        assert_mutation_rejected(name, workflow_text, runner_source, mutation)

    fake_results = fake_wrapper_checks(RUNNER_PATH)
    print(json.dumps({"guard": "channels-ci-parity-v1", "yaml": "safe-no-alias-no-duplicates", "mutations_rejected": list(mutations), "outer_wrapper": fake_results}, sort_keys=True))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if sys.argv[1:] != ["--self-test"]:
        print("Usage: python3 scripts/check-channels-ci-parity.py --self-test", file=sys.stderr)
        raise SystemExit(2)
    self_test()

```

## scripts/test-channels-local.sh

```text
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

ROOT_DIR="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
TARGET="${1:-all}"
case "$TARGET" in
  api|web|browser|all) ;;
  *) printf 'Usage: %s [api|web|browser|all]\n' "$0" >&2; exit 2 ;;
esac

CHANNELS_DOCKER_TRANSPORT="${CHANNELS_DOCKER_TRANSPORT:-windows}"
case "$CHANNELS_DOCKER_TRANSPORT" in
  windows)
    command -v docker.exe >/dev/null || { echo 'Windows Docker CLI (docker.exe) is required.' >&2; exit 1; }
    command -v wslpath >/dev/null || { echo 'wslpath is required to bind the principal checkout.' >&2; exit 1; }
    DOCKER=(docker.exe)
    host_path() { wslpath -w "$1"; }
    bind_host_path() { wslpath -w "$1"; }
    ;;
  linux)
    command -v docker >/dev/null || { echo 'Linux Docker CLI (docker) is required for the linux transport.' >&2; exit 1; }
    DOCKER=(docker)
    host_path() { printf '%s' "$1"; }
    bind_host_path() {
      local path="$1"
      if [[ -n "${WSL_DISTRO_NAME:-}" ]]; then
        if [[ "${DOCKER_HOST:-}" != "unix:///mnt/wsl/docker-desktop/shared-sockets/guest-services/docker.sock" ]]; then
          echo 'WSL Linux transport requires the verified Docker Desktop shared socket.' >&2
          return 1
        fi
        if [[ "$path" =~ ^/mnt/([a-z])/(.+)$ ]]; then
          printf '/run/desktop/mnt/host/%s/%s' "${BASH_REMATCH[1]}" "${BASH_REMATCH[2]}"
        else
          printf 'Unsupported WSL bind source path for Docker Desktop: %s\n' "$path" >&2
          return 1
        fi
      else
        printf '%s' "$path"
      fi
    }
    ;;
  *) printf 'Unsupported CHANNELS_DOCKER_TRANSPORT: %s\n' "$CHANNELS_DOCKER_TRANSPORT" >&2; exit 2 ;;
esac
CHANNELS_SOURCE_WINDOWS="$(bind_host_path "$ROOT_DIR")"
CHANNELS_EXPECTED_API_PACKAGE_SHA256="$(sha256sum "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/package.json" | cut -d ' ' -f 1)"
CHANNELS_EXPECTED_WEB_PACKAGE_SHA256="$(sha256sum "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/package.json" | cut -d ' ' -f 1)"

# Keep host inputs as a positive inventory. Do not discover dotenv files with
# find/glob over either application tree: those trees can contain private
# configuration and local-only data. The exact public bind paths are listed
# below; only api-app/.env.example is mounted, while .env and .env.testing are
# supplied inside the validation container by the Compose config masks below.

cd "$ROOT_DIR"
temp_env="$(mktemp)"
temp_compose_overlay=""
chmod 600 "$temp_env"
cat > "$temp_env" <<EOF
CHANNELS_SOURCE_WINDOWS=$CHANNELS_SOURCE_WINDOWS
SESSION_SECRET=channels-validation-session-only
INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN=channels-validation-internal-only
POSTGRES_PASSWORD=channels-validation-postgres-only
EVOLUTION_POSTGRES_PASSWORD=channels-validation-evolution-postgres-only
EVOLUTION_AUTHENTICATION_API_KEY=channels-validation-evolution-api-key-only
EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET=validation-evolution-header-secret
CHANNELS_EXPECTED_API_PACKAGE_SHA256=$CHANNELS_EXPECTED_API_PACKAGE_SHA256
CHANNELS_EXPECTED_WEB_PACKAGE_SHA256=$CHANNELS_EXPECTED_WEB_PACKAGE_SHA256
EOF
add_bind_path() {
  local key="$1" path="$2"
  local relative_path="${path#"$ROOT_DIR"/}"
  case "$relative_path" in
    .env|.env.testing|api-app/.env|api-app/.env.testing|api-app/.local-files|api-app/.local-files/*|web-app/.env|web-app/.env.*)
      printf 'Refusing excluded private configuration/data path: %s\n' "$relative_path" >&2
      exit 1
      ;;
  esac
  case "$relative_path" in
    api-app/.git|api-app/src|api-app/test|api-app/prisma|api-app/package.json|api-app/package-lock.json|api-app/.env.example|api-app/.dockerignore|api-app/.gitignore|api-app/docker/entrypoint.sh|api-app/Dockerfile|api-app/eslint.config.mjs|api-app/nest-cli.json|api-app/railway.json|api-app/tsconfig.json|api-app/tsconfig.build.json|api-app/vitest.config.ts|api-app/vitest.config.e2e.ts|api-app/vitest.config.cov.ts|web-app/.git|web-app/src|web-app/e2e|web-app/public|web-app/package.json|web-app/package-lock.json|web-app/index.html|web-app/playwright.config.ts|web-app/tsconfig.json|web-app/vite.config.ts|web-app/railway.json|web-app/.gitignore|web-app/.dockerignore|web-app/Dockerfile|web-app/docker/40-config-js.sh|web-app/nginx/default.conf.template) ;;
    *) printf 'Refusing path outside the public bind inventory: %s\n' "$relative_path" >&2; exit 1 ;;
  esac
  [[ -e "$path" ]] || { printf 'Expected principal source path is missing: %s\n' "${path#"$ROOT_DIR"/}" >&2; exit 1; }
  printf '%s=%s\n' "$key" "$(bind_host_path "$path")" >> "$temp_env"
}
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_GIT_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/.git"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_SRC_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/src"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_TEST_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/test"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_PRISMA_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/prisma"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_PACKAGE_JSON_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/package.json"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_PACKAGE_LOCK_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/package-lock.json"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_ENV_EXAMPLE_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/.env.example"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_DOCKERIGNORE_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/.dockerignore"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_GITIGNORE_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/.gitignore"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_ENTRYPOINT_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/docker/entrypoint.sh"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_DOCKERFILE_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/Dockerfile"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_ESLINT_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/eslint.config.mjs"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_NEST_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/nest-cli.json"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_RAILWAY_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/railway.json"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_TSCONFIG_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/tsconfig.json"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_TSCONFIG_BUILD_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/tsconfig.build.json"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_VITEST_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/vitest.config.ts"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_VITEST_E2E_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/vitest.config.e2e.ts"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_API_VITEST_COV_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/vitest.config.cov.ts"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_GIT_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/.git"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_SRC_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/src"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_E2E_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/e2e"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_PUBLIC_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/public"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_PACKAGE_JSON_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/package.json"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_PACKAGE_LOCK_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/package-lock.json"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_INDEX_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/index.html"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_PLAYWRIGHT_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/playwright.config.ts"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_TSCONFIG_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/tsconfig.json"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_VITE_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/vite.config.ts"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_RAILWAY_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/railway.json"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_GITIGNORE_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/.gitignore"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_DOCKERIGNORE_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/.dockerignore"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_DOCKERFILE_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/Dockerfile"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_CONFIG_JS_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/docker/40-config-js.sh"
add_bind_path CHANNELS_WEB_NGINX_TEMPLATE_WINDOWS "$ROOT_DIR/web-app/nginx/default.conf.template"
trap 'rm -f "$temp_env" "$temp_compose_overlay"' EXIT
COMPOSE=("${DOCKER[@]}" compose --project-name leadshug_channels_validation \
  --env-file "$(host_path "$ROOT_DIR/.env.example")" --env-file "$(host_path "$temp_env")" \
  -f "$(host_path "$ROOT_DIR/docker-compose.yml")" -f "$(host_path "$ROOT_DIR/docker-compose.channels.yml")" --profile validation)
if [[ "$CHANNELS_DOCKER_TRANSPORT" == linux && -n "${WSL_DISTRO_NAME:-}" ]]; then
  CHANNELS_API_ENV_EXAMPLE_WINDOWS="$(bind_host_path "$ROOT_DIR/api-app/.env.example")"
  CHANNELS_ROOT_ENV_EXAMPLE_WINDOWS="$(bind_host_path "$ROOT_DIR/.env.example")"
  yaml_scalar() {
    local value="$1"
    value="${value//\\/\\\\}"
    value="${value//\"/\\\"}"
    value="${value//\$/\$\$}"
    printf '"%s"' "$value"
  }
  temp_compose_overlay="$(mktemp)"
  chmod 600 "$temp_compose_overlay"
  {
    printf 'configs:\n'
    printf '  validation_api_env_mask:\n    file: %s\n' "$(yaml_scalar "$CHANNELS_API_ENV_EXAMPLE_WINDOWS")"
    printf '  validation_root_env_mask:\n    file: %s\n' "$(yaml_scalar "$CHANNELS_ROOT_ENV_EXAMPLE_WINDOWS")"
    printf '  validation_root_env_testing_mask:\n    file: %s\n' "$(yaml_scalar "$CHANNELS_ROOT_ENV_EXAMPLE_WINDOWS")"
  } > "$temp_compose_overlay"
  COMPOSE+=(-f "$temp_compose_overlay")
fi

resource_conflicts=()
capture_inventory() {
  local name="$1"; shift
  local output
  if ! output="$("${DOCKER[@]}" "$@")"; then
    printf 'Docker %s inventory failed; validation namespace state is unknown.\n' "$name" >&2
    exit 1
  fi
  printf '%s' "$output"
}
containers_inventory="$(capture_inventory 'container' ps -a --format '{{.Names}}')"
volumes_inventory="$(capture_inventory 'volume' volume ls --format '{{.Name}}')"
networks_inventory="$(capture_inventory 'network' network ls --format '{{.Name}}')"
while IFS= read -r resource_name; do
  if [[ "$resource_name" == leadshug_channels_validation_* || "$resource_name" == leadshug_channels_validation-* ]]; then
    resource_conflicts+=("container:$resource_name")
  fi
done <<< "$containers_inventory"
while IFS= read -r resource_name; do
  [[ "$resource_name" == leadshug_channels_validation_* ]] && resource_conflicts+=("volume:$resource_name")
done <<< "$volumes_inventory"
while IFS= read -r resource_name; do
  [[ "$resource_name" == leadshug_channels_validation_* ]] && resource_conflicts+=("network:$resource_name")
done <<< "$networks_inventory"
if ((${#resource_conflicts[@]})); then
  echo 'Refusing any pre-existing validation namespace resource, regardless of labels.' >&2
  printf '%s\n' "${resource_conflicts[@]}" >&2
  exit 1
fi

started=0
cleanup() {
  local status=$?
  trap - EXIT INT TERM
  if [[ "$started" == 1 ]]; then
    if ! "${COMPOSE[@]}" down --volumes --remove-orphans; then
      echo 'Validation cleanup failed; disposable namespace may require inspection.' >&2
      status=1
    fi
  fi
  rm -f "$temp_env"
  rm -f "$temp_compose_overlay"
  exit "$status"
}
trap cleanup EXIT
trap 'exit 130' INT
trap 'exit 143' TERM
"${COMPOSE[@]}" config --quiet
if [[ "${CHANNELS_CONFIG_ONLY:-0}" == 1 ]]; then
  echo 'Validation Compose config is valid; no resources were created.'
  exit 0
fi
started=1

RUNNER_SCRIPT='set -eu
target="$1"
printf "Runner Node: "
node --version
apt-get update -qq
DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends openssl libssl3 ca-certificates bash git python3 python3-yaml >/dev/null
python3 /workspace/scripts/check-channels-ci-parity.py --self-test
rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
printf "OpenSSL: "
openssl version
ldconfig -p | grep -q "libssl.so.3"
test -f /workspace/api-app/.env
cmp -s /workspace/api-app/.env /workspace/api-app/.env.example || { echo "API dotenv mask mismatch." >&2; exit 1; }
for private_path in /workspace/.env /workspace/.env.testing; do
  test -f "$private_path"
  cmp -s "$private_path" /workspace/.env.example || { echo "Root dotenv mask mismatch." >&2; exit 1; }
done
bound_sha="$(sha256sum /workspace/api-app/package.json | cut -d " " -f 1)"
test "$bound_sha" = "$CHANNELS_EXPECTED_API_PACKAGE_SHA256" || { echo "Source bind checksum mismatch before npm." >&2; exit 1; }
bound_web_sha="$(sha256sum /workspace/web-app/package.json | cut -d " " -f 1)"
test "$bound_web_sha" = "$CHANNELS_EXPECTED_WEB_PACKAGE_SHA256" || { echo "Web source bind checksum mismatch before npm." >&2; exit 1; }
test "$(git -c safe.directory=/workspace -C /workspace rev-parse --show-toplevel)" = /workspace
echo "Principal API/Web source binds and API/root dotenv masks verified before npm."
cd /workspace/api-app
npm ci
prisma_info="$(npx prisma -v)"
printf "%s\n" "$prisma_info"
printf "%s\n" "$prisma_info" | grep -q "debian-openssl-3.0.x" || { echo "Prisma did not detect the supported OpenSSL 3 engine." >&2; exit 1; }
npm run prisma:generate
cd /workspace/web-app
npm ci
case "$target" in
  api|all)
    cd /workspace/api-app
    npm run prisma:migrate
    npx prisma migrate status
    npm run lint
    npm run build
    npm test
    npm run test:e2e
    ;;
esac
case "$target" in
  web|all)
    cd /workspace/web-app
    npm run build
    npm test
    ;;
esac
case "$target" in
  browser|all)
    cd /workspace/api-app
    if [ "$target" = browser ]; then
      echo "Building API before starting browser fixture services."
      npm run build
    fi
    npm run prisma:migrate
    npx prisma migrate status
    cd /workspace/web-app
    npx playwright install --with-deps chromium
    npm run test:tela -- --trace off
    ;;
esac'

"${COMPOSE[@]}" run --rm -e CHANNELS_EXPECTED_API_PACKAGE_SHA256="$CHANNELS_EXPECTED_API_PACKAGE_SHA256" validation-runner sh -ec "$RUNNER_SCRIPT" channels-validation-runner "$TARGET"
```
