## web-app/e2e/channel-transport.spec.ts

```text
import { expect, test, type APIRequestContext, type Page } from '@playwright/test';

const FIXTURES = 'http://127.0.0.1:3101';

async function seed(request: APIRequestContext, includeEvolution = false, count = 3) {
  const response = await request.post(`${FIXTURES}/seed`, { data: { count, includeEvolution } });
  expect(response.status()).toBe(201);
  return response.json() as Promise<{
    email: string; password: string; businessUnitId: string; conversationId: string;
    otherConversationId: string; otherBusinessUnitId: string;
  }>;
}

async function loginAndOpen(page: Page, fixture: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof seed>>, messages = 3) {
  await page.context().clearCookies();
  await page.goto('/login');
  await page.getByLabel('E-mail').fill(fixture.email);
  await page.getByLabel('Senha').fill(fixture.password);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Entrar' }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/inbox$/);
  await page.getByLabel('Business Unit').selectOption(fixture.businessUnitId);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cliente Timeline' }).click();
  await expect(page.locator('.bolha')).toHaveCount(messages);
}

test.describe('Channel outbound pela Inbox com BFF e PostgreSQL reais', () => {
  test('envia por Meta, mantém rascunho em ACK sem ID e bloqueia conversa ambígua', async ({ page, request }) => {
    const fixture = await seed(request);
    await loginAndOpen(page, fixture);
    const composer = page.getByPlaceholder('Escreva uma resposta');
    await composer.fill('resposta oficial de teste');
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviar' }).click();
    await expect(page.locator('.bolha').filter({ hasText: 'resposta oficial de teste' })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(composer).toHaveValue('');

    await request.post(`${FIXTURES}/send-mode`, { data: { mode: 'unknown' } });
    await composer.fill('rascunho não reenviado');
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviar' }).click();
    await expect(page.getByRole('alert')).toContainText('aceitou o envio sem confirmar o ID externo');
    await expect(composer).toHaveValue('rascunho não reenviado');

    const ambiguous = await seed(request, true, 0);
    await page.context().clearCookies();
    await page.goto('/login');
    await page.getByLabel('E-mail').fill(ambiguous.email);
    await page.getByLabel('Senha').fill(ambiguous.password);
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Entrar' }).click();
    await page.getByLabel('Business Unit').selectOption(ambiguous.businessUnitId);
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cliente Timeline' }).click();
    await expect(page.getByText('Esta conversa ainda não tem um canal definido. Aguarde uma mensagem recebida para responder.')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviar' })).toBeDisabled();
  });

  test('seleciona Evolution pela última origem e descarta resposta atrasada após troca A→B→A', async ({ page, request }) => {
    const fixture = await seed(request, true);
    await request.post(`${FIXTURES}/select-evolution`, { data: { conversationId: fixture.conversationId } });
    await loginAndOpen(page, fixture, 4);
    const composer = page.getByPlaceholder('Escreva uma resposta');
    await composer.fill('resposta não oficial de teste');
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviar' }).click();
    await expect(page.locator('.bolha').filter({ hasText: 'resposta não oficial de teste' })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(composer).toHaveValue('');

    let liberar!: () => void;
    let iniciou!: () => void;
    const continua = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { liberar = resolve; });
    const solicitacao = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { iniciou = resolve; });
    const holdReply = (url: URL) => url.pathname === `/api/bff/conversations/${fixture.conversationId}/messages`;
    const holdResponse = async (route: import('@playwright/test').Route) => {
      if (route.request().method() !== 'POST') return route.continue();
      const response = await route.fetch();
      iniciou();
      await continua;
      await route.fulfill({ response });
    };
    await page.route(holdReply, holdResponse);
    const responsePath = `/api/bff/conversations/${fixture.conversationId}/messages`;
    await page.evaluate((pathname) => {
      type BodyReadState = {
        started: number;
        settled: number;
        processed: number;
        completion: Promise<void>;
        resolveCompletion: () => void;
        originalFetch: typeof window.fetch;
      };
      const w = window as typeof window & { __stcon06BodyRead?: BodyReadState };
      let resolveCompletion!: () => void;
      const state: BodyReadState = {
        started: 0,
        settled: 0,
        processed: 0,
        completion: new Promise<void>((resolve) => { resolveCompletion = resolve; }),
        resolveCompletion: () => resolveCompletion(),
        originalFetch: window.fetch,
      };
      const nativeFetch = window.fetch.bind(window);
      window.fetch = async (input, init) => {
        const requestUrl = input instanceof Request ? input.url : String(input);
        const method = init?.method ?? (input instanceof Request ? input.method : 'GET');
        const response = await nativeFetch(input, init);
        if (method === 'POST' && new URL(requestUrl, window.location.href).pathname === pathname) {
          const nativeText = response.text.bind(response);
          response.text = () => {
            state.started += 1;
            return nativeText().then((body) => {
              state.settled += 1;
              // A browser task runs after this promise and the awaiting api.ts / responder
              // microtasks, including the generation fence and draft decision.
              window.setTimeout(() => {
                state.processed += 1;
                state.resolveCompletion();
              }, 0);
              return body;
            });
          };
        }
        return response;
      };
      w.__stcon06BodyRead = state;
    }, responsePath);
    await composer.fill('resposta cuja resposta chega tarde');
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviar' }).click();
    await solicitacao;
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cliente Secundário' }).click();
    const activeThreadTitle = page.locator('.cartao.grade > .linha > strong');
    await expect(activeThreadTitle).toHaveCount(1);
    await expect(activeThreadTitle).toHaveText('Cliente Secundário');
    await expect(page.locator('.cartao.lista .item.ativo')).toHaveCount(1);
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Em atendimento' }).click();
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cliente Timeline' }).click();
    await expect(activeThreadTitle).toHaveCount(1);
    await expect(activeThreadTitle).toHaveText('Cliente Timeline');
    await expect(page.locator('.cartao.lista .item.ativo')).toHaveCount(1);
    await composer.fill('resposta cuja resposta chega tarde');
    const respostaAntiga = page.waitForResponse((response) => response.request().method() === 'POST'
      && new URL(response.url()).pathname === `/api/bff/conversations/${fixture.conversationId}/messages`);
    liberar();
    const responseAntiga = await respostaAntiga;
    expect(responseAntiga.status()).toBe(201);
    expect(await responseAntiga.finished()).toBeNull();
    const bodyRead = await page.evaluate(async () => {
      const state = (window as typeof window & { __stcon06BodyRead?: {
        completion: Promise<void>; started: number; settled: number; processed: number; originalFetch: typeof window.fetch;
      } }).__stcon06BodyRead;
      if (!state) throw new Error('The response body read instrumentation was not installed.');
      await state.completion;
      window.fetch = state.originalFetch;
      return { started: state.started, settled: state.settled, processed: state.processed };
    });
    expect(bodyRead).toEqual({ started: 1, settled: 1, processed: 1 });
    await expect(composer).toHaveValue('resposta cuja resposta chega tarde');
    await expect(page.getByRole('alert')).toHaveCount(0);
    await page.unroute(holdReply, holdResponse);
  });

  test('bloqueia vinte novos submits durante cada envio real em cinco ondas', async ({ page, request }) => {
    const fixture = await seed(request);
    await loginAndOpen(page, fixture);
    const composer = page.getByPlaceholder('Escreva uma resposta');
    const waveEvidence: Array<{ requests: number; retriggers: number; outcome: 'accepted' | 'unknown'; draftPreserved: boolean; messageCount: number }> = [];
    const form = page.locator('form').filter({ has: composer });
    for (let round = 0; round < 5; round += 1) {
      const outcome = round % 2 === 1 ? 'unknown' : 'accepted';
      const text = `composer wave ${round} ${outcome}`;
      await request.post(`${FIXTURES}/send-mode`, { data: { mode: outcome } });
      let requestCount = 0;
      let actualStatus = 0;
      let markEntered!: () => void;
      let release!: () => void;
      const entered = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { markEntered = resolve; });
      const held = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { release = resolve; });
      const matcher = (url: URL) => url.pathname === `/api/bff/conversations/${fixture.conversationId}/messages`;
      const holdRealResponse = async (route: import('@playwright/test').Route) => {
        if (route.request().method() !== 'POST') return route.continue();
        requestCount += 1;
        const response = await route.fetch();
        if (requestCount === 1) actualStatus = response.status();
        if (requestCount === 1) markEntered();
        await held;
        await route.fulfill({ response });
      };
      await page.route(matcher, holdRealResponse);
      await composer.fill(text);
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviar' }).click();
      await entered;
      try {
        for (let index = 0; index < 20; index += 1) {
          await form.evaluate((element) => element.dispatchEvent(new Event('submit', { bubbles: true, cancelable: true })));
        }
        expect(requestCount).toBe(1);
        expect(await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enviando…' }).isDisabled()).toBe(true);
      } finally {
        release();
      }
      if (outcome === 'unknown') {
        await expect(page.getByRole('alert')).toContainText('aceitou o envio sem confirmar o ID externo');
        await expect(composer).toHaveValue(text);
        expect(actualStatus).toBe(409);
      } else {
        await expect(page.locator('.bolha').filter({ hasText: text })).toBeVisible();
        await expect(composer).toHaveValue('');
        expect(actualStatus).toBe(201);
      }
      const auditResponse = await request.get(`${FIXTURES}/audit-outbound?conversationId=${encodeURIComponent(fixture.conversationId)}&content=${encodeURIComponent(text)}`);
      expect(auditResponse.status()).toBe(200);
      const audit = await auditResponse.json() as { total: number; accepted: number; failed: number; failedWithoutExternalId: number };
      expect(audit).toEqual(outcome === 'unknown'
        ? { total: 1, accepted: 0, failed: 1, failedWithoutExternalId: 1 }
        : { total: 1, accepted: 1, failed: 0, failedWithoutExternalId: 0 });
      waveEvidence.push({ requests: requestCount, retriggers: 20, outcome, draftPreserved: outcome === 'unknown', messageCount: audit.total });
      await page.unroute(matcher, holdRealResponse);
    }
    expect(waveEvidence).toHaveLength(5);
    expect(waveEvidence.every((wave) => wave.requests === 1 && wave.retriggers === 20)).toBe(true);
    expect(waveEvidence.filter((wave) => wave.outcome === 'accepted').every((wave) => wave.messageCount === 1 && !wave.draftPreserved)).toBe(true);
    expect(waveEvidence.filter((wave) => wave.outcome === 'unknown').every((wave) => wave.messageCount === 1 && wave.draftPreserved)).toBe(true);
    console.log(`[FRC-COMPOSER-EVIDENCE]${JSON.stringify({ waves: waveEvidence, limits: 'Five real BFF/PostgreSQL submits held after the actual response; twenty user-form submit events per in-flight send; one request per wave; no capacity/SLO claim.' })}`);
  });
});
```

## web-app/e2e/number-onboarding-freshness.ts

```text
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { mkdir, readFile, readdir, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { join, resolve } from 'node:path';
import timelineFreshness from './timeline-freshness';

const sha256 = (value: Buffer | string) => createHash('sha256').update(value).digest('hex');

async function hashes(directory: string, prefix: string): Promise<Array<{ path: string; sha256: string }>> {
  const files: Array<{ path: string; sha256: string }> = [];
  async function visit(current: string): Promise<void> {
    for (const entry of await readdir(current, { withFileTypes: true })) {
      const absolute = join(current, entry.name);
      if (entry.isDirectory()) await visit(absolute);
      else files.push({ path: absolute.slice(directory.length + 1), sha256: sha256(await readFile(absolute)) });
    }
  }
  await visit(directory);
  return files.filter(({ path }) => path.startsWith(prefix)).sort((a, b) => a.path.localeCompare(b.path));
}

export default async function numberOnboardingFreshness(): Promise<void> {
  await timelineFreshness();
  const web = process.cwd();
  const root = resolve(web, '..');
  const api = join(root, 'api-app');
  const git = (directory: string, ...args: string[]) => execFileSync('git', [
    '-c', `safe.directory=${directory}`, '-C', directory, ...args,
  ], { encoding: 'utf8' }).trim();
  const changed = (directory: string, scope: RegExp, publicPaths: string[]) => {
    const args = ['-c', `safe.directory=${directory}`, '-C', directory];
    const parse = (output: string, layer: string) => output.trim().split('\n').filter(Boolean)
      .map((line) => { const [status, ...paths] = line.split('\t'); return { status: `${layer}:${status}`, path: paths.at(-1)! }; });
    const staged = parse(execFileSync('git', [...args, 'diff', '--cached', '--name-status', 'HEAD', '--', ...publicPaths], { encoding: 'utf8' }), 'staged');
    const unstaged = parse(execFileSync('git', [...args, 'diff', '--name-status', '--', ...publicPaths], { encoding: 'utf8' }), 'unstaged');
    const untracked = execFileSync('git', [...args, 'ls-files', '--others', '--exclude-standard', '--', ...publicPaths], { encoding: 'utf8' }).trim().split('\n').filter(Boolean)
      .map((path) => ({ status: '??', path }));
    const inventory = new Map<string, string>();
    for (const item of [...staged, ...unstaged, ...untracked]) if (scope.test(item.path)) {
      inventory.set(item.path, [inventory.get(item.path), item.status].filter(Boolean).join(','));
    }
    return [...inventory.entries()].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b));
  };
  const webSources = changed(web, /^(src\/|e2e\/|playwright\.config\.ts$|vite\.config\.ts$|Dockerfile$|\.dockerignore$|package(?:-lock)?\.json$|tsconfig(?:\.[^/]+)?\.json$)/,
    ['src', 'e2e', 'package.json', 'package-lock.json', 'Dockerfile', '.dockerignore', 'playwright.config.ts', 'vite.config.ts', 'tsconfig.json', 'tsconfig.app.json', 'tsconfig.node.json']);
  const apiSources = changed(api, /^(src\/|prisma\/|test\/|package(?:-lock)?\.json$|Dockerfile$|\.dockerignore$|\.env\.example$|nest-cli\.json$|tsconfig(?:\.[^/]+)?\.json$)/,
    ['src', 'prisma', 'test', 'package.json', 'package-lock.json', 'Dockerfile', '.dockerignore', '.env.example', 'nest-cli.json', 'tsconfig.json', 'tsconfig.build.json']);
  const manifest = async (directory: string, entries: Array<[string, string]>) => Promise.all(entries.map(async ([path, status]) => {
    try { return { path, status, sha256: sha256(await readFile(resolve(directory, path))) }; }
    catch (error) {
      if (status.split(',').some((entry) => entry.endsWith(':D')) && error && typeof error === 'object' && 'code' in error && error.code === 'ENOENT') {
        return { path, status, sha256: null };
      }
      throw error;
    }
  }));
  const evidence = {
    capturedAtUtc: new Date().toISOString(),
    node: process.version,
    branches: {
      web: { name: git(web, 'branch', '--show-current'), head: git(web, 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'), declaredBaseline: '4e56f6c1f4515b918d05b8c87e890d42e8542c56' },
      api: { name: git(api, 'branch', '--show-current'), head: git(api, 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'), declaredBaseline: 'ae6a9662c29b77276351aa9de653a47276372246' },
    },
    webChangedSources: await manifest(web, webSources),
    apiChangedSources: await manifest(api, apiSources),
    webBundle: await hashes(join(web, 'dist'), 'assets/'),
    operationalDataBoundary: 'API .local-files payloads and private dotenv files are excluded from public-source inventories; an omitted file is not reported as a source deletion.',
    proof: 'timeline-freshness completed before Playwright workers; it verifies disposable DB family, API/fixture/preview health, and served JavaScript hash against dist.',
  };
  const target = resolve(root, 'foundation_documentation/artifacts/tmp/number-onboarding-execution');
  await mkdir(target, { recursive: true });
  await writeFile(join(target, 'pre-worker-freshness.json'), `${JSON.stringify(evidence, null, 2)}\n`);
}
```

## web-app/e2e/timeline-freshness.ts

```text
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { createRequire } from 'node:module';
import { access } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { join, resolve } from 'node:path';

const hash = (value: Buffer | string) => createHash('sha256').update(value).digest('hex');

async function changedSourceFiles(directory: string, scope: RegExp, publicPaths: string[]): Promise<Array<{ path: string; status: string; sha256: string | null }>> {
  const gitArgs = ['-c', `safe.directory=${directory}`, '-C', directory];
  const parse = (output: string, layer: string) => output.trim().split('\n').filter(Boolean)
    .map((line) => { const [status, ...paths] = line.split('\t'); return { status: `${layer}:${status}`, path: paths.at(-1)! }; });
  const staged = parse(execFileSync('git', [...gitArgs, 'diff', '--cached', '--name-status', 'HEAD', '--', ...publicPaths], { encoding: 'utf8' }), 'staged');
  const unstaged = parse(execFileSync('git', [...gitArgs, 'diff', '--name-status', '--', ...publicPaths], { encoding: 'utf8' }), 'unstaged');
  const untracked = execFileSync('git', [...gitArgs, 'ls-files', '--others', '--exclude-standard', '--', ...publicPaths], { encoding: 'utf8' }).trim().split('\n').filter(Boolean)
    .map((path) => ({ status: '??', path }));
  const inventory = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const item of [...staged, ...unstaged, ...untracked]) if (scope.test(item.path)) {
    inventory.set(item.path, [inventory.get(item.path), item.status].filter(Boolean).join(','));
  }
  return Promise.all([...inventory.entries()].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b)).map(async ([path, status]) => {
    try { await access(resolve(directory, path)); return { path, status, sha256: hash(await readFile(resolve(directory, path))) }; }
    catch (error) {
      if (status.split(',').some((entry) => entry.endsWith(':D')) && error && typeof error === 'object' && 'code' in error && error.code === 'ENOENT') {
        return { path, status, sha256: null };
      }
      throw error;
    }
  }));
}

export default async function timelineFreshness(): Promise<void> {
  const web = process.cwd();
  const root = resolve(web, '..');
  const api = join(root, 'api-app');
  const runtime = process.version;
  if (!/^v22\./.test(runtime)) throw new Error(`Attestation timeline exige Node 22; recebeu ${runtime}`);

  const databaseUrl = process.env.LEADSHUG_E2E_DATABASE_URL;
  if (!databaseUrl) throw new Error('LEADSHUG_E2E_DATABASE_URL ausente durante attestation.');
  const db = new URL(databaseUrl);
  const database = decodeURIComponent(db.pathname.slice(1));
  if (!database.startsWith('leadshug_timeline_')) throw new Error(`Banco fora da família descartável: ${database}`);

  const apiPort = process.env.LEADSHUG_API_PORT ?? '3100';
  const apiEndpoint = `http://127.0.0.1:${apiPort}`;
  const [apiHealth, fixtureHealth, htmlResponse] = await Promise.all([
    fetch(`${apiEndpoint}/health`),
    fetch('http://127.0.0.1:3101/health'),
    fetch('http://127.0.0.1:4173/'),
  ]);
  for (const [label, response] of [['API', apiHealth], ['fixtures', fixtureHealth], ['preview', htmlResponse]] as const) {
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`${label} indisponível na attestation: HTTP ${response.status}`);
  }
  const html = await htmlResponse.text();
  const asset = html.match(/src="([^"]+\.js)"/);
  if (!asset) throw new Error('HTML servido não referencia o bundle JavaScript esperado.');
  const jsResponse = await fetch(new URL(asset[1], 'http://127.0.0.1:4173'));
  if (!jsResponse.ok) throw new Error(`Bundle servido retornou HTTP ${jsResponse.status}`);
  const servedJs = Buffer.from(await jsResponse.arrayBuffer());
  const servedPath = resolve(web, 'dist', `.${new URL(asset[1], 'http://127.0.0.1').pathname}`);
  const distJs = await readFile(servedPath);
  if (hash(servedJs) !== hash(distJs)) throw new Error('Bundle servido não corresponde ao artefato em web-app/dist.');

  const gitArgs = (directory: string, ...args: string[]) => ['-c', `safe.directory=${directory}`, '-C', directory, ...args];
  const git = (directory: string, ...args: string[]) => execFileSync('git', gitArgs(directory, ...args), { encoding: 'utf8' }).trim();
  const diffHash = (directory: string, publicPaths: string[]) => hash(execFileSync('git', gitArgs(
    directory, 'diff', '--no-ext-diff', '--binary', 'HEAD', '--', ...publicPaths,
  )));
  const apiPublicPaths = [
    'src', 'test', 'prisma', 'e2e', 'public', 'package.json', 'package-lock.json',
    'Dockerfile', '.dockerignore', '.env.example', '.gitignore', 'eslint.config.mjs',
    'nest-cli.json', 'railway.json', 'tsconfig.json', 'tsconfig.build.json',
    'vitest.config.ts', 'vitest.config.e2e.ts', 'vitest.config.cov.ts', 'docker',
  ];
  const webPublicPaths = [
    'src', 'e2e', 'public', 'package.json', 'package-lock.json', 'index.html',
    'Dockerfile', '.dockerignore', '.gitignore', 'eslint.config.js', 'playwright.config.ts',
    'railway.json', 'tsconfig.json', 'vite.config.ts', 'docker', 'nginx',
  ];
  const apiSourceManifest = await changedSourceFiles(api, /^(src\/|prisma\/|test\/|package(?:-lock)?\.json$|Dockerfile$|\.dockerignore$|\.env\.example$|nest-cli\.json$|tsconfig(?:\.[^/]+)?\.json$)/,
    ['src', 'prisma', 'test', 'package.json', 'package-lock.json', 'Dockerfile', '.dockerignore', '.env.example', 'nest-cli.json', 'tsconfig.json', 'tsconfig.build.json']);
  const webSourceManifest = await changedSourceFiles(web, /^(src\/|e2e\/|playwright\.config\.ts$|vite\.config\.ts$|Dockerfile$|\.dockerignore$|package(?:-lock)?\.json$|tsconfig(?:\.[^/]+)?\.json$)/,
    ['src', 'e2e', 'package.json', 'package-lock.json', 'Dockerfile', '.dockerignore', 'playwright.config.ts', 'vite.config.ts', 'tsconfig.json', 'tsconfig.app.json', 'tsconfig.node.json']);
  const requireFromApi = createRequire(join(api, 'package.json'));
  const { PrismaClient } = requireFromApi('@prisma/client') as { PrismaClient: new (options: { datasources: { db: { url: string } } }) => any };
  const prisma = new PrismaClient({ datasources: { db: { url: databaseUrl } } });
  let completedMigrations: number;
  try {
    const rows = await prisma.$queryRawUnsafe('SELECT COUNT(*)::int AS count FROM "_prisma_migrations" WHERE finished_at IS NOT NULL AND rolled_back_at IS NULL') as Array<{ count: number }>;
    completedMigrations = Number(rows[0]?.count ?? 0);
  } finally {
    await prisma.$disconnect();
  }

  const artifactDirectory = resolve(root, 'foundation_documentation/artifacts/tmp/inbox-timeline-execution');
  await mkdir(artifactDirectory, { recursive: true });
  await writeFile(join(artifactDirectory, 'served-index-authority.html'), html);
  await writeFile(join(artifactDirectory, 'served-app-authority.js'), servedJs);
  const runId = process.env.LEADSHUG_ATTESTATION_RUN_ID ?? new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-');
  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/.test(runId)) throw new Error('LEADSHUG_ATTESTATION_RUN_ID contém caracteres inválidos.');
  const attestation = {
    capturedAtUtc: new Date().toISOString(),
    runId,
    branch: {
      api: { name: git(api, 'branch', '--show-current'), head: git(api, 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'), declaredBaseline: 'ae6a9662c29b77276351aa9de653a47276372246', diffSha256: diffHash(api, apiPublicPaths), sourceManifest: apiSourceManifest },
      web: { name: git(web, 'branch', '--show-current'), head: git(web, 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'), declaredBaseline: '4e56f6c1f4515b918d05b8c87e890d42e8542c56', diffSha256: diffHash(web, webPublicPaths), sourceManifest: webSourceManifest },
    },
    runtime,
    database: { name: database, endpoint: `${db.protocol}//${db.hostname}:${db.port || '5432'}/${database}`, completedMigrations },
    endpoints: { apiHealth: apiHealth.status, fixtureHealth: fixtureHealth.status, preview: htmlResponse.status, servedBundle: new URL(asset[1], 'http://127.0.0.1:4173').pathname },
    servedBundleSha256: hash(servedJs),
    distBundleSha256: hash(distJs),
    operationalDataBoundary: 'API .local-files payloads and private dotenv files are excluded from public-source inventories and binary diff pathspecs; an omitted file is not reported as a source deletion.',
  };
  const serialized = `${JSON.stringify(attestation, null, 2)}\n`;
  await writeFile(join(artifactDirectory, `attestation-browser-${runId}.json`), serialized);
  await writeFile(join(artifactDirectory, 'attestation-browser-authority.json'), serialized);
}
```

## web-app/playwright.config.ts

```text
import { defineConfig } from '@playwright/test';

/** Porta própria: a 3000 desta máquina é de outro projeto. */
const PORTA_DA_API = process.env.LEADSHUG_API_PORT ?? '3100';
const API = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORTA_DA_API}`;
const DATABASE_URL = process.env.LEADSHUG_E2E_DATABASE_URL;
if (!DATABASE_URL) throw new Error('LEADSHUG_E2E_DATABASE_URL precisa apontar para o banco descartável dedicado.');

/**
 * Teste de navegador contra a tela **buildada**, não contra o dev server.
 *
 * O que ele existe para pegar é justamente o que o teste de API não pega: erro
 * de renderização, tela branca, rota que não leva a lugar nenhum. Foi assim que
 * "Cannot read properties of undefined" apareceu — depois de o QA topar com ela.
 *
 * Sobe dois processos: a API (com o Postgres local do docker compose) e o
 * `vite preview` servindo o `dist`. Mesma origem, como em desenvolvimento.
 */
export default defineConfig({
  globalSetup: './e2e/number-onboarding-freshness.ts',
  testDir: './e2e',
  timeout: 30_000,
  fullyParallel: false,
  workers: 1,
  use: {
    baseURL: 'http://127.0.0.1:4173',
    trace: 'retain-on-failure',
  },
  webServer: [
    {
      command: 'node ../api-app/test/timeline-fixtures.cjs',
      url: 'http://127.0.0.1:3101/health',
      reuseExistingServer: false,
      timeout: 30_000,
      stdout: 'pipe',
      stderr: 'pipe',
      env: {
        DATABASE_URL,
        LEADSHUG_E2E_DATABASE_URL: DATABASE_URL,
        LEADSHUG_API_PORT: PORTA_DA_API,
        LEADSHUG_FIXTURE_PORT: '3101',
        META_APP_ID: 'fixture-app',
        META_APP_SECRET: 'fixture-meta-secret-only',
        META_SIGNUP_CONFIG_ID: 'fixture-config',
        GRAPH_VERSION: 'v26.0',
        CHANNEL_CREDENTIAL_KEY_ID: 'timeline-key-v1',
        CHANNEL_CREDENTIAL_KEY: Buffer.alloc(32, 29).toString('base64'),
        LH_CHANNEL_UNOFFICIAL: 'true',
        EVOLUTION_API_URL: 'http://127.0.0.1:3101/provider/evolution',
        EVOLUTION_API_KEY: 'timeline-provider-port-only',
        EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_URL: 'http://127.0.0.1:3100/webhooks/evolution',
        EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET: 'timeline-evolution-header-secret',
      },
    },
    {
      // `build &&` NÃO é zelo: `npm run start` da api é `node dist/main.js`, e
      // sem construir antes o teste de tela valida uma API VELHA — que é o
      // oposto do que ele existe para fazer. Foi assim que uma mudança de schema
      // passou por aqui reportando 500 de um dist de duas fases atrás.
      command: 'npm --prefix ../api-app run build && node ../api-app/test/browser-server.cjs',
      url: `${API}/health`,
      // Não reaproveitar: esta máquina roda outros serviços, e "já tem algo na
      // porta" foi o que fez a primeira corrida testar o projeto vizinho.
      reuseExistingServer: false,
      // 60s cobriam só o `start`; o comando passou a incluir o build, e num
      // runner frio isso não cabia.
      timeout: 180_000,
      // O log do servidor SOBE para a saída do teste.
      //
      // Sem isto, uma falha de boot aparece só como "Timed out waiting from
      // config.webServer" — sem uma linha do que a API disse. Foi exatamente
      // assim que este erro chegou do CI: um timeout, e nenhuma pista.
      stdout: 'pipe',
      stderr: 'pipe',
      env: {
        // O `.env` da API pode apontar para outro Postgres local (por exemplo,
        // o serviço legado na 5433). O harness deve ser determinístico e falar
        // com o Postgres deste compose, publicado na 55433.
        DATABASE_URL,
        LEADSHUG_E2E_DATABASE_URL: DATABASE_URL,
        SESSION_SECRET: 'segredo-de-e2e',
        INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN: 'token-de-e2e',
        // Public/fictitious registration and provider settings make every
        // capability deterministic without consulting the operator's .env.
        META_APP_ID: 'fixture-app',
        META_APP_SECRET: 'fixture-meta-secret-only',
        META_SIGNUP_CONFIG_ID: 'fixture-config',
        GRAPH_VERSION: 'v26.0',
        CHANNEL_CREDENTIAL_KEY_ID: 'timeline-key-v1',
        CHANNEL_CREDENTIAL_KEY: Buffer.alloc(32, 29).toString('base64'),
        LH_CHANNEL_UNOFFICIAL: 'true',
        EVOLUTION_API_URL: 'http://127.0.0.1:3101/provider/evolution',
        EVOLUTION_API_KEY: 'timeline-provider-port-only',
        EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_URL: `${API}/webhooks/evolution`,
        EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET: 'timeline-evolution-header-secret',
        WEBHOOK_VERIFY_TOKEN: 'verify-token-de-teste',
        // Volume de arquivo (ADR-0024). Sem ele o produto recusa upload — e o
        // teste de tela precisa exercitar o caminho que EXISTE em stage.
        LH_ARQUIVOS_DIR: '/tmp/leadshug-arquivos-e2e',
        // O link do convite é montado com isto (ADR-0025): sem ele, o e-mail sai
        // com caminho relativo, que não abre em lugar nenhum.
        WEB_ORIGIN: 'http://127.0.0.1:4173',
        PORT: PORTA_DA_API,
      },
    },
    {
      // `--host 127.0.0.1` NÃO é detalhe: o padrão do `vite preview` é `localhost`,
      // e quem resolve esse nome é o sistema. Nesta máquina ele dá só IPv4; no
      // runner do GitHub o `/etc/hosts` põe `::1` primeiro, o servidor sobe em
      // `[::1]:4173` e o Playwright — que pergunta por `127.0.0.1` — bate em
      // porta fechada. O log mostrava o preview NO AR e o teste estourando 60s.
      //
      // Todo o resto do harness já fala 127.0.0.1: a baseURL, o WEB_ORIGIN, o
      // CORS da API. Só a ponta que ESCUTA ficava a cargo do resolvedor.
      command: 'npm run preview -- --host 127.0.0.1 --port 4173 --strictPort',
      stdout: 'pipe',
      stderr: 'pipe',
      env: { LEADSHUG_API: API },
      url: 'http://127.0.0.1:4173',
      reuseExistingServer: false,
      timeout: 60_000,
    },
  ],
});
```

## web-app/src/api.spec.ts

```text
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { ErroDaApi, api } from './api';

function responde(corpo: string, init: { status?: number; ok?: boolean } = {}) {
  const status = init.status ?? 200;
  vi.stubGlobal(
    'fetch',
    vi.fn(async () => ({
      status,
      ok: init.ok ?? status < 400,
      text: async () => corpo,
    })),
  );
}

afterEach(() => vi.unstubAllGlobals());

describe('resposta que não é JSON (api.ts)', () => {
  it('falha DIZENDO a causa provável, em vez de devolver objeto vazio', async () => {
    // O que o nginx do próprio front devolve quando a base da API está vazia.
    responde('<!doctype html><html><body><div id="root"></div></body></html>');

    await expect(api.get('/painel')).rejects.toThrow(/base da API está VAZIA/);
    await expect(api.get('/painel')).rejects.toMatchObject({ codigo: 'RESPOSTA_NAO_E_JSON' });
  });

  it('a mensagem aponta para LEADSHUG_API_URL quando a base está vazia', async () => {
    responde('<html></html>');
    await expect(api.get('/painel')).rejects.toThrow(/LEADSHUG_API_URL/);
  });

  it('corpo vazio com 200 continua sendo objeto vazio — isso é legítimo', async () => {
    responde('');
    await expect(api.get('/x')).resolves.toEqual({});
  });

  /**
   * Com a base CONFIGURADA — o cenário do stage.
   *
   * `API_BASE` é lida na importação do módulo, então o `window` precisa existir
   * ANTES dela. Importar de novo com `resetModules` é o que faz este teste
   * exercitar o caminho de verdade, e não o de base vazia.
   */
  async function comBase(url: string) {
    vi.resetModules();
    vi.stubGlobal('window', { __LEADSHUG__: { apiUrl: url } });
    return (await import('./api')) as typeof import('./api');
  }

  it('405 é reconhecido como servidor de ARQUIVOS, não como "API confusa"', async () => {
    // nginx responde 405 a um POST para arquivo estático. É assinatura, não
    // ambiguidade — e dizer isso poupa uma rodada de investigação.
    const mod = await comBase('https://api.exemplo.com.br');
    responde('<html>405</html>', { status: 405 });

    await expect(mod.api.post('/auth/login', {})).rejects.toThrow(/servidor de ARQUIVOS/);
    await expect(mod.api.post('/auth/login', {})).rejects.toThrow(/LEADSHUG_API_URL/);
  });

  it('HTML com 200 também é reconhecido como servidor de arquivos', async () => {
    const mod = await comBase('https://api.exemplo.com.br');
    responde('<!doctype html><html></html>');
    await expect(mod.api.get('/painel')).rejects.toThrow(/servidor de ARQUIVOS/);
  });

  it('resposta não-JSON que NÃO parece estática cai na mensagem genérica', async () => {
    const mod = await comBase('https://api.exemplo.com.br');
    responde('isto não é json nem html', { status: 502 });
    await expect(mod.api.get('/painel')).rejects.toThrow(/não é JSON/);
  });

  it('JSON válido passa como sempre', async () => {
    responde(JSON.stringify({ atendimento: { emEspera: 3 } }));
    await expect(api.get('/painel')).resolves.toEqual({ atendimento: { emEspera: 3 } });
  });
});

describe('base sem esquema (o erro de stage)', () => {
  it('recusa host solto: sem esquema, o navegador trata como CAMINHO', async () => {
    vi.resetModules();
    vi.stubGlobal('window', { __LEADSHUG__: { apiUrl: 'leadshug-api-stage.up.railway.app' } });
    // A chamada viraria https://<o-proprio-front>/leadshug-api-stage.../api/bff/...
    // e bateria no nginx do front — 405 no login, sem nada citando a variável.
    await expect(import('./api')).rejects.toThrow(/sem esquema/);
  });

  it('aceita https e http', async () => {
    vi.resetModules();
    vi.stubGlobal('window', { __LEADSHUG__: { apiUrl: 'https://api.exemplo.com.br' } });
    await expect(import('./api')).resolves.toBeDefined();
  });
});

describe('falha do servidor (o 500 do Nest)', () => {
  it('usa `message` quando não há `error`, e SEMPRE mostra o status', async () => {
    // Formato do 500 do Nest. Antes, isto virava "Falha inesperada." — que não
    // dizia nem que era 500.
    responde(JSON.stringify({ statusCode: 500, message: 'Internal server error' }), { status: 500 });
    await expect(api.post('/auth/login', {})).rejects.toThrow('Internal server error (HTTP 500)');
  });

  it('sem `error` e sem `message`, diz o status mesmo assim', async () => {
    responde(JSON.stringify({ statusCode: 502 }), { status: 502 });
    await expect(api.get('/x')).rejects.toThrow(/respondeu 502 sem explicar/);
  });

  it('recusa do produto continua mostrando a mensagem do produto', async () => {
    responde(JSON.stringify({ error: 'E-mail ou senha inválidos.', code: 'CREDENCIAL_INVALIDA' }), { status: 401 });
    await expect(api.post('/auth/login', {})).rejects.toThrow(/E-mail ou senha inválidos/);
  });

  it('retém outcome e metadados seguros de incerteza', async () => {
    responde(JSON.stringify({
      error: 'O envio não foi confirmado.', code: 'OUTBOUND_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE',
      outcome: 'ACCEPTED_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE', evidence: 'ACK_WITHOUT_ID',
      localMessageState: 'UNCONFIRMED', diagnosticPersistence: 'UNCONFIRMED',
    }), { status: 503 });
    await expect(api.post('/conversations/c1/messages', { texto: 'draft' })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      codigo: 'OUTBOUND_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE', outcome: 'ACCEPTED_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE',
      evidence: 'ACK_WITHOUT_ID', localMessageState: 'UNCONFIRMED', diagnosticPersistence: 'UNCONFIRMED',
    });
  });
});

describe('requisição com cabeçalho de correlação', () => {
  it('envia Idempotency-Key sem colocar dados transitórios na URL', async () => {
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (_input: RequestInfo | URL, _init?: RequestInit) => ({ status: 201, ok: true, text: async () => '{"attempt":{"id":"a"}}' }));
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
    await api.postWithHeaders('/number-registrations', { tipo: 'OFICIAL' }, { 'idempotency-key': 'test-key' });
    const [url, init] = fetchMock.mock.calls[0] as unknown as [string, RequestInit];
    expect(url).toContain('/api/bff/number-registrations');
    expect(url).not.toContain('test-key');
    expect(new Headers(init.headers).get('idempotency-key')).toBe('test-key');
    expect(init.body).toBe('{"tipo":"OFICIAL"}');
  });
});
```
